// ============================================================================
// PINYIN NORMALIZATION, ANDROID KEYBOARD ('v' -> 'ü'), TONE & DICTATION HELPERS
// ============================================================================
const TONE_VOWEL_MAP = {
  ā: { base: 'a', tone: 1 },
  á: { base: 'a', tone: 2 },
  ǎ: { base: 'a', tone: 3 },
  à: { base: 'a', tone: 4 },
  ē: { base: 'e', tone: 1 },
  é: { base: 'e', tone: 2 },
  ě: { base: 'e', tone: 3 },
  è: { base: 'e', tone: 4 },
  ī: { base: 'i', tone: 1 },
  í: { base: 'i', tone: 2 },
  ǐ: { base: 'i', tone: 3 },
  ì: { base: 'i', tone: 4 },
  ō: { base: 'o', tone: 1 },
  ó: { base: 'o', tone: 2 },
  ǒ: { base: 'o', tone: 3 },
  ò: { base: 'o', tone: 4 },
  ū: { base: 'u', tone: 1 },
  ú: { base: 'u', tone: 2 },
  ǔ: { base: 'u', tone: 3 },
  ù: { base: 'u', tone: 4 },
  ǖ: { base: 'ü', tone: 1 },
  ǘ: { base: 'ü', tone: 2 },
  ǚ: { base: 'ü', tone: 3 },
  ǜ: { base: 'ü', tone: 4 },
  ü: { base: 'ü', tone: 5 }
};

function stripToneMarks(input = '') {
  return String(input || '')
    .toLowerCase()
    .replace(/u:/g, 'ü')
    .replace(/v/g, 'ü')
    .split('')
    .map((ch) => (TONE_VOWEL_MAP[ch] ? TONE_VOWEL_MAP[ch].base : ch))
    .join('')
    .replace(/[1-50]/g, '')
    .replace(/['’\s\p{P}]/gu, '');
}

function convertPinyinToNumeric(pinyin = '') {
  const tokens = String(pinyin || '')
    .trim()
    .toLowerCase()
    .replace(/u:/g, 'ü')
    .replace(/v/g, 'ü')
    .split(/[\s']+/)
    .filter(Boolean);

  return tokens
    .map((tok) => {
      if (/[1-5]$/.test(tok)) return tok;
      let detectedTone = 5;
      const baseChars = tok.split('').map((ch) => {
        if (TONE_VOWEL_MAP[ch]) {
          if (TONE_VOWEL_MAP[ch].tone !== 5) {
            detectedTone = TONE_VOWEL_MAP[ch].tone;
          }
          return TONE_VOWEL_MAP[ch].base;
        }
        return ch;
      });
      return `${baseChars.join('')}${detectedTone}`;
    })
    .join(' ');
}

function parsePinyinSyllablesWithTones(pinyin = '', hanzi = '') {
  const rawTokens = String(pinyin || '')
    .trim()
    .split(/[\s']+/)
    .filter(Boolean);

  const tokens =
    rawTokens.length === 1 && hanzi.length === 2 && rawTokens[0].length >= 4
      ? [
          rawTokens[0].slice(0, Math.ceil(rawTokens[0].length / 2)),
          rawTokens[0].slice(Math.ceil(rawTokens[0].length / 2))
        ]
      : rawTokens;

  return tokens.map((syl, idx) => {
    let tone = 5;
    for (const ch of syl) {
      if (TONE_VOWEL_MAP[ch] && TONE_VOWEL_MAP[ch].tone !== 5) {
        tone = TONE_VOWEL_MAP[ch].tone;
        break;
      }
    }
    const numMatch = syl.match(/([1-5])/);
    if (numMatch) tone = Number(numMatch[1]);
    return {
      syllable: syl,
      tone,
      char: hanzi[idx] || ''
    };
  });
}

// ============================================================================
// CHAO TONE CONTOUR VISUALIZER & AUTOMATIC TONE SANDHI BADGE
// ============================================================================
function ChaoToneVisualizer({ pinyin = '', hanzi = '', compact = false }) {
  const syllables = useMemo(
    () => parsePinyinSyllablesWithTones(pinyin, hanzi),
    [pinyin, hanzi]
  );

  const sandhiNote = useMemo(() => {
    for (let i = 0; i < syllables.length - 1; i++) {
      if (syllables[i].tone === 3 && syllables[i + 1].tone === 3) {
        return 'Tone Sandhi: 3rd + 3rd spoken as 2nd + 3rd (2 + 3)';
      }
      const cleanBase = stripToneMarks(syllables[i].syllable);
      if ((hanzi[i] === '不' || cleanBase === 'bu') && syllables[i + 1].tone === 4) {
        return 'Tone Sandhi: 不 (bù) shifts to 2nd tone (bú) before 4th tone';
      }
      if ((hanzi[i] === '一' || cleanBase === 'yi') && syllables[i + 1].tone === 4) {
        return 'Tone Sandhi: 一 (yī) shifts to 2nd tone (yí) before 4th tone';
      }
    }
    return null;
  }, [syllables, hanzi]);

  const getTonePath = (tone) => {
    switch (tone) {
      case 1: // 55 High Flat
        return { d: 'M 6 6 L 42 6', label: '55 Flat', color: '#38bdf8' };
      case 2: // 35 Rising
        return { d: 'M 6 20 Q 24 14 42 5', label: '35 Rising', color: '#34d399' };
      case 3: // 214 Dipping
        return { d: 'M 6 14 L 20 23 L 42 7', label: '214 Dip', color: '#fbbf24' };
      case 4: // 51 Falling
        return { d: 'M 6 5 L 42 23', label: '51 Fall', color: '#f43f5e' };
      default: // Neutral dot
        return { d: null, label: 'Neutral', color: '#94a3b8' };
    }
  };

  return (
    <div className="flex flex-col items-center gap-1.5">
      <div className="flex items-center justify-center flex-wrap gap-2">
        {syllables.map((item, idx) => {
          const meta = getTonePath(item.tone);
          return (
            <div
              key={`${item.syllable}_${idx}`}
              className="px-2.5 py-1 rounded-xl bg-slate-950/85 border border-slate-800 flex flex-col items-center"
            >
              <svg width="48" height="26" viewBox="0 0 48 28" className="overflow-visible">
                <line x1="4" y1="6" x2="44" y2="6" stroke="#1e293b" strokeWidth="1" />
                <line x1="4" y1="15" x2="44" y2="15" stroke="#1e293b" strokeWidth="1" />
                <line x1="4" y1="23" x2="44" y2="23" stroke="#1e293b" strokeWidth="1" />
                {meta.d ? (
                  <path
                    d={meta.d}
                    fill="none"
                    stroke={meta.color}
                    strokeWidth="3"
                    strokeLinecap="round"
                    strokeLinejoin="round"
                  />
                ) : (
                  <circle cx="24" cy="15" r="3.5" fill={meta.color} />
                )}
              </svg>
              <div className="flex items-center gap-1 text-[10px] font-bold">
                <span className="text-white">{item.syllable}</span>
                {!compact && <span className="text-slate-400">({meta.label})</span>}
              </div>
            </div>
          );
        })}
      </div>

      {sandhiNote && (
        <span className="inline-flex items-center gap-1 px-2.5 py-0.5 rounded-full bg-amber-500/15 border border-amber-400/40 text-amber-300 text-[10px] font-extrabold">
          <span>⚡ {sandhiNote}</span>
        </span>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}

// ============================================================================
// CHARACTER INSPECTOR MODAL (FOR CHARACTER MATRIX & RADICAL EXPLORER PILLS)
// ============================================================================
function CharacterInspectModal({
  charEntry,
  isSlowMode,
  onSetSlowMode,
  speakMandarin,
  speakingState,
  onClose
}) {
  const [scriptStatus, setScriptStatus] = useState('loading');
  const [charDetails, setCharDetails] = useState({ strokeCount: null, radStrokes: [] });
  const previewRef = useRef(null);
  const writerRef = useRef(null);

  const targetChar = charEntry?.char || '你';
  const knownInfo = KNOWN_CHARACTER_INFO[targetChar] || null;

  useEffect(() => {
    let mounted = true;
    loadHanziWriterScript()
      .then(() => {
        if (mounted) setScriptStatus('ready');
      })
      .catch(() => {
        if (mounted) setScriptStatus('error');
      });
    return () => {
      mounted = false;
    };
  }, []);

  useEffect(() => {
    speakMandarin(targetChar);
  }, [targetChar, speakMandarin]);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (scriptStatus !== 'ready' || !previewRef.current || !window.HanziWriter) return;
    const containerEl = previewRef.current;
    containerEl.innerHTML = '';
    writerRef.current = null;

    const writer = window.HanziWriter.create(containerEl, targetChar, {
      width: 190,
      height: 190,
      padding: 14,
      showOutline: true,
      showCharacter: true,
      strokeAnimationSpeed: 1.1,
      delayBetweenStrokes: 180,
      strokeColor: '#f8fafc',
      radicalColor: '#dc2626',
      outlineColor: '#334155',
      onLoadCharDataSuccess: (data) => {
        const radStrokes = Array.isArray(data.radStrokes) ? data.radStrokes : [];
        setCharDetails({
          strokeCount: Array.isArray(data.strokes) ? data.strokes.length : null,
          radStrokes
        });
        setTimeout(() => {
          if (writerRef.current === writer) {
            writer.animateCharacter();
          }
        }, 120);
      }
    });

    writerRef.current = writer;

    return () => {
      if (containerEl) containerEl.innerHTML = '';
      writerRef.current = null;
    };
  }, [scriptStatus, targetChar]);

  return (
    <div className="fixed inset-0 z-50 bg-slate-950/90 backdrop-blur-sm flex items-center justify-center p-4">
      <div className="w-full max-w-sm rounded-3xl bg-slate-900 border border-slate-700 p-5 space-y-4 shadow-2xl">
        <div className="flex items-center justify-between border-b border-slate-800 pb-3">
          <div>
            <span className="text-[10px] font-extrabold uppercase text-amber-400">
              Hanzi Inspector • Mastery Lv.{charEntry?.mastery ?? 0}/5
            </span>
            <h3 className="text-lg font-black text-white">
              Character "{targetChar}" ({knownInfo?.pinyin || charEntry?.pinyinSyllables?.join(' / ')})
            </h3>
          </div>
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={onClose}
            className="p-1.5 rounded-xl bg-slate-800 text-slate-400 hover:text-white"
          >
            <X className="w-4 h-4" />
          </button>
        </div>

        <div className="flex flex-col items-center space-y-2.5">
          <div className="w-full flex items-center justify-between">
            <span className="text-xs font-extrabold text-slate-300">
              {charDetails.strokeCount ? `${charDetails.strokeCount} Total Strokes` : 'Loading Strokes...'}
            </span>
            <MandarinAudioControls
              text={targetChar}
              isSlowMode={isSlowMode}
              onSetSlowMode={onSetSlowMode}
              speakMandarin={speakMandarin}
              speakingState={speakingState}
              size="sm"
            />
          </div>

          <div className="relative w-[196px] h-[196px] rounded-2xl bg-slate-950 border-2 border-amber-500/40 flex items-center justify-center overflow-hidden shadow-inner">
            <svg
              viewBox="0 0 196 196"
              className="absolute inset-0 w-full h-full pointer-events-none opacity-25"
            >
              <rect x="4" y="4" width="188" height="188" fill="none" stroke="#f59e0b" strokeWidth="1.5" />
              <line x1="4" y1="98" x2="192" y2="98" stroke="#f59e0b" strokeWidth="1" strokeDasharray="5 5" />
              <line x1="98" y1="4" x2="98" y2="192" stroke="#f59e0b" strokeWidth="1" strokeDasharray="5 5" />
            </svg>
            <div ref={previewRef} className="relative z-10 w-[190px] h-[190px]" />
          </div>

          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={() => {
              soundFX.playPop();
              writerRef.current?.animateCharacter();
            }}
            className="game-btn-3d px-4 py-2 rounded-xl bg-amber-500/20 hover:bg-amber-500/30 border border-amber-400/50 text-amber-300 text-xs font-extrabold flex items-center gap-1.5"
          >
            <Play className="w-3.5 h-3.5 fill-current" />
            <span>Replay Stroke Animation</span>
          </button>
        </div>

        <div className="rounded-2xl bg-slate-950/90 border border-slate-800 p-3 space-y-1.5 text-xs">
          <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
            <span className="text-slate-400 font-bold">Core Meaning:</span>
            <span className="text-emerald-300 font-extrabold">
              {knownInfo?.meaning || charEntry?.meanings?.join('; ')}
            </span>
          </div>
          <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
            <span className="text-slate-400 font-bold">Semantic Radical:</span>
            <span className="text-red-400 font-extrabold">
              {knownInfo?.radical ||
                (charDetails.radStrokes?.length > 0
                  ? `Highlighted in Red (${charDetails.radStrokes.length} strokes)`
                  : 'Highlighted in Red (#dc2626)')}
            </span>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

// ============================================================================
// WORD ENTRY NORMALIZER, DICTATION CHECKER, SPEECH SIMILARITY & SENTENCE PUZZLE
// ============================================================================
function normalizeWordEntry(raw = {}, idx = 0) {
  const hanzi = String(raw.hanzi || raw.simplified || '你好').trim();
  const builtInMatch = UNIT_1_WORDS.find((u) => u.hanzi === hanzi);
  if (builtInMatch && !raw.example && !raw.meaning) {
    return builtInMatch;
  }

  const chars = Array.from(hanzi).filter((ch) => /[\u4e00-\u9fff]/.test(ch));
  const pinyin = String(raw.pinyin || builtInMatch?.pinyin || hanzi).trim();
  const pinyinNum = String(
    raw.pinyinNum || builtInMatch?.pinyinNum || convertPinyinToNumeric(pinyin)
  ).trim();
  const meaning = String(
    raw.meaning || raw.english || builtInMatch?.meaning || 'Mandarin vocabulary'
  ).trim();

  const rawExample = String(raw.example || builtInMatch?.example || '').trim();
  let exampleHanzi = raw.exampleHanzi || builtInMatch?.exampleHanzi || '';
  let exampleEnglish = raw.exampleEnglish || builtInMatch?.exampleEnglish || '';

  if (rawExample.includes('—')) {
    const parts = rawExample.split('—');
    exampleHanzi = exampleHanzi || parts[0].trim();
    exampleEnglish = exampleEnglish || parts.slice(1).join(' — ').trim();
  } else if (rawExample) {
    exampleHanzi = exampleHanzi || rawExample;
    exampleEnglish = exampleEnglish || meaning;
  } else {
    exampleHanzi = exampleHanzi || `${hanzi}！`;
    exampleEnglish = exampleEnglish || meaning;
  }

  const finalExample = `${exampleHanzi} — ${exampleEnglish}`;

  return {
    id: raw.id || builtInMatch?.id || `word_${hanzi}_${idx}`,
    hanzi,
    chars: chars.length > 0 ? chars : Array.from(hanzi),
    pinyin,
    pinyinNum,
    pinyinNumeric: raw.pinyinNumeric || builtInMatch?.pinyinNumeric || [pinyinNum],
    pinyinToneless: stripToneMarks(pinyin),
    homophones: raw.homophones || builtInMatch?.homophones || chars,
    toneNote: raw.toneNote || builtInMatch?.toneNote || `Pinyin: ${pinyin}`,
    meaning,
    english: meaning,
    literal: raw.literal || builtInMatch?.literal || `Characters: ${chars.join(' + ')}`,
    example: finalExample,
    exampleHanzi,
    examplePinyin: raw.examplePinyin || builtInMatch?.examplePinyin || pinyin,
    exampleEnglish,
    usageTip:
      raw.usageTip ||
      builtInMatch?.usageTip ||
      `Practice writing and pronouncing "${hanzi}" (${pinyin}).`
  };
}

function validateDictationInput(userInput = '', targetWord = UNIT_1_WORDS[0], subMode = 'pinyin') {
  const raw = String(userInput || '').trim();
  if (!raw) {
    return {
      isValid: false,
      matchType: 'Empty',
      feedback: 'Type your answer above and press Check.'
    };
  }

  if (subMode === 'hanzi') {
    const cleanUserHanzi = raw.replace(/[\s\p{P}]/gu, '');
    const cleanTargetHanzi = String(targetWord.hanzi || '').replace(/[\s\p{P}]/gu, '');
    if (cleanUserHanzi === cleanTargetHanzi) {
      return {
        isValid: true,
        matchType: 'Exact Hanzi',
        feedback: `${targetWord.hanzi} (${targetWord.pinyin}) — ${targetWord.meaning}`
      };
    }
    return {
      isValid: false,
      matchType: 'Mismatch',
      feedback: `Expected "${targetWord.hanzi}" (${targetWord.pinyin}).`
    };
  }

  // Android Keyboard & Pinyin Normalization: convert 'v' / 'u:' to 'ü', strip spaces, apostrophes, and punctuation
  const normalizeKeepTones = (s) =>
    String(s || '')
      .toLowerCase()
      .replace(/u:/g, 'ü')
      .replace(/v/g, 'ü')
      .replace(/['’\s\p{P}]/gu, '');

  const userToneMarked = normalizeKeepTones(raw);
  const targetToneMarked = normalizeKeepTones(targetWord.pinyin);

  if (userToneMarked === targetToneMarked) {
    return {
      isValid: true,
      matchType: 'Tone-Marked Pinyin',
      feedback: `Spot on! ${targetWord.hanzi} (${targetWord.pinyin}) — ${targetWord.meaning}`
    };
  }

  const userNumeric = convertPinyinToNumeric(raw).replace(/[\s'’\p{P}]/gu, '');
  const numericCandidates = [
    targetWord.pinyinNum,
    ...(targetWord.pinyinNumeric || []),
    convertPinyinToNumeric(targetWord.pinyin)
  ]
    .filter(Boolean)
    .map((c) => String(c).toLowerCase().replace(/u:/g, 'ü').replace(/v/g, 'ü').replace(/[\s'’\p{P}]/gu, ''));

  if (numericCandidates.includes(userNumeric)) {
    return {
      isValid: true,
      matchType: 'Numbered Pinyin',
      feedback: `Correct tones! ${targetWord.hanzi} (${targetWord.pinyin}) — ${targetWord.meaning}`
    };
  }

  const userToneless = stripToneMarks(raw);
  const targetToneless = stripToneMarks(targetWord.pinyin);

  if (userToneless && userToneless === targetToneless) {
    return {
      isValid: true,
      matchType: 'Toneless Pinyin (v → ü normalized)',
      feedback: `Accepted! Full tone-marked Pinyin is "${targetWord.pinyin}" (${targetWord.hanzi}).`
    };
  }

  return {
    isValid: false,
    matchType: 'Incorrect',
    feedback: `Expected "${targetWord.pinyin}" (${targetWord.pinyinNum}) for ${targetWord.hanzi}.`
  };
}

function computeSpeechSimilarity(transcript = '', targetWord = UNIT_1_WORDS[0]) {
  const cleanTranscript = String(transcript || '').replace(/[\s\p{P}]/gu, '');
  const cleanTarget = String(targetWord.hanzi || '').replace(/[\s\p{P}]/gu, '');

  if (!cleanTranscript) {
    return {
      score: 0,
      matchedText: '',
      feedback: 'No speech detected. Try speaking closer to the microphone.'
    };
  }

  if (cleanTranscript.includes(cleanTarget)) {
    return {
      score: 100,
      matchedText: cleanTranscript,
      feedback: `Native-level match! Recognized "${targetWord.hanzi}" (${targetWord.pinyin}).`
    };
  }

  const targetChars = Array.from(cleanTarget);
  const homophonePool = new Set([...targetChars, ...(targetWord.homophones || [])]);
  let exactHits = 0;
  let homophoneHits = 0;

  targetChars.forEach((ch) => {
    if (cleanTranscript.includes(ch)) {
      exactHits += 1;
    }
  });

  Array.from(cleanTranscript).forEach((spokenCh) => {
    if (homophonePool.has(spokenCh)) {
      homophoneHits += 1;
    }
  });

  if (exactHits > 0) {
    const ratio = Math.min(95, Math.round((exactHits / targetChars.length) * 90) + 10);
    return {
      score: ratio,
      matchedText: cleanTranscript,
      feedback: `Good pronunciation! Matched ${exactHits}/${targetChars.length} character(s) of ${targetWord.hanzi}.`
    };
  }

  if (homophoneHits > 0) {
    return {
      score: 75,
      matchedText: cleanTranscript,
      feedback: `Close syllable match ("${cleanTranscript}")! Watch the tone contour on ${targetWord.pinyin}.`
    };
  }

  return {
    score: 25,
    matchedText: cleanTranscript,
    feedback: `Heard "${cleanTranscript}". Listen to ${targetWord.hanzi} (${targetWord.pinyin}) and try again!`
  };
}

function buildSentenceBuilderPuzzle(word = UNIT_1_WORDS[0]) {
  const norm = normalizeWordEntry(word);
  const rawHanzi = String(
    norm.example?.includes('—')
      ? norm.example.split('—')[0].trim()
      : norm.exampleHanzi || `${norm.hanzi}！`
  ).trim();

  const cleanSentence = rawHanzi.replace(/[，。！？、；：“”‘’!?,.\s]/g, '');

  // Segment sentence into logical word chunks while preserving the target word intact
  const tokens = [];
  let cursor = 0;
  while (cursor < cleanSentence.length) {
    if (cleanSentence.slice(cursor).startsWith(norm.hanzi)) {
      tokens.push(norm.hanzi);
      cursor += norm.hanzi.length;
    } else {
      const remaining = cleanSentence.length - cursor;
      const nextTargetIdx = cleanSentence.indexOf(norm.hanzi, cursor);
      const maxStep =
        nextTargetIdx > cursor ? Math.min(2, nextTargetIdx - cursor) : Math.min(2, remaining);
      const step = remaining === 3 && nextTargetIdx === -1 ? 1 : maxStep;
      tokens.push(cleanSentence.slice(cursor, cursor + step));
      cursor += step;
    }
  }

  const baseTiles = tokens.map((text, idx) => ({
    tileId: `tile_${idx}_${text}`,
    text,
    correctIndex: idx
  }));

  const shuffledTiles = [...baseTiles];
  if (shuffledTiles.length > 1) {
    shuffledTiles.reverse();
    if (shuffledTiles.length >= 3) {
      const first = shuffledTiles.shift();
      shuffledTiles.push(first);
    }
  }

  return {
    targetHanziSentence: rawHanzi,
    cleanSentence,
    englishTranslation: norm.exampleEnglish || norm.meaning,
    pinyinHint: norm.examplePinyin || norm.pinyin,
    correctTokens: tokens,
    shuffledTiles
  };
}

// ============================================================================
// FILL-IN-THE-BLANK (选词填空 CLOZE) & TONE SANDHI EAR DRILL (声调辨析) HELPERS
// ============================================================================
const FALLBACK_CLOZE_DISTRACTORS = [
  ...UNIT_1_WORDS,
  normalizeWordEntry({
    id: 'cloze_pengyou',
    hanzi: '朋友',
    pinyin: 'péngyou',
    pinyinNum: 'peng2 you5',
    meaning: 'friend / companion',
    example: '他是我的好朋友。 — He is my good friend.'
  }),
  normalizeWordEntry({
    id: 'cloze_xuexi',
    hanzi: '学习',
    pinyin: 'xuéxí',
    pinyinNum: 'xue2 xi2',
    meaning: 'to study / to learn',
    example: '我每天学习中文。 — I study Chinese every day.'
  })
];

function buildClozeExerciseData(targetWord = UNIT_1_WORDS[0], lessonPool = UNIT_1_WORDS, seed = 0) {
  const normTarget = normalizeWordEntry(targetWord, seed);

  // Extract Mandarin portion before '—' from `example` or fallback to `exampleHanzi`
  const rawExample = String(normTarget.example || '').trim();
  const mandarinPart = rawExample.includes('—')
    ? rawExample.split('—')[0].trim()
    : normTarget.exampleHanzi || `${normTarget.hanzi}！`;

  const englishPart = rawExample.includes('—')
    ? rawExample.split('—').slice(1).join(' — ').trim()
    : normTarget.exampleEnglish || normTarget.meaning;

  const blankSentence = mandarinPart.includes(normTarget.hanzi)
    ? mandarinPart.replace(normTarget.hanzi, '[ ___ ]')
    : `[ ___ ]，${mandarinPart}`;

  // Collect 3 unique distractors from the same lesson (with fallback if lesson has < 4 words)
  const combinedPool = [...(Array.isArray(lessonPool) ? lessonPool : []), ...FALLBACK_CLOZE_DISTRACTORS];
  const distractors = [];
  for (let i = 0; i < combinedPool.length && distractors.length < 3; i++) {
    const candidate = normalizeWordEntry(combinedPool[(i + seed) % combinedPool.length], i);
    if (
      candidate.hanzi !== normTarget.hanzi &&
      !distractors.some((d) => d.hanzi === candidate.hanzi)
    ) {
      distractors.push(candidate);
    }
  }

  const rawOptions = [
    {
      id: `cloze_opt_correct_${normTarget.hanzi}`,
      hanzi: normTarget.hanzi,
      pinyin: normTarget.pinyin,
      meaning: (normTarget.meaning || normTarget.english || '').split(/[;/]/)[0].trim(),
      isCorrect: true
    },
    ...distractors.slice(0, 3).map((d, idx) => ({
      id: `cloze_opt_d_${idx}_${d.hanzi}`,
      hanzi: d.hanzi,
      pinyin: d.pinyin,
      meaning: (d.meaning || d.english || '').split(/[;/]/)[0].trim(),
      isCorrect: false
    }))
  ];

  const shift = (seed + normTarget.hanzi.charCodeAt(0)) % rawOptions.length;
  const options = rawOptions.map((_, idx) => rawOptions[(idx + shift) % rawOptions.length]);

  return {
    mandarinSentence: mandarinPart,
    blankSentence,
    englishSentence: englishPart,
    pinyinSentence: normTarget.examplePinyin || normTarget.pinyin,
    targetWord: normTarget,
    options
  };
}

function formatToneName(t) {
  if (t === 1) return '1st';
  if (t === 2) return '2nd';
  if (t === 3) return '3rd';
  if (t === 4) return '4th';
  return 'Neutral';
}

function deriveWordTonePattern(word = UNIT_1_WORDS[0]) {
  const norm = normalizeWordEntry(word);
  // Extract tone numbers from pinyinNum (e.g. "ni3 hao3", "xie4 xie5") or fallback to Pinyin marks
  const numSource = String(norm.pinyinNum || convertPinyinToNumeric(norm.pinyin)).trim();
  const tokens = numSource.split(/[\s']+/).filter(Boolean);

  let tones = tokens.map((tok) => {
    const m = tok.match(/[1-5]/);
    return m ? Number(m[0]) : 5;
  });

  if (tones.length === 0) {
    const parsed = parsePinyinSyllablesWithTones(norm.pinyin, norm.hanzi);
    tones = parsed.map((s) => s.tone || 5);
  }

  let has33Sandhi = false;
  for (let i = 0; i < tones.length - 1; i++) {
    if (tones[i] === 3 && tones[i + 1] === 3) {
      has33Sandhi = true;
      break;
    }
  }

  let hasBuSandhi = false;
  let hasYiSandhi = false;
  const syllables = parsePinyinSyllablesWithTones(norm.pinyin, norm.hanzi);
  for (let i = 0; i < syllables.length - 1; i++) {
    const cleanSyl = stripToneMarks(syllables[i].syllable);
    if ((norm.hanzi.includes('不') || cleanSyl === 'bu') && tones[i + 1] === 4) {
      hasBuSandhi = true;
    }
    if ((norm.hanzi.includes('一') || cleanSyl === 'yi') && tones[i + 1] === 4) {
      hasYiSandhi = true;
    }
  }

  let label = '';
  let detailNote = '';

  if (has33Sandhi && tones.length === 2) {
    label = '2nd + 3rd (Sandhi from 3+3)';
    detailNote = '3rd + 3rd tone sandhi: first syllable rises like a 2nd tone (3+3 → 2+3).';
  } else if (has33Sandhi) {
    const spokenTones = tones.map((t, idx) => (t === 3 && tones[idx + 1] === 3 ? 2 : t));
    label = `${spokenTones.map(formatToneName).join(' + ')} (Sandhi from ${tones.join('+')})`;
    detailNote = 'Consecutive 3rd tones trigger 3+3 → 2+3 tone sandhi.';
  } else if (hasBuSandhi) {
    label = '2nd + 4th (不 Sandhi: 4+4 → 2+4)';
    detailNote = '不 (bù) shifts to 2nd tone (bú) before a 4th-tone syllable.';
  } else if (hasYiSandhi) {
    label = '2nd + 4th (一 Sandhi: 1+4 → 2+4)';
    detailNote = '一 (yī) shifts to 2nd tone (yí) before a 4th-tone syllable.';
  } else {
    label = tones.map(formatToneName).join(' + ');
    detailNote = `Standard citation tones (${norm.pinyinNum || norm.pinyin}) with no sandhi shift.`;
  }

  return {
    tones,
    label,
    detailNote,
    isSandhi: has33Sandhi || hasBuSandhi || hasYiSandhi
  };
}

function buildToneSandhiOptions(word = UNIT_1_WORDS[0], seed = 0) {
  const correctPattern = deriveWordTonePattern(word);
  const distractorLabels = [
    '2nd + 3rd (Sandhi from 3+3)',
    '4th + Neutral',
    '4th + 4th',
    '1st + 4th',
    '2nd + 1st',
    '3rd + 4th',
    '1st + 2nd',
    '2nd + 4th (不 Sandhi: 4+4 → 2+4)',
    '1st + Neutral'
  ].filter((lbl) => lbl !== correctPattern.label);

  const chosenDistractors = [];
  for (let i = 0; i < distractorLabels.length && chosenDistractors.length < 3; i++) {
    const pick = distractorLabels[(i + seed) % distractorLabels.length];
    if (!chosenDistractors.includes(pick)) {
      chosenDistractors.push(pick);
    }
  }

  const rawChoices = [
    { id: 'tone_correct', label: correctPattern.label, isCorrect: true },
    ...chosenDistractors.map((lbl, idx) => ({
      id: `tone_dist_${idx}`,
      label: lbl,
      isCorrect: false
    }))
  ];

  const shift = (seed + (word.hanzi?.charCodeAt(0) || 0)) % rawChoices.length;
  return {
    correctPattern,
    choices: rawChoices.map((_, i) => rawChoices[(i + shift) % rawChoices.length])
  };
}

// ============================================================================
// REUSABLE CLOZE EXERCISE CARD COMPONENT (选词填空)
// ============================================================================
function ClozeExerciseCard({
  word,
  lessonPool = UNIT_1_WORDS,
  seed = 0,
  selectedOptionId,
  isCompleted,
  cardAnim = 'idle',
  isSlowMode,
  onSetSlowMode,
  speakMandarin,
  speakingState,
  onSelectOption
}) {
  const [showPinyinHint, setShowPinyinHint] = useState(false);

  useEffect(() => {
    setShowPinyinHint(false);
  }, [word?.hanzi, seed]);

  const clozeData = useMemo(
    () => buildClozeExerciseData(word, lessonPool, seed),
    [word, lessonPool, seed]
  );

  return (
    <div
      className={`my-auto rounded-3xl bg-slate-800/90 border p-4 space-y-3.5 shadow-xl transition-all ${
        cardAnim === 'wrong'
          ? 'border-rose-500 animate-game-shake'
          : cardAnim === 'correct'
          ? 'border-emerald-400 animate-game-green-pulse'
          : 'border-cyan-500/40'
      }`}
    >
      <div className="flex items-center justify-between gap-2">
        <span className="text-[10px] font-extrabold uppercase tracking-wider px-2.5 py-1 rounded-full bg-cyan-500/20 text-cyan-300 border border-cyan-500/40">
          Fill-in-the-Blank • 选词填空
        </span>
        <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5">
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={() => {
              soundFX.playPop();
              setShowPinyinHint((prev) => !prev);
            }}
            className="px-2.5 py-1 rounded-xl bg-slate-900 border border-slate-700 text-[10px] font-bold text-amber-300 hover:border-amber-400/50"
          >
            {showPinyinHint ? 'Hide Pinyin' : 'Hint: Pinyin'}
          </button>
          <MandarinAudioControls
            text={clozeData.mandarinSentence}
            isSlowMode={isSlowMode}
            onSetSlowMode={onSetSlowMode}
            speakMandarin={speakMandarin}
            speakingState={speakingState}
            size="sm"
          />
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* Sentence Prompt Card with [ ___ ] Blank */}
      <div className="rounded-2xl bg-slate-950/90 border border-slate-800 p-4 space-y-2 text-center">
        <span className="text-[10px] font-bold uppercase text-amber-400 block">
          Select the missing Mandarin word for [ ___ ]:
        </span>
        <div className="text-xl font-black text-white tracking-wide leading-relaxed">
          {isCompleted ? clozeData.mandarinSentence : clozeData.blankSentence}
        </div>
        {(showPinyinHint || isCompleted) && (
          <div className="text-xs font-bold text-rose-300">{clozeData.pinyinSentence}</div>
        )}
        <div className="text-xs text-slate-300 italic pt-1 border-t border-slate-800/80">
          "{clozeData.englishSentence}"
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* 4 Multiple-Choice Hanzi Options from the Same Lesson */}
      <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-2.5">
        {clozeData.options.map((opt) => {
          const isSelected = selectedOptionId === opt.id;
          let btnClass =
            'bg-slate-900/90 hover:bg-slate-850 border-slate-700 text-white';

          if (isCompleted && opt.isCorrect) {
            btnClass =
              'bg-emerald-500/25 border-emerald-400 text-emerald-200 shadow-lg shadow-emerald-950/50';
          } else if (isSelected && !opt.isCorrect) {
            btnClass = 'bg-rose-500/25 border-rose-500 text-rose-200';
          }

          return (
            <button
              key={opt.id}
              type="button"
              disabled={isCompleted}
              onClick={() => onSelectOption(opt, clozeData)}
              className={`game-btn-3d p-3.5 rounded-2xl border-2 flex flex-col items-center justify-center text-center transition-all ${btnClass}`}
            >
              <span className="text-xl font-black">{opt.hanzi}</span>
              {(showPinyinHint || isCompleted) && (
                <span className="text-[11px] font-bold text-rose-300 mt-0.5">
                  {opt.pinyin}
                </span>
              )}
              {isCompleted && (
                <span className="text-[10px] text-slate-300 mt-0.5 truncate max-w-full">
                  {opt.meaning}
                </span>
              )}
            </button>
          );
        })}
      </div>

      {isCompleted && (
        <div
          className={`rounded-xl p-3 text-xs flex items-center justify-between gap-2 border ${
            clozeData.options.find((o) => o.id === selectedOptionId)?.isCorrect
              ? 'bg-emerald-500/15 border-emerald-400/50 text-emerald-200'
              : 'bg-rose-500/15 border-rose-400/50 text-rose-200'
          }`}
        >
          <div>
            <p className="font-black">
              {clozeData.options.find((o) => o.id === selectedOptionId)?.isCorrect
                ? `✓ Correct! ${clozeData.targetWord.hanzi} (${clozeData.targetWord.pinyin})`
                : `✗ Incorrect — Correct answer: ${clozeData.targetWord.hanzi} (${clozeData.targetWord.pinyin})`}
            </p>
            <p className="text-[11px] opacity-90 mt-0.5">
              {clozeData.mandarinSentence} — "{clozeData.englishSentence}"
            </p>
          </div>
          {clozeData.options.find((o) => o.id === selectedOptionId)?.isCorrect ? (
            <CheckCircle2 className="w-5 h-5 text-emerald-400 shrink-0" />
          ) : (
            <X className="w-5 h-5 text-rose-400 shrink-0" />
          )}
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}

// ============================================================================
// TONE & SANDHI EAR DRILL MODAL (声调辨析)
// ============================================================================
function ToneSandhiDrillModal({
  words = UNIT_1_WORDS,
  isSlowMode,
  onSetSlowMode,
  speakMandarin,
  speakingState,
  onRecordSkillEvent,
  onClose,
  onComplete
}) {
  const drillQueue = useMemo(() => {
    const base = Array.isArray(words) && words.length > 0 ? words : UNIT_1_WORDS;
    const expanded = [];
    const targetLen = Math.max(base.length, 6);
    for (let i = 0; i < targetLen; i++) {
      expanded.push(normalizeWordEntry(base[i % base.length], i));
    }
    return expanded;
  }, [words]);

  const [stepIdx, setStepIdx] = useState(0);
  const [selectedChoiceId, setSelectedChoiceId] = useState(null);
  const [isAnswered, setIsAnswered] = useState(false);
  const [wasCorrect, setWasCorrect] = useState(false);
  const [cardAnim, setCardAnim] = useState('idle');
  const [combo, setCombo] = useState(0);
  const [maxCombo, setMaxCombo] = useState(0);
  const [bonusXp, setBonusXp] = useState(0);
  const [xpPopupKey, setXpPopupKey] = useState(0);
  const [xpPopupAmount, setXpPopupAmount] = useState(15);

  const feedbackTimerRef = useRef(null);
  const currentWord = drillQueue[stepIdx] || drillQueue[0];
  const drillData = useMemo(
    () => buildToneSandhiOptions(currentWord, stepIdx),
    [currentWord, stepIdx]
  );

  const clearFeedbackTimer = useCallback(() => {
    if (feedbackTimerRef.current) {
      clearTimeout(feedbackTimerRef.current);
      feedbackTimerRef.current = null;
    }
  }, []);

  useEffect(() => {
    clearFeedbackTimer();
    setSelectedChoiceId(null);
    setIsAnswered(false);
    setWasCorrect(false);
    setCardAnim('idle');
    speakMandarin(currentWord.hanzi);
    return () => clearFeedbackTimer();
  }, [stepIdx, currentWord.hanzi, speakMandarin, clearFeedbackTimer]);

  const handleCloseModal = () => {
    clearFeedbackTimer();
    onClose();
  };

  const handleSelectToneChoice = (choice) => {
    if (isAnswered) return;
    clearFeedbackTimer();
    setSelectedChoiceId(choice.id);
    setIsAnswered(true);

    let nextBonusXp = bonusXp;
    let nextMaxCombo = maxCombo;

    if (choice.isCorrect) {
      setWasCorrect(true);
      setCardAnim('correct');
      setTimeout(() => setCardAnim('idle'), 500);
      onRecordSkillEvent?.(currentWord, 'listening', { mistakesDelta: 0, completionsDelta: 1 });

      const nextCombo = combo + 1;
      nextMaxCombo = Math.max(maxCombo, nextCombo);
      setCombo(nextCombo);
      setMaxCombo(nextMaxCombo);
      soundFX.playCorrect(nextCombo);
      const earned = 15 + (nextCombo >= 2 ? nextCombo * 3 : 0);
      nextBonusXp = bonusXp + earned;
      setBonusXp(nextBonusXp);
      setXpPopupAmount(earned);
      setXpPopupKey((k) => k + 1);
      speakMandarin(currentWord.hanzi);
    } else {
      setWasCorrect(false);
      soundFX.playMistake();
      setCombo(0);
      setCardAnim('wrong');
      setTimeout(() => setCardAnim('idle'), 450);
      onRecordSkillEvent?.(currentWord, 'listening', { mistakesDelta: 1, completionsDelta: 0 });
    }

    const delayMs = choice.isCorrect ? 1400 : 1800;
    feedbackTimerRef.current = setTimeout(() => {
      if (stepIdx < drillQueue.length - 1) {
        setStepIdx((prev) => prev + 1);
      } else {
        onComplete(drillQueue, 60 + nextBonusXp, nextMaxCombo);
      }
    }, delayMs);
  };

  return (
    <div className="fixed inset-0 z-50 bg-slate-950/95 backdrop-blur-md flex justify-center">
      <div className="w-full max-w-md bg-slate-900 min-h-screen flex flex-col justify-between p-4 overflow-y-auto">
        <SessionGameTopHud
          title="Tone & Sandhi Ear Drill • 声调辨析"
          subtitle={`Item ${stepIdx + 1} of ${drillQueue.length} • Listen & Identify Tones`}
          totalSegments={drillQueue.length}
          completedSegments={stepIdx + (isAnswered ? 1 : 0)}
          combo={combo}
          xpPopupKey={xpPopupKey}
          xpPopupAmount={xpPopupAmount}
          onClose={handleCloseModal}
        />

        <div className="my-auto space-y-4 py-3">
          <div
            className={`rounded-3xl bg-slate-800/90 border p-5 text-center space-y-3 shadow-xl transition-all ${
              cardAnim === 'wrong'
                ? 'border-rose-500 animate-game-shake'
                : cardAnim === 'correct'
                ? 'border-emerald-400 animate-game-green-pulse'
                : 'border-amber-500/40'
            }`}
          >
            <div className="flex items-center justify-between gap-2">
              <span className="text-[10px] font-extrabold uppercase tracking-wider px-2.5 py-1 rounded-full bg-amber-500/20 text-amber-300 border border-amber-400/40 flex items-center gap-1">
                <Headphones className="w-3 h-3" />
                <span>Pinyin Hidden • Ear Check</span>
              </span>
              <MandarinAudioControls
                text={currentWord.hanzi}
                isSlowMode={isSlowMode}
                onSetSlowMode={onSetSlowMode}
                speakMandarin={speakMandarin}
                speakingState={speakingState}
              />
            </div>

            <div className="py-2 space-y-1.5">
              <div className="text-5xl font-black text-white tracking-wider">
                {currentWord.hanzi}
              </div>
              <div className="text-xs font-bold text-slate-400">
                Meaning: <span className="text-emerald-300">{currentWord.meaning || currentWord.english}</span>
              </div>

              {isAnswered ? (
                <div className="pt-2 space-y-1.5">
                  <div className="text-lg font-extrabold text-rose-400">
                    {currentWord.pinyin}{' '}
                    <span className="text-xs text-slate-400">({currentWord.pinyinNum})</span>
                  </div>
                  <ChaoToneVisualizer
                    pinyin={currentWord.pinyin}
                    hanzi={currentWord.hanzi}
                    compact
                  />
                </div>
              ) : (
                <div className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 px-3 py-1 rounded-full bg-slate-950 border border-slate-800 text-[11px] font-bold text-amber-300 mt-1">
                  <span>🔊 Listen carefully and choose the spoken tone pattern</span>
                </div>
              )}
            </div>
          </div>

          {/* 4 Tone Pattern Choices derived from pinyinNum & Sandhi Rules */}
          <div className="space-y-2">
            <span className="text-[10px] font-extrabold uppercase tracking-wider text-slate-400 block">
              Select the correct Tone & Sandhi pattern:
            </span>
            {drillData.choices.map((choice) => {
              const isSelected = selectedChoiceId === choice.id;
              let btnStyle =
                'bg-slate-800/90 hover:bg-slate-800 border-slate-700 text-slate-100';
              if (isAnswered && choice.isCorrect) {
                btnStyle =
                  'bg-emerald-500/20 border-emerald-400 text-emerald-200 font-black';
              } else if (isAnswered && isSelected && !choice.isCorrect) {
                btnStyle = 'bg-rose-500/20 border-rose-500 text-rose-200 font-bold';
              } else if (isAnswered) {
                btnStyle = 'bg-slate-900 border-slate-800 text-slate-500 opacity-60';
              }

              return (
                <button
                  key={choice.id}
                  type="button"
                  disabled={isAnswered}
                  onClick={() => handleSelectToneChoice(choice)}
                  className={`game-btn-3d w-full p-3.5 rounded-2xl border text-left text-xs font-bold flex items-center justify-between transition-all ${btnStyle}`}
                >
                  <span>{choice.label}</span>
                  {isAnswered && choice.isCorrect && (
                    <Check className="w-4 h-4 text-emerald-400 shrink-0" />
                  )}
                  {isAnswered && isSelected && !choice.isCorrect && (
                    <X className="w-4 h-4 text-rose-400 shrink-0" />
                  )}
                </button>
              );
            })}
          </div>

          {isAnswered && (
            <div
              className={`rounded-2xl p-3 text-xs space-y-1 border ${
                wasCorrect
                  ? 'bg-emerald-500/15 border-emerald-400/50 text-emerald-200'
                  : 'bg-rose-500/15 border-rose-400/50 text-rose-200'
              }`}
            >
              <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
                <span className="font-black">
                  {wasCorrect
                    ? `✓ Correct! ${drillData.correctPattern.label}`
                    : `✗ Incorrect — Correct answer: ${drillData.correctPattern.label}`}
                </span>
                {drillData.correctPattern.isSandhi && (
                  <span className="text-[10px] font-black px-2 py-0.5 rounded-full bg-amber-500/20 text-amber-300 border border-amber-400/40">
                    ⚡ Sandhi Active
                  </span>
                )}
              </div>
              <p className="text-[11px] opacity-90">{drillData.correctPattern.detailNote}</p>
            </div>
          )}
        </div>

        <div className="pt-1 text-center text-[11px] font-bold text-slate-400">
          {isAnswered
            ? 'Auto-advancing to next item...'
            : 'Tap a tone pattern above to answer'}
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

// ============================================================================
// HSK MOCK EXAM GENERATOR & MODAL (模拟测试 • 20 QUESTIONS • 15-MIN TIMER)
// ============================================================================
function buildHskMockExamQuestions(unlockedWords = UNIT_1_WORDS) {
  const basePool =
    Array.isArray(unlockedWords) && unlockedWords.length > 0
      ? unlockedWords.map((w, idx) => normalizeWordEntry(w, idx))
      : UNIT_1_WORDS;

  const distractorPool = [...basePool, ...FALLBACK_CLOZE_DISTRACTORS];
  const shuffledSource = [...basePool].sort(() => Math.random() - 0.5);

  const pickDistractors = (targetWord, count = 3, seed = 0) => {
    const picked = [];
    for (let i = 0; i < distractorPool.length && picked.length < count; i++) {
      const cand = normalizeWordEntry(
        distractorPool[(i + seed) % distractorPool.length],
        i
      );
      if (
        cand.hanzi !== targetWord.hanzi &&
        !picked.some((p) => p.hanzi === cand.hanzi)
      ) {
        picked.push(cand);
      }
    }
    return picked;
  };

  const questions = [];

  // Part I: 10 Listening Questions (1–10)
  for (let i = 0; i < 10; i++) {
    const word = normalizeWordEntry(
      shuffledSource[i % shuffledSource.length],
      i
    );
    const distractors = pickDistractors(word, 3, i * 3 + 1);
    const isSentenceListening = i % 2 === 1;

    const rawOptions = isSentenceListening
      ? [
          {
            id: `listen_${i}_correct`,
            label: `${word.hanzi} (${word.pinyin}) — ${
              (word.meaning || word.english).split(/[;/]/)[0]
            }`,
            isCorrect: true
          },
          ...distractors.map((d, dIdx) => ({
            id: `listen_${i}_d_${dIdx}`,
            label: `${d.hanzi} (${d.pinyin}) — ${
              (d.meaning || d.english).split(/[;/]/)[0]
            }`,
            isCorrect: false
          }))
        ]
      : [
          {
            id: `listen_${i}_correct`,
            label: `${word.meaning || word.english} (${word.pinyin})`,
            isCorrect: true
          },
          ...distractors.map((d, dIdx) => ({
            id: `listen_${i}_d_${dIdx}`,
            label: `${d.meaning || d.english} (${d.pinyin})`,
            isCorrect: false
          }))
        ];

    const shift = (i + word.hanzi.charCodeAt(0)) % rawOptions.length;
    const options = rawOptions.map(
      (_, idx) => rawOptions[(idx + shift) % rawOptions.length]
    );
    const correctOption = options.find((o) => o.isCorrect) || options[0];

    questions.push({
      qNumber: i + 1,
      section: 'Listening',
      subType: isSentenceListening ? 'Sentence Audio Match' : 'Word Audio Comprehension',
      word,
      audioText: isSentenceListening
        ? word.exampleHanzi || word.hanzi
        : word.hanzi,
      prompt: isSentenceListening
        ? 'Listen to the spoken Mandarin sentence and select the matching vocabulary item:'
        : 'Listen to the spoken Mandarin word and choose the correct meaning & Pinyin:',
      displayPromptHanzi: null,
      displayPromptSub: isSentenceListening
        ? 'Audio Prompt: Spoken Mandarin Sentence'
        : 'Audio Prompt: Spoken Mandarin Vocabulary',
      options,
      correctOptionId: correctOption.id,
      correctAnswerText: `${word.hanzi} (${word.pinyin}) — ${word.meaning || word.english}`
    });
  }

  // Part II: 10 Reading / Cloze Questions (11–20)
  for (let j = 0; j < 10; j++) {
    const qNum = j + 11;
    const word = normalizeWordEntry(
      shuffledSource[(j + 2) % shuffledSource.length],
      j + 10
    );
    const isCloze = j % 2 === 0;

    if (isCloze) {
      const cloze = buildClozeExerciseData(word, basePool, j + 5);
      const options = cloze.options.map((opt) => ({
        id: `read_${qNum}_${opt.id}`,
        label: `${opt.hanzi} (${opt.pinyin})`,
        isCorrect: opt.isCorrect
      }));
      const correctOption = options.find((o) => o.isCorrect) || options[0];

      questions.push({
        qNumber: qNum,
        section: 'Reading',
        subType: 'Cloze Sentence (选词填空)',
        word,
        audioText: cloze.mandarinSentence,
        prompt: 'Choose the best Mandarin word to complete the sentence blank [ ___ ]:',
        displayPromptHanzi: cloze.blankSentence,
        displayPromptSub: `"${cloze.englishSentence}"`,
        options,
        correctOptionId: correctOption.id,
        correctAnswerText: `${word.hanzi} (${word.pinyin}) in "${cloze.mandarinSentence}"`
      });
    } else {
      const distractors = pickDistractors(word, 3, j * 5 + 2);
      const rawOptions = [
        {
          id: `read_${qNum}_correct`,
          label: `${word.pinyin} — ${word.meaning || word.english}`,
          isCorrect: true
        },
        ...distractors.map((d, dIdx) => ({
          id: `read_${qNum}_d_${dIdx}`,
          label: `${d.pinyin} — ${d.meaning || d.english}`,
          isCorrect: false
        }))
      ];
      const shift = (j + word.hanzi.charCodeAt(0)) % rawOptions.length;
      const options = rawOptions.map(
        (_, idx) => rawOptions[(idx + shift) % rawOptions.length]
      );
      const correctOption = options.find((o) => o.isCorrect) || options[0];

      questions.push({
        qNumber: qNum,
        section: 'Reading',
        subType: 'Hanzi Reading Comprehension',
        word,
        audioText: word.hanzi,
        prompt: 'Select the correct Pinyin and English meaning for this character/word:',
        displayPromptHanzi: word.hanzi,
        displayPromptSub: `Context: ${word.exampleHanzi || word.hanzi}`,
        options,
        correctOptionId: correctOption.id,
        correctAnswerText: `${word.hanzi} (${word.pinyin}) — ${word.meaning || word.english}`
      });
    }
  }

  return questions;
}

function HskMockExamModal({
  words = UNIT_1_WORDS,
  isSlowMode,
  onSetSlowMode,
  speakMandarin,
  speakingState,
  onRecordSkillEvent,
  onClose,
  onComplete
}) {
  const questions = useMemo(() => buildHskMockExamQuestions(words), [words]);
  const [currentIdx, setCurrentIdx] = useState(0);
  const [answersMap, setAnswersMap] = useState({}); // { [qNumber]: optionId }
  const [timeLeftSec, setTimeLeftSec] = useState(15 * 60); // 15 minutes (900s)
  const [submitted, setSubmitted] = useState(false);
  const [confirmEarlySubmit, setConfirmEarlySubmit] = useState(false);
  const [isAutoAdvancing, setIsAutoAdvancing] = useState(false);

  const feedbackTimerRef = useRef(null);
  const currentQ = questions[currentIdx] || questions[0];
  const answeredCount = Object.keys(answersMap).length;

  const clearFeedbackTimer = useCallback(() => {
    if (feedbackTimerRef.current) {
      clearTimeout(feedbackTimerRef.current);
      feedbackTimerRef.current = null;
    }
  }, []);

  useEffect(() => {
    setIsAutoAdvancing(false);
    return () => clearFeedbackTimer();
  }, [currentIdx, clearFeedbackTimer]);

  // Auto-play audio when entering a Listening question (1–10)
  useEffect(() => {
    if (!submitted && currentQ?.section === 'Listening' && currentQ?.audioText) {
      speakMandarin(currentQ.audioText);
    }
  }, [currentIdx, submitted, currentQ, speakMandarin]);

  // 15-Minute Countdown Timer
  useEffect(() => {
    if (submitted) return;
    if (timeLeftSec <= 0) {
      handleFinalizeExam();
      return;
    }
    const timerId = setTimeout(() => {
      setTimeLeftSec((prev) => Math.max(0, prev - 1));
    }, 1000);
    return () => clearTimeout(timerId);
  }, [timeLeftSec, submitted]);

  const formatTimer = (secs) => {
    const m = Math.floor(secs / 60);
    const s = secs % 60;
    return `${String(m).padStart(2, '0')}:${String(s).padStart(2, '0')}`;
  };

  const handleSelectAnswer = (optionId) => {
    if (submitted || isAutoAdvancing) return;
    clearFeedbackTimer();
    soundFX.playPop();
    setIsAutoAdvancing(true);
    setAnswersMap((prev) => ({
      ...prev,
      [currentQ.qNumber]: optionId
    }));

    // Auto-advance after 500ms without revealing right/wrong feedback
    feedbackTimerRef.current = setTimeout(() => {
      setIsAutoAdvancing(false);
      if (currentIdx < questions.length - 1) {
        setCurrentIdx((idx) => Math.min(questions.length - 1, idx + 1));
      }
    }, 500);
  };

  const handleFinalizeExam = () => {
    if (submitted) return;
    clearFeedbackTimer();
    setSubmitted(true);
    setConfirmEarlySubmit(false);
    soundFX.playVictory();

    questions.forEach((q) => {
      const picked = answersMap[q.qNumber];
      const isRight = picked === q.correctOptionId;
      const skillKey = q.section === 'Listening' ? 'listening' : 'recognition';
      onRecordSkillEvent?.(q.word, skillKey, {
        mistakesDelta: isRight ? 0 : 1,
        completionsDelta: isRight ? 1 : 0
      });
    });
  };

  // Compute Final Score Report
  const examReport = useMemo(() => {
    let listeningCorrect = 0;
    let readingCorrect = 0;
    const missedItems = [];

    questions.forEach((q) => {
      const chosenId = answersMap[q.qNumber];
      const chosenOption = q.options.find((o) => o.id === chosenId);
      const isCorrect = chosenId === q.correctOptionId;

      if (isCorrect) {
        if (q.section === 'Listening') listeningCorrect += 1;
        else readingCorrect += 1;
      } else {
        missedItems.push({
          ...q,
          userAnswerLabel: chosenOption ? chosenOption.label : 'No Answer Selected'
        });
      }
    });

    const totalCorrect = listeningCorrect + readingCorrect;
    const percentage = Math.round((totalCorrect / questions.length) * 100);
    const passed = totalCorrect >= 12; // 60% Pass threshold (12/20)
    const xpReward = Math.max(40, totalCorrect * 6 + (passed ? 30 : 0));

    return {
      listeningCorrect,
      readingCorrect,
      totalCorrect,
      percentage,
      passed,
      missedItems,
      xpReward
    };
  }, [questions, answersMap]);

  return (
    <div className="fixed inset-0 z-50 bg-slate-950/95 backdrop-blur-md flex justify-center">
      <div className="w-full max-w-md bg-slate-900 min-h-screen flex flex-col justify-between p-4 overflow-y-auto">
        {/* Top Exam Header */}
        <div className="space-y-2.5 border-b border-slate-800 pb-3">
          <div className="flex items-center justify-between gap-2">
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={onClose}
              className="game-btn-3d p-2 rounded-xl bg-slate-800 hover:bg-slate-700 text-slate-300 flex items-center gap-1 text-xs font-bold"
            >
              <ArrowLeft className="w-4 h-4" />
              <span>Exit</span>
            </button>

            <div className="text-center">
              <span className="text-[11px] font-black uppercase tracking-wider text-amber-400 block">
                HSK Mock Exam • 模拟测试
              </span>
              <span className="text-[10px] text-slate-400">
                {submitted
                  ? 'Official Score Report'
                  : `Answered ${answeredCount} / 20 • No Instant Feedback`}
              </span>
            </div>

            <div
              className={`px-2.5 py-1 rounded-xl border text-xs font-black flex items-center gap-1 ${
                !submitted && timeLeftSec <= 120
                  ? 'bg-rose-600/30 border-rose-400 text-rose-200 animate-pulse'
                  : 'bg-slate-950 border-slate-800 text-amber-300'
              }`}
            >
              <Clock className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
              <span>{formatTimer(timeLeftSec)}</span>
            </div>
          </div>

          {/* 20-Question Navigator Strip */}
          {!submitted && (
            <div className="grid grid-cols-10 gap-1 pt-0.5">
              {questions.map((q, idx) => {
                const isAnswered = Boolean(answersMap[q.qNumber]);
                const isCurrent = idx === currentIdx;
                return (
                  <button
                    key={q.qNumber}
                    type="button"
                    onClick={() => {
                      soundFX.playPop();
                      setCurrentIdx(idx);
                    }}
                    className={`h-6 rounded-lg text-[10px] font-black border transition-all ${
                      isCurrent
                        ? 'bg-amber-500 text-slate-950 border-amber-200 scale-105 shadow'
                        : isAnswered
                        ? 'bg-sky-500/25 border-sky-400/60 text-sky-200'
                        : 'bg-slate-950 border-slate-800 text-slate-500'
                    }`}
                  >
                    {q.qNumber}
                  </button>
                );
              })}
            </div>
          )}
        </div>

        {/* EXAM IN-PROGRESS VIEW (NO RIGHT/WRONG FEEDBACK UNTIL SUBMISSION) */}
        {!submitted ? (
          <div className="my-auto space-y-4 py-3">
            <div className="rounded-3xl bg-slate-800/90 border border-slate-700 p-4 space-y-3.5 shadow-xl">
              <div className="flex items-center justify-between gap-2">
                <span
                  className={`text-[10px] font-extrabold uppercase tracking-wider px-2.5 py-1 rounded-full border ${
                    currentQ.section === 'Listening'
                      ? 'bg-emerald-500/20 text-emerald-300 border-emerald-400/40'
                      : 'bg-sky-500/20 text-sky-300 border-sky-400/40'
                  }`}
                >
                  Part {currentQ.section === 'Listening' ? 'I • Listening (1–10)' : 'II • Reading (11–20)'}
                </span>
                <span className="text-xs font-black text-slate-300">
                  Question {currentQ.qNumber} / 20
                </span>
              </div>

              <p className="text-xs font-bold text-slate-300">{currentQ.prompt}</p>

              {/* Question Prompt Box */}
              <div className="rounded-2xl bg-slate-950/90 border border-slate-800 p-4 text-center space-y-2.5">
                {currentQ.section === 'Listening' ? (
                  <div className="flex flex-col items-center gap-2 py-2">
                    <div className="w-12 h-12 rounded-2xl bg-emerald-500/15 border border-emerald-400/40 flex items-center justify-center text-emerald-300">
                      <Headphones className="w-6 h-6" />
                    </div>
                    <p className="text-xs font-bold text-slate-300">
                      {currentQ.displayPromptSub}
                    </p>
                    <MandarinAudioControls
                      text={currentQ.audioText}
                      isSlowMode={isSlowMode}
                      onSetSlowMode={onSetSlowMode}
                      speakMandarin={speakMandarin}
                      speakingState={speakingState}
                    />
                  </div>
                ) : (
                  <div className="space-y-1.5 py-1">
                    <div className="text-2xl font-black text-white tracking-wide">
                      {currentQ.displayPromptHanzi}
                    </div>
                    <div className="text-xs text-slate-400 italic">
                      {currentQ.displayPromptSub}
                    </div>
                  </div>
                )}
              </div>

              {/* 4 Multiple-Choice Options (Neutral selection highlight, no spoilers) */}
              <div className="space-y-2">
                {currentQ.options.map((opt, oIdx) => {
                  const isPicked = answersMap[currentQ.qNumber] === opt.id;
                  const letter = ['A', 'B', 'C', 'D'][oIdx] || '•';
                  return (
                    <button
                      key={opt.id}
                      type="button"
                      onClick={() => handleSelectAnswer(opt.id)}
                      className={`game-btn-3d w-full p-3.5 rounded-2xl border text-left text-xs font-bold flex items-center justify-between gap-2 transition-all ${
                        isPicked
                          ? 'bg-amber-500/20 border-amber-400 text-amber-200 font-extrabold shadow-md'
                          : 'bg-slate-900/90 hover:bg-slate-850 border-slate-700 text-slate-200'
                      }`}
                    >
                      <div className="flex items-center gap-2.5 min-w-0">
                        <span
                          className={`w-6 h-6 rounded-lg text-[11px] font-black flex items-center justify-center shrink-0 ${
                            isPicked
                              ? 'bg-amber-400 text-slate-950'
                              : 'bg-slate-800 text-slate-400'
                          }`}
                        >
                          {letter}
                        </span>
                        <span className="truncate">{opt.label}</span>
                      </div>
                      {isPicked && (
                        <span className="text-[10px] font-black uppercase px-2 py-0.5 rounded bg-amber-400/20 text-amber-300 shrink-0">
                          Selected
                        </span>
                      )}
                    </button>
                  );
                })}
              </div>
            </div>

            {confirmEarlySubmit && (
              <div className="rounded-2xl bg-amber-500/15 border border-amber-400/50 p-3.5 space-y-2.5 text-xs">
                <p className="font-bold text-amber-200">
                  You have answered {answeredCount} of 20 questions. Submit your HSK Mock Exam now?
                </p>
                <div className="flex gap-2">
                  <button
                    type="button"
                    onClick={() => setConfirmEarlySubmit(false)}
                    className="flex-1 py-2 rounded-xl bg-slate-900 text-slate-300 font-bold"
                  >
                    Keep Working
                  </button>
                  <button
                    type="button"
                    onClick={handleFinalizeExam}
                    className="flex-1 py-2 rounded-xl bg-emerald-500 text-slate-950 font-black"
                  >
                    Submit & Grade Exam
                  </button>
                </div>
              </div>
            )}
          </div>
        ) : (
          /* FINAL HSK SCORE REPORT VIEW (PASS THRESHOLD: 60% / 12/20) */
          <div className="my-auto space-y-4 py-3">
            <div
              className={`rounded-3xl border p-5 text-center space-y-3 shadow-2xl ${
                examReport.passed
                  ? 'bg-gradient-to-b from-emerald-950/70 to-slate-900 border-emerald-400/50'
                  : 'bg-gradient-to-b from-rose-950/60 to-slate-900 border-rose-500/50'
              }`}
            >
              <div className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 px-3 py-1 rounded-full text-xs font-black uppercase border bg-slate-950/80 border-slate-700">
                {examReport.passed ? (
                  <span className="text-emerald-400">★ HSK Mock Exam Passed (合格)</span>
                ) : (
                  <span className="text-rose-400">Needs Review • Pass Mark: 12/20 (60%)</span>
                )}
              </div>

              <div className="text-4xl font-black text-white">
                {examReport.totalCorrect} / 20{' '}
                <span className="text-lg text-amber-300">({examReport.percentage}%)</span>
              </div>

              <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-2.5 pt-1">
                <div className="rounded-2xl bg-slate-950/80 border border-slate-800 p-2.5">
                  <div className="text-[10px] font-extrabold uppercase text-emerald-400">
                    Listening (听力)
                  </div>
                  <div className="text-base font-black text-white mt-0.5">
                    {examReport.listeningCorrect} / 10
                  </div>
                </div>
                <div className="rounded-2xl bg-slate-950/80 border border-slate-800 p-2.5">
                  <div className="text-[10px] font-extrabold uppercase text-sky-400">
                    Reading (阅读)
                  </div>
                  <div className="text-base font-black text-white mt-0.5">
                    {examReport.readingCorrect} / 10
                  </div>
                </div>
              </div>
            </div>

            {/* Missed Questions Review List */}
            <div className="rounded-2xl bg-slate-800/85 border border-slate-700 p-3.5 space-y-2.5">
              <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
                <h3 className="text-xs font-extrabold uppercase tracking-wider text-slate-300">
                  Missed Items Review ({examReport.missedItems.length})
                </h3>
                <span className="text-[10px] font-bold text-amber-300">
                  Tap Speaker to Listen
                </span>
              </div>

              {examReport.missedItems.length === 0 ? (
                <div className="rounded-xl bg-emerald-500/15 border border-emerald-400/40 p-3 text-center text-xs font-bold text-emerald-200">
                  Perfect 20/20 Score! Zero missed questions!
                </div>
              ) : (
                <div className="space-y-2 max-h-56 overflow-y-auto pr-1">
                  {examReport.missedItems.map((item) => (
                    <div
                      key={item.qNumber}
                      className="rounded-xl bg-slate-950/90 border border-rose-500/30 p-2.5 text-xs space-y-1"
                    >
                      <div className="flex items-center justify-between gap-2">
                        <span className="font-black text-rose-300">
                          Q{item.qNumber} ({item.section} • {item.subType})
                        </span>
                        <button
                          type="button"
                          onClick={() => speakMandarin(item.audioText || item.word.hanzi)}
                          className="px-2 py-0.5 rounded-lg bg-slate-800 hover:bg-slate-700 text-amber-300 text-[10px] font-bold flex items-center gap-1"
                        >
                          <Volume2 className="w-3 h-3" />
                          <span>{item.word.hanzi}</span>
                        </button>
                      </div>
                      <p className="text-[11px] text-slate-400">
                        Your Answer:{' '}
                        <span className="text-rose-300 font-semibold">
                          {item.userAnswerLabel}
                        </span>
                      </p>
                      <p className="text-[11px] text-emerald-300 font-bold">
                        ✓ Correct: {item.correctAnswerText}
                      </p>
                    </div>
                  ))}
                </div>
              )}
            </div>
          </div>
        )}

        {/* Bottom Navigation / Submit Controls */}
        {!submitted ? (
          <div className="flex items-center gap-2 pt-2">
            <button
              type="button"
              disabled={currentIdx === 0}
              onClick={() => {
                clearFeedbackTimer();
                soundFX.playPop();
                setCurrentIdx((i) => Math.max(0, i - 1));
              }}
              className="game-btn-3d px-3.5 py-3.5 rounded-xl bg-slate-800 disabled:opacity-40 text-xs font-bold text-slate-200 border-b-4 border-slate-950"
            >
              Prev
            </button>

            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={() => {
                clearFeedbackTimer();
                if (answeredCount < 20 && !confirmEarlySubmit) {
                  setConfirmEarlySubmit(true);
                } else {
                  handleFinalizeExam();
                }
              }}
              className="game-btn-3d flex-1 py-3.5 rounded-xl bg-gradient-to-r from-amber-500 to-emerald-500 text-slate-950 font-black text-xs shadow-lg border-b-4 border-emerald-700"
            >
              Submit Exam ({answeredCount}/20 Answered)
            </button>
          </div>
        ) : (
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={() => {
              const uniqueWords = [];
              questions.forEach((q) => {
                if (!uniqueWords.some((w) => w.hanzi === q.word.hanzi)) {
                  uniqueWords.push(q.word);
                }
              });
              onComplete(uniqueWords, examReport.xpReward, examReport.totalCorrect);
            }}
            className="game-btn-3d w-full py-3.5 rounded-xl bg-gradient-to-r from-amber-500 via-rose-500 to-emerald-500 text-slate-950 font-black text-xs shadow-lg border-b-4 border-emerald-700 flex items-center justify-center gap-2"
          >
            <Trophy className="w-4 h-4" />
            <span>Claim HSK Exam Reward (+{examReport.xpReward} XP)</span>
          </button>
        )}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

// ============================================================================
// PROGRESSIVE MODE UNLOCKING & DAILY QUESTS GENERATOR
// ============================================================================
const ADVANCED_MODE_UNLOCK_THRESHOLDS = {
  sentence_builder: 12,
  tone_sandhi_drill: 12,
  cloze: 25,
  blitz_match: 25,
  hsk_mock_exam: 40
};

function computeUnlockedWordsPool(courses = [], activeCourse = null, activeLesson = null, progress = {}) {
  const wordMap = new window.Map();

  // Scan all courses and lessons that the user has unlocked or started
  courses.forEach((course) => {
    const lessons = course.lessons || [];
    lessons.forEach((lesson, lIdx) => {
      const lessonKey = `${course.id}__${lesson.id}`;
      const lp = progress.lessonProgress?.[lessonKey];

      // A lesson is unlocked/started if:
      // - It's the very first lesson of the active course (or default course)
      // - It's the currently selected activeLesson
      // - It has recorded progress in lessonProgress
      // - The previous lesson in the same course has completed its Boss stage (stage 4)
      const prevLesson = lIdx > 0 ? lessons[lIdx - 1] : null;
      const prevLessonKey = prevLesson ? `${course.id}__${prevLesson.id}` : null;
      const prevLessonCompleted =
        prevLessonKey &&
        progress.lessonProgress?.[prevLessonKey]?.completedStages?.includes(4);

      const isDefaultUnit1 =
        course.id === 'course_default_unit1' && lesson.id === 'lesson_unit_1';
      const hasDefaultProgress =
        isDefaultUnit1 &&
        ((progress.unlockedStage || 1) > 1 || (progress.completedStages?.length || 0) > 0);

      const isUnlockedOrStarted =
        (course.id === activeCourse?.id && lIdx === 0) ||
        lesson.id === activeLesson?.id ||
        hasDefaultProgress ||
        prevLessonCompleted ||
        Boolean(
          lp &&
            ((lp.unlockedStage || 1) > 1 ||
              (Array.isArray(lp.completedStages) && lp.completedStages.length > 0))
        );

      if (isUnlockedOrStarted) {
        (lesson.words || []).forEach((w, wIdx) => {
          const norm = normalizeWordEntry(w, wIdx);
          if (norm.hanzi && !wordMap.has(norm.hanzi)) {
            wordMap.set(norm.hanzi, norm);
          }
        });
      }
    });
  });

  // Also count words where the user has already earned mastery >= 1
  courses.forEach((course) => {
    (course.lessons || []).forEach((lesson) => {
      (lesson.words || []).forEach((w, wIdx) => {
        const norm = normalizeWordEntry(w, wIdx);
        if (norm.hanzi && getWordMasteryLevel(progress.mastery, norm) >= 1) {
          wordMap.set(norm.hanzi, norm);
        }
      });
    });
  });

  // If the user has completed all 4 stages of the built-in Unit 1 (Lesson 1 done),
  // grant the Lesson 1 completion milestone baseline of 12 unlocked words
  const defaultUnitKey = 'course_default_unit1__lesson_unit_1';
  const defaultUnitDone =
    (progress.completedStages || []).includes(4) ||
    (progress.lessonProgress?.[defaultUnitKey]?.completedStages || []).includes(4);

  const wordsArray = Array.from(wordMap.values());
  const effectiveCount = defaultUnitDone
    ? Math.max(12, wordsArray.length)
    : wordsArray.length;

  return {
    unlockedWords: wordsArray,
    unlockedWordsCount: effectiveCount
  };
}

function hashDateSeed(dateStr = '') {
  let h = 2166136261;
  for (let i = 0; i < dateStr.length; i++) {
    h ^= dateStr.charCodeAt(i);
    h = Math.imul(h, 16777619);
  }
  return Math.abs(h);
}

function generateDeterministicDailyQuests(dateStr = '', unlockedWordsCount = 3) {
  // Always-available core quests + progressively unlocked mode quests
  const candidatePool = [
    {
      id: 'quest_smart_review',
      modeKey: 'smart_review',
      title: 'Finish a Smart Review session',
      xpReward: 30,
      badge: 'Smart Review',
      minWords: 0
    },
    {
      id: 'quest_flashcards',
      modeKey: 'flashcards',
      title: 'Complete 1 Flashcards Discovery run',
      xpReward: 25,
      badge: 'Discovery',
      minWords: 0
    },
    {
      id: 'quest_writing',
      modeKey: 'writing',
      title: 'Complete 1 Stroke Writing session',
      xpReward: 35,
      badge: 'Calligraphy',
      minWords: 0
    },
    {
      id: 'quest_dictation',
      modeKey: 'dictation',
      title: 'Finish 1 Listening Dictation drill',
      xpReward: 30,
      badge: 'Ear Training',
      minWords: 0
    },
    {
      id: 'quest_sentence_builder',
      modeKey: 'sentence_builder',
      title: 'Complete 1 Sentence Builder puzzle',
      xpReward: 35,
      badge: '12+ Words Mode',
      minWords: ADVANCED_MODE_UNLOCK_THRESHOLDS.sentence_builder
    },
    {
      id: 'quest_tone_drill',
      modeKey: 'tone_sandhi_drill',
      title: 'Complete 1 Tone & Sandhi Drill',
      xpReward: 35,
      badge: '12+ Words Mode',
      minWords: ADVANCED_MODE_UNLOCK_THRESHOLDS.tone_sandhi_drill
    },
    {
      id: 'quest_cloze',
      modeKey: 'cloze',
      title: 'Finish 1 Fill-in-the-Blank (Cloze) drill',
      xpReward: 40,
      badge: '25+ Words Mode',
      minWords: ADVANCED_MODE_UNLOCK_THRESHOLDS.cloze
    },
    {
      id: 'quest_blitz',
      modeKey: 'blitz_match',
      title: 'Win a 60s Blitz round',
      xpReward: 50,
      badge: '25+ Words Mode',
      minWords: ADVANCED_MODE_UNLOCK_THRESHOLDS.blitz_match
    },
    {
      id: 'quest_hsk_mock',
      modeKey: 'hsk_mock_exam',
      title: 'Complete an HSK Mock Exam',
      xpReward: 65,
      badge: '40+ Words Mode',
      minWords: ADVANCED_MODE_UNLOCK_THRESHOLDS.hsk_mock_exam
    }
  ];

  // Filter strictly to modes currently unlocked by the user
  const eligible = candidatePool.filter((q) => unlockedWordsCount >= q.minWords);
  const seed = hashDateSeed(dateStr || '2026-11-13');

  // Deterministically shuffle eligible quests using date seed
  const poolCopy = [...eligible];
  for (let i = poolCopy.length - 1; i > 0; i--) {
    const j = (seed + i * 31) % (i + 1);
    const temp = poolCopy[i];
    poolCopy[i] = poolCopy[j];
    poolCopy[j] = temp;
  }

  return poolCopy.slice(0, 3);
}
