        launchType: 'boss',
        allowedModalities: ['recognition']
      }
    ]
  }
];

function buildDynamicModalityQueue(
  words = UNIT_1_WORDS,
  allowedModalities = ['recognition'],
  badgeText = 'Dynamic Mix',
  lessonIndex = 0
) {
  const baseModes =
    Array.isArray(allowedModalities) && allowedModalities.length > 0
      ? [...allowedModalities]
      : ['recognition'];

  // Include Fill-in-the-Blank ('cloze') inside Mix and Gauntlet nodes starting from Lesson 3 onward (lessonIndex >= 2)
  if (lessonIndex >= 2 && baseModes.length > 1 && !baseModes.includes('cloze')) {
    baseModes.push('cloze');
  }
  const modes = baseModes;

  // Expand pool so multi-skill nodes test at least 5–6 steps even on a 3-word unit
  const targetSteps = Math.max(words.length, Math.min(6, words.length * 2));
  const queue = [];

  for (let i = 0; i < targetSteps; i++) {
    const word = normalizeWordEntry(words[i % words.length], i);
    const prev1 = queue.length >= 1 ? queue[queue.length - 1].modality : null;
    const prev2 = queue.length >= 2 ? queue[queue.length - 2].modality : null;

    // Pick a modality without repeating the exact same modality more than twice in a row
    const candidates = modes.filter((m) => !(m === prev1 && m === prev2));
    const poolToPick = candidates.length > 0 ? candidates : modes;
    const chosenModality = poolToPick[(i + Math.floor(Math.random() * poolToPick.length)) % poolToPick.length];

    queue.push({
      stepId: `mix_${word.id}_${chosenModality}_${i}`,
      word,
      modality: chosenModality,
      reason: `${badgeText} • ${MODALITY_META[chosenModality]?.label || chosenModality}`
    });
  }

  return queue;
}

// ============================================================================
// MASTERY, TELEMETRY & BOSS QUESTION BUILDER HELPERS
// ============================================================================
function getWordMasteryLevel(masteryMap = {}, word = {}) {
  if (!word) return 0;
  const byId = masteryMap[word.id];
  const byHanzi = masteryMap[word.hanzi];
  const val = typeof byId === 'number' ? byId : typeof byHanzi === 'number' ? byHanzi : 0;
  return Math.max(0, Math.min(5, val));
}

function getWordSkillTelemetry(telemetryMap = {}, word = {}) {
  const key = word?.hanzi || word?.id || 'default';
  const entry = telemetryMap[key] || telemetryMap[word?.id] || {};
  return {
    recognition: {
      mistakes: entry.recognition?.mistakes || 0,
      completions: entry.recognition?.completions || 0
    },
    writing: {
      mistakes: entry.writing?.mistakes || 0,
      completions: entry.writing?.completions || 0
    },
    listening: {
      mistakes: entry.listening?.mistakes || 0,
      completions: entry.listening?.completions || 0
    },
    speaking: {
      mistakes: entry.speaking?.mistakes || 0,
      completions: entry.speaking?.completions || 0
    },
    sentence_builder: {
      mistakes: entry.sentence_builder?.mistakes || 0,
      completions: entry.sentence_builder?.completions || 0
    },
    cloze: {
      mistakes: entry.cloze?.mistakes || 0,
      completions: entry.cloze?.completions || 0
    }
  };
}

function buildBossQuestionsForWords(words = UNIT_1_WORDS) {
  const normalizedPool = (words.length > 0 ? words : UNIT_1_WORDS).map((w, idx) =>
    normalizeWordEntry(w, idx)
  );
  const fallbackPool = [
    ...normalizedPool,
    ...UNIT_1_WORDS.filter((u) => !normalizedPool.some((p) => p.hanzi === u.hanzi))
  ];

  const questions = [];

  normalizedPool.forEach((word, idx) => {
    const distractors = fallbackPool.filter((w) => w.hanzi !== word.hanzi);
    const d1 = distractors[idx % distractors.length] || UNIT_1_WORDS[1];
    const d2 = distractors[(idx + 1) % distractors.length] || UNIT_1_WORDS[2];

    // Question Type A: Meaning Recognition
    const meaningOpts = [
      { id: `${word.id}_m_correct`, text: word.meaning || word.english, isCorrect: true },
      { id: `${word.id}_m_d1`, text: d1.meaning || d1.english, isCorrect: false },
      { id: `${word.id}_m_d2`, text: d2.meaning || d2.english, isCorrect: false }
    ];
    const rotatedMeaningOpts = meaningOpts.map(
      (_, oIdx) => meaningOpts[(oIdx + idx) % meaningOpts.length]
    );

    questions.push({
      id: `boss_q_meaning_${word.id}_${idx}`,
      wordId: word.id,
      prompt: 'Select the correct English meaning for this Mandarin word:',
      focusHanzi: word.hanzi,
      focusPinyin: word.pinyin,
      speechText: word.hanzi,
      explanation: `${word.hanzi} (${word.pinyin}) means "${word.meaning || word.english}".`,
      options: rotatedMeaningOpts
    });

    // Question Type B: Pinyin & Context Match
    const pinyinOpts = [
      {
        id: `${word.id}_p_correct`,
        text: `${word.pinyin} — "${(word.meaning || word.english).split(/[;/]/)[0]}"`,
        isCorrect: true
      },
      {
        id: `${word.id}_p_d1`,
        text: `${d1.pinyin} — "${(d1.meaning || d1.english).split(/[;/]/)[0]}"`,
        isCorrect: false
      },
      {
        id: `${word.id}_p_d2`,
        text: `${d2.pinyin} — "${(d2.meaning || d2.english).split(/[;/]/)[0]}"`,
        isCorrect: false
      }
    ];
    const rotatedPinyinOpts = pinyinOpts.map(
      (_, oIdx) => pinyinOpts[(oIdx + idx + 1) % pinyinOpts.length]
    );

    questions.push({
      id: `boss_q_pinyin_${word.id}_${idx}`,
      wordId: word.id,
      prompt: `Which Pinyin & meaning matches "${word.hanzi}" in "${word.exampleHanzi}"?`,
      focusHanzi: word.hanzi,
      focusPinyin: 'Listen & Identify Pinyin',
      speechText: word.exampleHanzi || word.hanzi,
      explanation: `${word.exampleHanzi} (${word.examplePinyin}) — "${word.exampleEnglish}"`,
      options: rotatedPinyinOpts
    });
  });

  return questions.slice(0, 6);
}

// ============================================================================
// IN-SESSION GAMIFIED TOP HUD (SEGMENTED PROGRESS BAR + LIVE COMBO + FLOATING XP)
// ============================================================================
function SessionGameTopHud({
  title,
  subtitle,
  totalSegments = 3,
  completedSegments = 0,
  combo = 0,
  xpPopupKey = 0,
  xpPopupAmount = 15,
  onClose,
  rightElement = null
}) {
  const segCount = Math.max(1, Math.min(24, totalSegments));

  return (
    <div className="space-y-2.5 border-b border-slate-800 pb-3 relative">
      <div className="flex items-center justify-between gap-2">
        <button
          type="button"
          onClick={onClose}
          className="game-btn-3d p-2 rounded-xl bg-slate-800 hover:bg-slate-700 text-slate-300 flex items-center gap-1 text-xs font-bold shrink-0"
        >
          <ArrowLeft className="w-4 h-4" />
          <span>Map</span>
        </button>

        <div className="text-center min-w-0 flex-1 px-1">
          <span className="text-[11px] font-black uppercase tracking-wider text-rose-400 block truncate">
            {title}
          </span>
          <span className="text-[10px] text-slate-400 block truncate">{subtitle}</span>
        </div>

        <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5 shrink-0">
          {combo >= 2 && (
            <div className="px-2.5 py-1 rounded-full bg-amber-500/20 border border-amber-400/50 text-amber-300 text-[11px] font-black flex items-center gap-1 animate-bounce">
              <Flame className="w-3.5 h-3.5 fill-amber-400 text-amber-400" />
              <span>{combo}x Combo!</span>
            </div>
          )}
          {rightElement}
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* Segmented Session Progress Bar */}
      <div className="grid gap-1" style={{ gridTemplateColumns: `repeat(${segCount}, minmax(0, 1fr))` }}>
        {Array.from({ length: segCount }).map((_, idx) => {
          const filled = idx < completedSegments;
          return (
            <div
              key={idx}
              className={`h-2 rounded-full transition-all duration-300 ${
                filled
                  ? 'bg-gradient-to-r from-rose-500 via-amber-400 to-emerald-400 shadow-sm shadow-amber-500/30'
                  : 'bg-slate-800'
              }`}
            />
          );
        })}
      </div>

      {/* Floating "+XP" Popup on Correct Answers */}
      {xpPopupKey > 0 && (
        <div
          key={xpPopupKey}
          className="animate-xp-float pointer-events-none absolute right-3 top-9 z-30 px-2.5 py-0.5 rounded-full bg-emerald-500 text-slate-950 font-black text-xs shadow-lg border border-emerald-200"
        >
          +{xpPopupAmount} XP
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}

// ============================================================================

// Apply #dc2626 tint to semantic radical outline strokes during Stage 1 & Stage 2
function highlightRadicalOutlineStrokes(containerEl, radStrokes = [], shouldHighlight = true) {
  if (!containerEl || !Array.isArray(radStrokes) || radStrokes.length === 0) return;
  try {
    const svgPaths = containerEl.querySelectorAll('svg path');
    // HanziWriter renders outline paths first in the SVG group
    radStrokes.forEach((strokeIdx) => {
      const targetPath = svgPaths[strokeIdx];
      if (targetPath) {
        targetPath.style.stroke = shouldHighlight ? '#dc2626' : '';
        targetPath.style.fill = shouldHighlight ? '#dc2626' : '';
        targetPath.style.opacity = shouldHighlight ? '0.68' : '';
      }
    });
  } catch (e) {
    // Ignore DOM query issues
  }
}

const WRITING_MASTERY_STAGES = [
  {
    stage: 1,
    shortLabel: '1. Guided',
    title: 'Stage 1 — Guided Stroke-by-Stroke',
    badgeClass: 'bg-sky-500/20 text-sky-300 border-sky-400/50',
    showOutline: true,
    showHintAfterMisses: 1,
    autoHighlightNextStroke: true,
    instruction: 'Follow the flashing stroke guide step-by-step (hint after 1 miss).'
  },
  {
    stage: 2,
    shortLabel: '2. Trace',
    title: 'Stage 2 — Ghost Outline Trace',
    badgeClass: 'bg-amber-500/20 text-amber-300 border-amber-400/50',
    showOutline: true,
    showHintAfterMisses: 2,
    autoHighlightNextStroke: false,
    instruction: 'Trace the full character guided only by the gray ghost silhouette (hint after 2 misses).'
  },
  {
    stage: 3,
    shortLabel: '3. Memory',
    title: 'Stage 3 — Blind Memory Freehand',
    badgeClass: 'bg-emerald-500/20 text-emerald-300 border-emerald-400/50',
    showOutline: false,
    showHintAfterMisses: 3,
    autoHighlightNextStroke: false,
    instruction: 'Blank 米字格 grid! Write from memory recall (hint after 3 consecutive misses).'
  }
];

function getWritingStageMeta(stageNum = 1) {
  return (
    WRITING_MASTERY_STAGES.find((s) => s.stage === stageNum) ||
    WRITING_MASTERY_STAGES[0]
  );
}

function loadHanziWriterScript() {
  if (typeof window !== 'undefined' && window.HanziWriter) {
    return Promise.resolve(window.HanziWriter);
  }
  if (hanziWriterLoadPromise) {
    return hanziWriterLoadPromise;
  }

  hanziWriterLoadPromise = new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
    const existingScript = document.querySelector(`script[src="${HANZI_WRITER_CDN}"]`);
    if (existingScript) {
      existingScript.addEventListener('load', () => resolve(window.HanziWriter));
      existingScript.addEventListener('error', (err) => reject(err));
      return;
    }

    const script = document.createElement('script');
    script.src = HANZI_WRITER_CDN;
    script.async = true;
    script.onload = () => {
      if (window.HanziWriter) {
        resolve(window.HanziWriter);
      } else {
        reject(new Error('HanziWriter loaded but global object was not found.'));
      }
    };
    script.onerror = () => {
      hanziWriterLoadPromise = null;
      reject(new Error('Failed to load hanzi-writer from CDN.'));
    };
    document.head.appendChild(script);
  });

  return hanziWriterLoadPromise;
}

// ============================================================================
// STAGE 2 MODAL: STROKE ORDER WRITING (HANZI-WRITER v3.5, MI ZI GE, 3-STAGE PROGRESSIVE MASTERY)
// ============================================================================
function WritingStageModal({
  words = UNIT_1_WORDS,
  isSlowMode,
  onSetSlowMode,
  speakMandarin,
  speakingState,
  onRecordSkillEvent,
  onClose,
  onComplete
}) {
  const [wordIndex, setWordIndex] = useState(0);
  const [charIndex, setCharIndex] = useState(0);
  const [charStage, setCharStage] = useState(1); // 1: Guided, 2: Trace, 3: Memory
  const [scriptStatus, setScriptStatus] = useState('loading'); // 'loading' | 'ready' | 'error'
  const [modeStatus, setModeStatus] = useState('quiz'); // 'animating' | 'quiz' | 'stage_transition' | 'completed'
  const [strokeStats, setStrokeStats] = useState({
    completedStrokes: 0,
    totalStrokes: 0,
    mistakesOnCurrent: 0,
    totalMistakes: 0,
    hintTriggered: false
  });
  const [completedCharsInWord, setCompletedCharsInWord] = useState([]);
  const [wordXpAwarded, setWordXpAwarded] = useState(false);

  // Gamified Combo & Feedback states
  const [combo, setCombo] = useState(0);
  const [maxCombo, setMaxCombo] = useState(0);
  const [bonusXp, setBonusXp] = useState(0);
  const [xpPopupKey, setXpPopupKey] = useState(0);
  const [xpPopupAmount, setXpPopupAmount] = useState(15);
  const [boardFeedback, setBoardFeedback] = useState('idle'); // 'idle' | 'correct' | 'wrong'
  const [charStrokeMeta, setCharStrokeMeta] = useState({
    strokeCount: null,
    radStrokes: [],
    loading: false
  });

  const writerTargetRef = useRef(null);
  const writerInstanceRef = useRef(null);
  const stageTransitionTimerRef = useRef(null);
  const feedbackTimerRef = useRef(null);

  const currentWord = words[wordIndex] || words[0];
  const currentChar = currentWord.chars[charIndex] || currentWord.chars[0];
  const stageMeta = getWritingStageMeta(charStage);
  const knownCharMeta = KNOWN_CHARACTER_INFO[currentChar] || null;

  const clearAllWritingTimers = useCallback(() => {
    if (stageTransitionTimerRef.current) {
      clearTimeout(stageTransitionTimerRef.current);
      stageTransitionTimerRef.current = null;
    }
    if (feedbackTimerRef.current) {
      clearTimeout(feedbackTimerRef.current);
      feedbackTimerRef.current = null;
    }
  }, []);

  // Total 3-stage character steps across all words in the session for segmented bar
  const totalStageSteps = useMemo(() => {
    return words.reduce((acc, w) => acc + (w.chars?.length || 1) * 3, 0);
  }, [words]);

  const completedStageSteps = useMemo(() => {
    let count = 0;
    for (let i = 0; i < wordIndex; i++) {
      count += (words[i]?.chars?.length || 1) * 3;
    }
    count += completedCharsInWord.length * 3;
    if (!completedCharsInWord.includes(charIndex)) {
      count += Math.max(0, charStage - 1);
    }
    return count;
  }, [words, wordIndex, completedCharsInWord, charIndex, charStage]);

  useEffect(() => {
    let isMounted = true;
    loadHanziWriterScript()
      .then(() => {
        if (isMounted) setScriptStatus('ready');
      })
      .catch(() => {
        if (isMounted) setScriptStatus('error');
      });
    return () => {
      isMounted = false;
      clearAllWritingTimers();
    };
  }, [clearAllWritingTimers]);

  useEffect(() => {
    speakMandarin(currentWord.hanzi);
  }, [wordIndex, currentWord.hanzi, speakMandarin]);

  // Preload character stroke count & radical stroke indices via HanziWriter.loadCharacterData(char)
  useEffect(() => {
    if (scriptStatus !== 'ready' || !window.HanziWriter?.loadCharacterData || !currentChar) {
      return;
    }
    let active = true;
    setCharStrokeMeta((prev) => ({ ...prev, loading: true }));
    window.HanziWriter.loadCharacterData(currentChar)
      .then((charData) => {
        if (!active || !charData) return;
        const radStrokes = Array.isArray(charData.radStrokes) ? charData.radStrokes : [];
        setCharStrokeMeta({
          strokeCount: Array.isArray(charData.strokes) ? charData.strokes.length : null,
          radStrokes,
          loading: false
        });
        if (writerTargetRef.current && (charStage === 1 || charStage === 2)) {
          highlightRadicalOutlineStrokes(writerTargetRef.current, radStrokes, true);
        }
      })
      .catch(() => {
        if (active) {
          setCharStrokeMeta({ strokeCount: null, radStrokes: [], loading: false });
        }
      });

    return () => {
      active = false;
    };
  }, [scriptStatus, currentChar, charStage]);

  const triggerBoardFeedback = useCallback((type) => {
    setBoardFeedback(type);
    setTimeout(() => setBoardFeedback('idle'), 500);
  }, []);

  const startInteractiveQuiz = useCallback(
    (writer, targetStage = charStage) => {
      const activeWriter = writer || writerInstanceRef.current;
      if (!activeWriter) return;

      clearAllWritingTimers();

      const activeStageMeta = getWritingStageMeta(targetStage);
      setModeStatus('quiz');
      setStrokeStats((prev) => ({
        ...prev,
        completedStrokes: 0,
        mistakesOnCurrent: 0,
        totalMistakes: 0,
        hintTriggered: false
      }));

      activeWriter.hideCharacter();
      if (activeStageMeta.showOutline) {
        activeWriter.showOutline();
      } else {
        activeWriter.hideOutline();
      }

      activeWriter.quiz({
        showHintAfterMisses: activeStageMeta.showHintAfterMisses,
        highlightOnComplete: true,
        onMistake: (strokeData) => {
          soundFX.playMistake();
          setCombo(0);
          triggerBoardFeedback('wrong');
          onRecordSkillEvent?.(currentWord, 'writing', { mistakesDelta: 1, completionsDelta: 0 });
          setStrokeStats((prev) => ({
            ...prev,
            mistakesOnCurrent: strokeData.mistakesOnStroke,
            totalMistakes: strokeData.totalMistakes,
            hintTriggered: strokeData.mistakesOnStroke >= activeStageMeta.showHintAfterMisses
          }));
        },
        onCorrectStroke: (strokeData) => {
          const doneCount = strokeData.strokeNum + 1;
          const total = doneCount + strokeData.strokesRemaining;
          setCombo((prev) => {
            const next = prev + 1;
            setMaxCombo((m) => Math.max(m, next));
            soundFX.playCorrect(next);
            return next;
          });
          setStrokeStats({
            completedStrokes: doneCount,
            totalStrokes: total,
            mistakesOnCurrent: 0,
            totalMistakes: strokeData.totalMistakes,
            hintTriggered: false
          });

          // Stage 1 (Guided): Automatically highlight/flash the upcoming stroke before user draws it
          if (activeStageMeta.autoHighlightNextStroke && strokeData.strokesRemaining > 0) {
            try {
              activeWriter.highlightStroke(strokeData.strokeNum + 1);
            } catch (e) {
              // Ignore if writer was unmounted
            }
          }
        },
        onComplete: (summaryData) => {
          triggerBoardFeedback('correct');
          setStrokeStats((prev) => ({
            ...prev,
            completedStrokes: prev.totalStrokes || prev.completedStrokes,
            mistakesOnCurrent: 0,
            totalMistakes: summaryData.totalMistakes,
            hintTriggered: false
          }));

          // Sequential 3-Stage Progression per Character: 1. Guided -> 2. Trace -> 3. Memory
          if (targetStage < 3) {
            soundFX.playCorrect(combo + 1);
            setModeStatus('stage_transition');
            stageTransitionTimerRef.current = setTimeout(() => {
              setCharStage(targetStage + 1);
            }, 620);
          } else {
            // Completed Stage 3 (Memory Freehand) for this character!
            setModeStatus('completed');
            soundFX.playCorrect(combo + 2);

            const earned = summaryData.totalMistakes === 0 ? 30 : 20;
            const isFinalCharInWord = charIndex >= (currentWord.chars?.length || 1) - 1;

            setCompletedCharsInWord((prev) => {
              const nextCompleted = prev.includes(charIndex) ? prev : [...prev, charIndex];
              const allCharsDone = nextCompleted.length >= (currentWord.chars?.length || 1);

              // Award Mastery XP only after completing Stage 3 for all characters in the word
              if (allCharsDone && !wordXpAwarded) {
                setWordXpAwarded(true);
                onRecordSkillEvent?.(currentWord, 'writing', {
                  mistakesDelta: 0,
                  completionsDelta: 1
                });
                setBonusXp((prevXp) => prevXp + earned);
                setXpPopupAmount(earned);
                setXpPopupKey((prevKey) => prevKey + 1);
              }
              return nextCompleted;
            });

            // Auto-transition after Stage 3 completion (850ms to next char in word, 1200ms to next word)
            if (!isFinalCharInWord) {
              feedbackTimerRef.current = setTimeout(() => {
                setCharStage(1);
                setCharIndex((prev) => prev + 1);
              }, 850);
            } else {
              const nextTotalXp = bonusXp + (wordXpAwarded ? 0 : earned);
              feedbackTimerRef.current = setTimeout(() => {
                if (wordIndex < words.length - 1) {
                  setCompletedCharsInWord([]);
                  setWordXpAwarded(false);
                  setCharStage(1);
                  setCharIndex(0);
                  setWordIndex((prev) => prev + 1);
                } else {
                  onComplete(nextTotalXp, maxCombo);
                }
              }, 1200);
            }
          }
        }
      });

      // Stage 1 (Guided): Trigger highlightStroke(0) at the start of the quiz
      if (activeStageMeta.autoHighlightNextStroke) {
        setTimeout(() => {
          if (writerInstanceRef.current === activeWriter) {
            try {
              activeWriter.highlightStroke(0);
            } catch (e) {
              // Ignore
            }
          }
        }, 60);
      }
    },
    [
      bonusXp,
      charIndex,
      charStage,
      clearAllWritingTimers,
      combo,
      currentWord,
      maxCombo,
      onComplete,
      onRecordSkillEvent,
      triggerBoardFeedback,
      wordIndex,
      wordXpAwarded,
      words.length
    ]
  );

  useEffect(() => {
    if (scriptStatus !== 'ready' || !writerTargetRef.current || !window.HanziWriter) return;

    const containerEl = writerTargetRef.current;
    if (writerInstanceRef.current?.cancelQuiz) {
      try {
        writerInstanceRef.current.cancelQuiz();
      } catch (e) {
        // Ignore
      }
    }
    // Explicitly clear container before every HanziWriter.create() call so SVG grids never duplicate
    containerEl.innerHTML = '';
    writerInstanceRef.current = null;

    const currentStageConfig = getWritingStageMeta(charStage);

    const writer = window.HanziWriter.create(containerEl, currentChar, {
      width: 240,
      height: 240,
      padding: 18,
      showOutline: currentStageConfig.showOutline,
      showCharacter: false,
      strokeAnimationSpeed: 1.1,
      delayBetweenStrokes: 160,
      strokeColor: '#f8fafc',
      radicalColor: '#dc2626',
      outlineColor: '#334155',
      drawingColor: '#f43f5e',
      drawingWidth: 22,
      highlightColor: '#38bdf8',
      showHintAfterMisses: currentStageConfig.showHintAfterMisses,
      highlightOnComplete: true,
      onLoadCharDataSuccess: (charData) => {
        const radStrokes = Array.isArray(charData.radStrokes) ? charData.radStrokes : [];
        setCharStrokeMeta({
          strokeCount: charData.strokes.length,
          radStrokes,
          loading: false
        });
        setStrokeStats({
          completedStrokes: 0,
          totalStrokes: charData.strokes.length,
          mistakesOnCurrent: 0,
          totalMistakes: 0,
          hintTriggered: false
        });
        if (writerInstanceRef.current === writer) {
          startInteractiveQuiz(writer, charStage);
          if (charStage === 1 || charStage === 2) {
            setTimeout(() => {
              highlightRadicalOutlineStrokes(containerEl, radStrokes, true);
            }, 40);
          }
        }
      }
    });

    writerInstanceRef.current = writer;

    return () => {
      if (stageTransitionTimerRef.current) {
        clearTimeout(stageTransitionTimerRef.current);
      }
      if (writerInstanceRef.current?.cancelQuiz) {
        try {
          writerInstanceRef.current.cancelQuiz();
        } catch (e) {
          // Ignore
        }
      }
      if (containerEl) {
        containerEl.innerHTML = '';
      }
      if (writerTargetRef.current) {
        writerTargetRef.current.innerHTML = '';
      }
      writerInstanceRef.current = null;
    };
  }, [scriptStatus, wordIndex, charIndex, charStage, currentChar, startInteractiveQuiz]);

  const handleReplayAnimation = () => {
    const writer = writerInstanceRef.current;
    if (!writer) return;
    soundFX.playPop();

    if (stageTransitionTimerRef.current) {
      clearTimeout(stageTransitionTimerRef.current);
      stageTransitionTimerRef.current = null;
    }

    setModeStatus('animating');
    writer.cancelQuiz();
    writer.showOutline();
    writer.showCharacter();
    writer.animateCharacter({
      onComplete: () => {
        setTimeout(() => {
          if (writerInstanceRef.current === writer) {
            writer.hideCharacter();
            startInteractiveQuiz(writer, charStage);
          }
        }, 450);
      }
    });
  };

  const handleAdvanceStep = () => {
    soundFX.playPop();
    if (charIndex < currentWord.chars.length - 1) {
      setCharStage(1);
      setCharIndex((prev) => prev + 1);
    } else if (wordIndex < words.length - 1) {
      setCompletedCharsInWord([]);
      setWordXpAwarded(false);
      setCharStage(1);
      setCharIndex(0);
      setWordIndex((prev) => prev + 1);
    } else {
      onComplete(bonusXp, maxCombo);
    }
  };

  const handleSelectCharTab = (idx) => {
    if (idx === charIndex) return;
    // Enforce completing all 3 stages on earlier characters before jumping ahead
    const isUnlockedChar = idx === 0 || completedCharsInWord.includes(idx - 1) || completedCharsInWord.includes(idx);
    if (!isUnlockedChar) {
      soundFX.playMistake();
      return;
    }
    soundFX.playPop();
    setCharStage(completedCharsInWord.includes(idx) ? 3 : 1);
    setCharIndex(idx);
  };

  const isCharComplete =
    (charStage === 3 && modeStatus === 'completed') ||
    completedCharsInWord.includes(charIndex);
  const isLastCharOfWord = charIndex === currentWord.chars.length - 1;
  const isLastWord = wordIndex === words.length - 1;

  return (
    <div className="fixed inset-0 z-50 bg-slate-950/95 backdrop-blur-md flex justify-center">
      <div className="w-full max-w-md bg-slate-900 min-h-screen flex flex-col justify-between p-4 overflow-y-auto">
        <div>
          <SessionGameTopHud
            title="Stage 2 • Progressive Calligraphy"
            subtitle={`Word ${wordIndex + 1}/${words.length} • Char ${charIndex + 1}/${currentWord.chars.length} • ${stageMeta.shortLabel}`}
            totalSegments={totalStageSteps}
            completedSegments={completedStageSteps}
            combo={combo}
            xpPopupKey={xpPopupKey}
            xpPopupAmount={xpPopupAmount}
            onClose={onClose}
            rightElement={
              <MandarinAudioControls
                text={currentWord.hanzi}
                isSlowMode={isSlowMode}
                onSetSlowMode={onSetSlowMode}
                speakMandarin={speakMandarin}
                speakingState={speakingState}
                size="sm"
              />
            }
          />

          <div className="mt-3 bg-slate-800/80 border border-slate-700/70 rounded-2xl p-3 flex items-center justify-between gap-2">
            <div className="min-w-0">
              <span className="text-[10px] font-bold uppercase text-amber-400 block truncate">
                {currentWord.meaning || currentWord.english}
              </span>
              <div className="flex items-baseline gap-2 mt-0.5">
                <h3 className="text-xl font-black text-white">{currentWord.hanzi}</h3>
                <span className="text-xs font-bold text-rose-400">{currentWord.pinyin}</span>
              </div>
              <p className="text-[11px] text-slate-400 mt-0.5 truncate">
                Complete all 3 stages per character to earn word mastery XP
              </p>
            </div>

            <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5 shrink-0">
              {currentWord.chars.map((ch, idx) => {
                const isDone = completedCharsInWord.includes(idx);
                const isActive = idx === charIndex;
                const isLocked = idx > 0 && !completedCharsInWord.includes(idx - 1) && !isDone && !isActive;
                return (
                  <button
                    key={`${currentWord.id}-char-${idx}`}
                    type="button"
                    onClick={() => handleSelectCharTab(idx)}
                    className={`game-btn-3d w-11 h-11 rounded-xl font-black text-lg flex flex-col items-center justify-center border transition-all relative ${
                      isActive
                        ? 'bg-amber-500/20 border-amber-400 text-white shadow-md shadow-amber-950/50'
                        : isDone
                        ? 'bg-emerald-500/20 border-emerald-400/50 text-emerald-300'
                        : isLocked
                        ? 'bg-slate-950 border-slate-800 text-slate-600 opacity-60'
                        : 'bg-slate-900 border-slate-700 text-slate-400'
                    }`}
                  >
                    <span>{ch}</span>
                    {isDone && (
                      <CheckCircle2 className="w-3 h-3 text-emerald-400 absolute -top-1 -right-1 bg-slate-900 rounded-full" />
                    )}
                    {isLocked && (
                      <Lock className="w-2.5 h-2.5 text-slate-500 absolute -top-1 -right-1 bg-slate-900 rounded-full" />
                    )}
                  </button>
                );
              })}
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>

        <div className="my-2.5 flex flex-col items-center">
          {/* 3-Step Progressive Mastery Indicator Pill: 1. Guided -> 2. Trace -> 3. Memory */}
          <div className="w-full max-w-[244px] mb-2.5">
            <div className="grid grid-cols-3 gap-1 p-1 rounded-2xl bg-slate-950 border border-slate-800">
              {WRITING_MASTERY_STAGES.map((st) => {
                const isCompletedStage =
                  isCharComplete || charStage > st.stage;
                const isCurrentStage = charStage === st.stage && !isCharComplete;
                return (
                  <button
                    key={st.stage}
                    type="button"
                    onClick={() => {
                      if (st.stage <= charStage || isCharComplete) {
                        soundFX.playPop();
                        setCharStage(st.stage);
                      }
                    }}
                    className={`py-1.5 px-2 rounded-xl text-[11px] font-black flex items-center justify-center gap-1 transition-all border ${
                      isCurrentStage
                        ? 'bg-gradient-to-r from-amber-500 to-rose-500 text-slate-950 border-amber-300 shadow'
                        : isCompletedStage
                        ? 'bg-emerald-500/20 border-emerald-500/40 text-emerald-300'
                        : 'bg-slate-900/70 border-transparent text-slate-500'
                    }`}
                  >
                    {isCompletedStage && <Check className="w-3 h-3 stroke-[3px] shrink-0" />}
                    <span className="truncate">{st.shortLabel}</span>
                  </button>
                );
              })}
            </div>
          </div>

          {/* Action Controls Row: Replay Animation & Reset Stage */}
          <div className="w-full max-w-[244px] flex items-center justify-between mb-2">
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={handleReplayAnimation}
              disabled={scriptStatus !== 'ready'}
              className="game-btn-3d px-3 py-1.5 rounded-xl bg-amber-500/15 hover:bg-amber-500/25 border border-amber-400/40 text-amber-300 text-xs font-bold flex items-center gap-1.5 transition-all disabled:opacity-40"
            >
              <Eye className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
              <span>Replay Animation</span>
            </button>

            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={() => {
                soundFX.playPop();
                startInteractiveQuiz(writerInstanceRef.current, charStage);
              }}
              disabled={scriptStatus !== 'ready'}
              className="game-btn-3d px-3 py-1.5 rounded-xl bg-slate-800 hover:bg-slate-700 border border-slate-700 text-slate-300 text-xs font-bold flex items-center gap-1.5 transition-all disabled:opacity-40"
            >
              <RotateCcw className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
              <span>Reset Stage {charStage}</span>
            </button>
          </div>

          {/* Stroke Count & Radical Info Strip (via HanziWriter.loadCharacterData) */}
          <div className="w-full max-w-[244px] mb-2 px-3 py-1.5 rounded-xl bg-slate-950/90 border border-slate-800 flex items-center justify-between gap-2 text-[11px]">
            <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5 font-extrabold text-slate-200">
              <PenTool className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-amber-400 shrink-0" />
              <span>
                "{currentChar}" •{' '}
                {charStrokeMeta.strokeCount || strokeStats.totalStrokes || '?'}{' '}
                strokes
              </span>
            </div>
            <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5 truncate">
              <span
                className="w-2.5 h-2.5 rounded-full shrink-0 border border-red-300/60"
                style={{ backgroundColor: '#dc2626' }}
              />
              <span className="text-[10px] font-bold text-red-300 truncate">
                {knownCharMeta?.radical
                  ? `Radical: ${knownCharMeta.radical}`
                  : charStrokeMeta.radStrokes?.length > 0
                  ? `Radical (${charStrokeMeta.radStrokes.length} strokes)`
                  : 'Radical in Red (#dc2626)'}
              </span>
            </div>
          </div>

          <div
            style={{ touchAction: 'none' }}
            className={`relative w-[244px] h-[244px] rounded-2xl bg-slate-950 border-2 shadow-2xl overflow-hidden select-none touch-none flex items-center justify-center transition-colors ${
              boardFeedback === 'wrong'
                ? 'border-rose-500 bg-rose-950/20 animate-game-shake'
                : boardFeedback === 'correct'
                ? 'border-emerald-400 animate-game-green-pulse'
                : charStage === 3
                ? 'border-emerald-500/50'
                : charStage === 2
                ? 'border-amber-500/50'
                : 'border-sky-500/50'
            }`}
          >
            <svg
              viewBox="0 0 240 240"
              className="absolute inset-0 w-full h-full pointer-events-none opacity-30"
            >
              <rect
                x="6"
                y="6"
                width="228"
                height="228"
                fill="none"
                stroke="#f59e0b"
                strokeWidth="1.5"
              />
              <line
                x1="6"
                y1="120"
                x2="234"
                y2="120"
                stroke="#f59e0b"
                strokeWidth="1"
                strokeDasharray="6 5"
              />
              <line
                x1="120"
                y1="6"
                x2="120"
                y2="234"
                stroke="#f59e0b"
                strokeWidth="1"
                strokeDasharray="6 5"
              />
              <line
                x1="6"
                y1="6"
                x2="234"
                y2="234"
                stroke="#f59e0b"
                strokeWidth="1"
                strokeDasharray="6 6"
              />
              <line
                x1="6"
                y1="234"
                x2="234"
                y2="6"
                stroke="#f59e0b"
                strokeWidth="1"
                strokeDasharray="6 6"
              />
            </svg>

            {scriptStatus === 'loading' && (
              <div className="z-20 text-center p-4 space-y-2">
                <RefreshCw className="w-6 h-6 text-amber-400 animate-spin mx-auto" />
                <p className="text-xs font-bold text-slate-300">Loading Hanzi Writer 3.5...</p>
              </div>
            )}

            {scriptStatus === 'error' && (
              <div className="z-20 text-center p-4 space-y-2">
                <AlertCircle className="w-6 h-6 text-rose-400 mx-auto" />
                <p className="text-xs font-bold text-rose-300">
                  Could not reach Hanzi Writer CDN.
                </p>
                <button
                  type="button"
                  onClick={() => {
                    if (charStage < 3) {
                      setCharStage((s) => s + 1);
                    } else {
                      setModeStatus('completed');
                      setCompletedCharsInWord((prev) =>
                        prev.includes(charIndex) ? prev : [...prev, charIndex]
                      );
                      feedbackTimerRef.current = setTimeout(() => {
                        if (charIndex < currentWord.chars.length - 1) {
                          setCharStage(1);
                          setCharIndex((prev) => prev + 1);
                        } else if (wordIndex < words.length - 1) {
                          setCompletedCharsInWord([]);
                          setWordXpAwarded(false);
                          setCharStage(1);
                          setCharIndex(0);
                          setWordIndex((prev) => prev + 1);
                        } else {
                          onComplete(bonusXp, maxCombo);
                        }
                      }, 1200);
                    }
                  }}
                  className="px-3 py-1.5 rounded-lg bg-amber-500 text-slate-950 font-bold text-xs"
                >
                  Mark "{currentChar}" Stage {charStage} Complete
                </button>
              </div>
            )}

            {/* Completed! Checkmark Overlay during 1200ms Auto-Transition */}
            {isCharComplete && (
              <div className="absolute inset-0 z-20 bg-slate-950/80 backdrop-blur-[2px] flex flex-col items-center justify-center gap-2 animate-game-green-pulse pointer-events-none">
                <div className="w-14 h-14 rounded-full bg-emerald-500/25 border-2 border-emerald-400 flex items-center justify-center shadow-lg">
                  <CheckCircle2 className="w-8 h-8 text-emerald-400" />
                </div>
                <span className="text-sm font-black text-emerald-300 tracking-wide">
                  Completed! ✓
                </span>
                <span className="text-[11px] font-bold text-slate-300">
                  {!isLastCharOfWord
                    ? `Advancing to "${currentWord.chars[charIndex + 1]}"...`
                    : !isLastWord
                    ? `Advancing to ${words[wordIndex + 1].hanzi}...`
                    : 'Finishing Stage...'}
                </span>
              </div>
            )}

            <div
              ref={writerTargetRef}
              style={{ touchAction: 'none' }}
              className="relative z-10 w-[240px] h-[240px] touch-none select-none cursor-crosshair"
            />
          </div>

          <div className="w-full max-w-[244px] mt-2.5 rounded-2xl bg-slate-800/80 border border-slate-700/80 p-3 space-y-1.5">
            <div className="flex items-center justify-between text-xs">
              <span className="font-bold text-slate-200 truncate">
                {stageMeta.title}
              </span>
              <span className="font-extrabold text-amber-400 shrink-0">
                {strokeStats.completedStrokes} / {strokeStats.totalStrokes || '?'}
              </span>
            </div>

            <div className="w-full h-2 bg-slate-900 rounded-full overflow-hidden">
              <div
                className="h-full bg-gradient-to-r from-sky-400 via-amber-500 to-emerald-400 transition-all duration-300"
                style={{
                  width: `${
                    strokeStats.totalStrokes > 0
                      ? Math.round((strokeStats.completedStrokes / strokeStats.totalStrokes) * 100)
                      : 0
                  }%`
                }}
              />
            </div>

            <div className="text-[11px] pt-0.5">
              {modeStatus === 'animating' ? (
                <span className="text-amber-300 font-bold">
                  Replaying stroke order animation for "{currentChar}"...
                </span>
              ) : modeStatus === 'stage_transition' ? (
                <span className="text-emerald-400 font-bold flex items-center gap-1">
                  <Sparkles className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
                  <span>
                    {stageMeta.shortLabel} cleared! Advancing to Stage {charStage + 1}...
                  </span>
                </span>
              ) : isCharComplete ? (
                <span className="text-emerald-400 font-bold flex items-center gap-1">
                  <CheckCircle2 className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
                  <span>
                    Completed! Auto-advancing...
                  </span>
                </span>
              ) : strokeStats.hintTriggered ? (
                <span className="text-rose-300 font-bold">
                  {stageMeta.showHintAfterMisses} miss
                  {stageMeta.showHintAfterMisses > 1 ? 'es' : ''} — follow the flashing stroke hint!
                </span>
              ) : strokeStats.mistakesOnCurrent > 0 ? (
                <span className="text-amber-300 font-semibold">
                  Try again ({strokeStats.mistakesOnCurrent}/{stageMeta.showHintAfterMisses} misses before hint)
                </span>
              ) : (
                <span className="text-slate-400">{stageMeta.instruction}</span>
              )}
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>

        <div className="pt-2 text-center text-[11px] font-bold text-slate-400">
          {isCharComplete
            ? 'Completed! Advancing automatically...'
            : `Draw strokes in order to complete ${stageMeta.shortLabel}`}
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

// ============================================================================
// STAGE 3 MODAL: LISTENING (DICTATION) & SPEAKING (PRONUNCIATION) WITH COMBO & SFX
// ============================================================================
function AudioSpeechStageModal({
  words = UNIT_1_WORDS,
  initialFocusMode = 'combined',
  isSlowMode,
  onSetSlowMode,
  speakMandarin,
  speakingState,
  onRecordSkillEvent,
  onClose,
  onComplete
}) {
  const [stepIndex, setStepIndex] = useState(0);
  const [dictationSubMode, setDictationSubMode] = useState('pinyin'); // 'pinyin' | 'hanzi'
  const [dictationInput, setDictationInput] = useState('');
  const [dictationResult, setDictationResult] = useState(null);
  const [dictationSubmitted, setDictationSubmitted] = useState(false);
  const [dictationAnim, setDictationAnim] = useState('idle'); // 'idle' | 'correct' | 'wrong'

  const [isRecording, setIsRecording] = useState(false);
  const [recognizedText, setRecognizedText] = useState('');
  const [similarityData, setSimilarityData] = useState(null);
  const [speakingEvaluated, setSpeakingEvaluated] = useState(false);
  const [speakingSkipped, setSpeakingSkipped] = useState(false);
  const [micErrorMsg, setMicErrorMsg] = useState('');

  // Gamified Combo & Floating XP states
  const [combo, setCombo] = useState(0);
  const [maxCombo, setMaxCombo] = useState(0);
  const [bonusXp, setBonusXp] = useState(0);
  const [xpPopupKey, setXpPopupKey] = useState(0);
  const [xpPopupAmount, setXpPopupAmount] = useState(15);

  const recognitionRef = useRef(null);
  const feedbackTimerRef = useRef(null);
  const currentWord = words[stepIndex] || words[0];

  const showDictation = initialFocusMode === 'combined' || initialFocusMode === 'dictation';
  const showSpeaking = initialFocusMode === 'combined' || initialFocusMode === 'speaking';

  const clearFeedbackTimer = useCallback(() => {
    if (feedbackTimerRef.current) {
      clearTimeout(feedbackTimerRef.current);
      feedbackTimerRef.current = null;
    }
  }, []);

  useEffect(() => {
    clearFeedbackTimer();
    setDictationInput('');
    setDictationResult(null);
    setDictationSubmitted(false);
    setDictationAnim('idle');
    setIsRecording(false);
    setRecognizedText('');
    setSimilarityData(null);
    setSpeakingEvaluated(false);
    setSpeakingSkipped(false);
    setMicErrorMsg('');
    speakMandarin(currentWord.hanzi);

    return () => {
      clearFeedbackTimer();
      if (recognitionRef.current) {
        try {
          recognitionRef.current.abort();
        } catch (err) {
          // Ignore
        }
      }
    };
  }, [stepIndex, currentWord.hanzi, speakMandarin, clearFeedbackTimer]);

  const scheduleAutoAdvanceIfReady = useCallback(
    (wasCorrectAnswer, nextDictationDone, nextSpeakingDone, updatedBonusXp, updatedMaxCombo) => {
      if (!nextDictationDone || !nextSpeakingDone) return;
      clearFeedbackTimer();
      const delayMs = wasCorrectAnswer ? 1400 : 1800;
      feedbackTimerRef.current = setTimeout(() => {
        if (stepIndex < words.length - 1) {
          setStepIndex((prev) => prev + 1);
        } else {
          onComplete(updatedBonusXp, updatedMaxCombo);
        }
      }, delayMs);
    },
    [clearFeedbackTimer, onComplete, stepIndex, words.length]
  );

  const handleCheckDictation = (e) => {
    if (e) e.preventDefault();
    if (dictationSubmitted || !dictationInput.trim()) return;

    const res = validateDictationInput(dictationInput, currentWord, dictationSubMode);
    setDictationResult(res);
    setDictationSubmitted(true);

    let nextBonus = bonusXp;
    let nextMax = maxCombo;

    if (res.isValid) {
      setDictationAnim('correct');
      setTimeout(() => setDictationAnim('idle'), 550);
      onRecordSkillEvent?.(currentWord, 'listening', { mistakesDelta: 0, completionsDelta: 1 });
      const nextCombo = combo + 1;
      nextMax = Math.max(maxCombo, nextCombo);
      setCombo(nextCombo);
      setMaxCombo(nextMax);
      soundFX.playCorrect(nextCombo);
      const gain = 15 + (nextCombo >= 2 ? nextCombo * 3 : 0);
      nextBonus = bonusXp + gain;
      setBonusXp(nextBonus);
      setXpPopupAmount(gain);
      setXpPopupKey((k) => k + 1);
      speakMandarin(currentWord.hanzi);
    } else {
      soundFX.playMistake();
      setCombo(0);
      onRecordSkillEvent?.(currentWord, 'listening', { mistakesDelta: 1, completionsDelta: 0 });
      setDictationAnim('wrong');
      setTimeout(() => setDictationAnim('idle'), 450);
    }

    const speakingAlreadyDone = !showSpeaking || speakingSkipped || speakingEvaluated;
    scheduleAutoAdvanceIfReady(res.isValid, true, speakingAlreadyDone, nextBonus, nextMax);
  };

  const handleStartSpeechRecognition = () => {
    if (speakingEvaluated || speakingSkipped) return;
    setMicErrorMsg('');
    const SpeechRec =
      typeof window !== 'undefined' &&
      (window.SpeechRecognition || window.webkitSpeechRecognition);

    if (!SpeechRec) {
      setMicErrorMsg(
        'SpeechRecognition API is not supported in this browser. Click "Skip / Can\'t speak right now" below to continue.'
      );
      return;
    }

    try {
      const recognition = new SpeechRec();
      recognitionRef.current = recognition;
      recognition.lang = 'zh-CN';
      recognition.continuous = false;
      recognition.interimResults = false;
      recognition.maxAlternatives = 3;

      setIsRecording(true);

      recognition.onresult = (event) => {
        setIsRecording(false);
        const bestTranscript = event.results?.[0]?.[0]?.transcript || '';
        setRecognizedText(bestTranscript);
        const computed = computeSpeechSimilarity(bestTranscript, currentWord);
        setSimilarityData(computed);
        setSpeakingEvaluated(true);

        const isPass = computed.score >= 50;
        let nextBonus = bonusXp;
        let nextMax = maxCombo;

        if (isPass) {
          onRecordSkillEvent?.(currentWord, 'speaking', { mistakesDelta: 0, completionsDelta: 1 });
          const nextCombo = combo + 1;
          nextMax = Math.max(maxCombo, nextCombo);
          setCombo(nextCombo);
          setMaxCombo(nextMax);
          soundFX.playCorrect(nextCombo);
          const gain = (computed.score >= 85 ? 20 : 15) + (nextCombo >= 2 ? nextCombo * 3 : 0);
          nextBonus = bonusXp + gain;
          setBonusXp(nextBonus);
          setXpPopupAmount(gain);
          setXpPopupKey((k) => k + 1);
        } else {
          soundFX.playMistake();
          setCombo(0);
          onRecordSkillEvent?.(currentWord, 'speaking', { mistakesDelta: 1, completionsDelta: 0 });
        }

        const dictationAlreadyDone = !showDictation || dictationSubmitted;
        scheduleAutoAdvanceIfReady(isPass, dictationAlreadyDone, true, nextBonus, nextMax);
      };

      recognition.onerror = (event) => {
        setIsRecording(false);
        const errCode = event?.error || 'unknown';
        setMicErrorMsg(
          `Microphone access unavailable (${errCode}). Use "Skip / Can't speak right now" below to continue.`
        );
      };

      recognition.onend = () => {
        setIsRecording(false);
      };

      recognition.start();
    } catch (err) {
      setIsRecording(false);
      setMicErrorMsg(
        'Microphone blocked by browser/iframe permissions. Use "Skip / Can\'t speak right now" to proceed.'
      );
    }
  };

  const handleSkipSpeaking = () => {
    if (speakingEvaluated || speakingSkipped) return;
    soundFX.playPop();
    if (recognitionRef.current && isRecording) {
      try {
        recognitionRef.current.abort();
      } catch (e) {
        // Ignore
      }
    }
    setIsRecording(false);
    setSpeakingSkipped(true);
    setSpeakingEvaluated(true);

    const dictationAlreadyDone = !showDictation || dictationSubmitted;
    scheduleAutoAdvanceIfReady(true, dictationAlreadyDone, true, bonusXp, maxCombo);
  };

  const isDictationDone = !showDictation || dictationSubmitted;
  const isSpeakingDone = !showSpeaking || speakingSkipped || speakingEvaluated;
  const canAdvance = isDictationDone && isSpeakingDone;

  return (
    <div className="fixed inset-0 z-50 bg-slate-950/95 backdrop-blur-md flex justify-center">
      <div className="w-full max-w-md bg-slate-900 min-h-screen flex flex-col justify-between p-4 overflow-y-auto">
        <SessionGameTopHud
          title={
            initialFocusMode === 'dictation'
              ? 'Listening (Dictation)'
              : initialFocusMode === 'speaking'
              ? 'Speaking (Pronunciation)'
              : 'Stage 3 • Listening & Speaking'
          }
          subtitle={`Word ${stepIndex + 1} of ${words.length} • Native zh-CN`}
          totalSegments={words.length}
          completedSegments={stepIndex + (canAdvance ? 1 : 0)}
          combo={combo}
          xpPopupKey={xpPopupKey}
          xpPopupAmount={xpPopupAmount}
          onClose={() => {
            clearFeedbackTimer();
            onClose();
          }}
        />

        <div className="my-auto space-y-4 py-3">
          {showDictation && (
            <div
              className={`rounded-3xl bg-slate-800/90 border p-4 space-y-3.5 shadow-xl transition-all ${
                dictationAnim === 'wrong'
                  ? 'border-rose-500 bg-rose-950/20 animate-game-shake'
                  : dictationAnim === 'correct'
                  ? 'border-emerald-400 animate-game-green-pulse'
                  : 'border-slate-700'
              }`}
            >
              <div className="flex items-center justify-between gap-2">
                <span className="text-[10px] font-extrabold uppercase tracking-wider px-2.5 py-1 rounded-full bg-emerald-500/15 text-emerald-300 border border-emerald-500/30 flex items-center gap-1 shrink-0">
                  <Headphones className="w-3 h-3" />
                  <span>Listening (Dictation)</span>
                </span>
                <span className="text-xs font-semibold text-slate-400 truncate">
                  Meaning:{' '}
                  <strong className="text-white">
                    {(currentWord.meaning || currentWord.english).split(' / ')[0]}
                  </strong>
                </span>
              </div>

              <div className="rounded-2xl bg-slate-950/70 border border-slate-800 p-3.5 flex items-center justify-between gap-2">
                <div>
                  <p className="text-xs font-bold text-white">Listen to the Mandarin word</p>
                  <p className="text-[11px] text-slate-400">
                    {dictationSubmitted
                      ? `${currentWord.hanzi} (${currentWord.pinyin})`
                      : 'Tap 1.0x Normal or 0.5x Slow to hear pronunciation'}
                  </p>
                </div>
                <MandarinAudioControls
                  text={currentWord.hanzi}
                  isSlowMode={isSlowMode}
                  onSetSlowMode={onSetSlowMode}
                  speakMandarin={speakMandarin}
                  speakingState={speakingState}
                />
              </div>

              <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-1.5 p-1 rounded-xl bg-slate-950 border border-slate-800">
                <button
                  type="button"
                  disabled={dictationSubmitted}
                  onClick={() => {
                    soundFX.playPop();
                    setDictationSubMode('pinyin');
                    setDictationResult(null);
                  }}
                  className={`game-btn-3d py-2 rounded-lg text-xs font-extrabold transition-all flex items-center justify-center gap-1.5 ${
                    dictationSubMode === 'pinyin'
                      ? 'bg-emerald-600 text-white shadow'
                      : 'text-slate-400 hover:text-slate-200'
                  }`}
                >
                  <Keyboard className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
                  <span>Type Pinyin</span>
                </button>
                <button
                  type="button"
                  disabled={dictationSubmitted}
                  onClick={() => {
                    soundFX.playPop();
                    setDictationSubMode('hanzi');
                    setDictationResult(null);
                  }}
                  className={`game-btn-3d py-2 rounded-lg text-xs font-extrabold transition-all flex items-center justify-center gap-1.5 ${
                    dictationSubMode === 'hanzi'
                      ? 'bg-emerald-600 text-white shadow'
                      : 'text-slate-400 hover:text-slate-200'
                  }`}
                >
                  <span>字</span>
                  <span>Type Hanzi</span>
                </button>
              </div>

              <form onSubmit={handleCheckDictation} className="space-y-2">
                <div className="flex gap-2">
                  <input
                    type="text"
                    disabled={dictationSubmitted}
                    value={dictationInput}
                    onChange={(e) => setDictationInput(e.target.value)}
                    placeholder={
                      dictationSubMode === 'pinyin'
                        ? 'e.g. nǐ hǎo, ni3 hao3, or nihao'
                        : 'Type Chinese characters (e.g. 你好)'
                    }
                    className={`flex-1 bg-slate-950 border rounded-xl px-3.5 py-2.5 text-xs font-bold text-white placeholder:text-slate-500 outline-none ${
                      dictationSubmitted
                        ? dictationResult?.isValid
                          ? 'border-emerald-400 bg-emerald-950/30 text-emerald-200'
                          : 'border-rose-500 bg-rose-950/30 text-rose-200'
                        : 'border-slate-700 focus:border-emerald-400'
                    }`}
                  />
                  <button
                    type="submit"
                    disabled={dictationSubmitted || !dictationInput.trim()}
                    className="game-btn-3d px-4 py-2.5 rounded-xl bg-emerald-600 hover:bg-emerald-500 disabled:bg-slate-800 disabled:text-slate-500 text-white font-extrabold text-xs shrink-0 border-b-2 border-emerald-800 disabled:border-slate-900"
                  >
                    Submit
                  </button>
                </div>

                <p className="text-[11px] text-slate-400">
                  {dictationSubMode === 'pinyin'
                    ? 'Accepts tone marks, tone numbers, or toneless Pinyin (v → ü).'
                    : `Hint: ${currentWord.literal}`}
                </p>
              </form>

              {dictationResult && (
                <div
                  className={`rounded-xl p-3 border text-xs space-y-2.5 ${
                    dictationResult.isValid
                      ? 'bg-emerald-500/15 border-emerald-400/50 text-emerald-200'
                      : 'bg-rose-500/15 border-rose-400/50 text-rose-200'
                  }`}
                >
                  <div>
                    <p className="font-extrabold">
                      {dictationResult.isValid
                        ? `✓ Correct! (${dictationResult.matchType})`
                        : `✗ Incorrect — Correct answer: ${currentWord.hanzi} (${currentWord.pinyin})`}
                    </p>
                    <p className="text-[11px] mt-0.5 opacity-90">{dictationResult.feedback}</p>
                  </div>

                  {/* Chao Tone Contour Visualizer & Sandhi Hint on Listening Feedback */}
                  <div className="pt-1 border-t border-white/10">
                    <ChaoToneVisualizer
                      pinyin={currentWord.pinyin}
                      hanzi={currentWord.hanzi}
                      compact
                    />
                  </div>
                </div>
              )}
            </div>
          )}

          {showSpeaking && (
            <div className="rounded-3xl bg-slate-800/90 border border-slate-700 p-4 space-y-3.5 shadow-xl">
              <div className="flex items-center justify-between gap-2">
                <span className="text-[10px] font-extrabold uppercase tracking-wider px-2.5 py-1 rounded-full bg-sky-500/15 text-sky-300 border border-sky-500/30 flex items-center gap-1">
                  <Mic className="w-3 h-3" />
                  <span>Speaking (Pronunciation)</span>
                </span>
                <MandarinAudioControls
                  text={currentWord.hanzi}
                  isSlowMode={isSlowMode}
                  onSetSlowMode={onSetSlowMode}
                  speakMandarin={speakMandarin}
                  speakingState={speakingState}
                  size="sm"
                />
              </div>

              <div className="rounded-2xl bg-slate-950/70 border border-slate-800 p-3.5 text-center space-y-2">
                <div className="text-3xl font-black text-white tracking-wider">
                  {currentWord.hanzi}
                </div>
                <div className="text-sm font-bold text-rose-400">{currentWord.pinyin}</div>
                <ChaoToneVisualizer
                  pinyin={currentWord.pinyin}
                  hanzi={currentWord.hanzi}
                  compact
                />
                <p className="text-[11px] text-slate-400">
                  {currentWord.meaning || currentWord.english}
                </p>
              </div>

              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={handleStartSpeechRecognition}
                disabled={isRecording || speakingEvaluated || speakingSkipped}
                className={`game-btn-3d w-full py-3 rounded-2xl font-extrabold text-xs flex items-center justify-center gap-2 transition-all border ${
                  isRecording
                    ? 'bg-rose-600 text-white border-rose-400 animate-pulse shadow-lg shadow-rose-950/50'
                    : similarityData && similarityData.score >= 50
                    ? 'bg-emerald-500/20 border-emerald-400 text-emerald-300'
                    : similarityData
                    ? 'bg-rose-500/20 border-rose-500 text-rose-200'
                    : 'bg-sky-600 hover:bg-sky-500 text-white border-sky-400 shadow-md'
                }`}
              >
                <Mic className="w-4 h-4" />
                <span>
                  {isRecording
                    ? 'Listening in Mandarin (zh-CN)... Speak now!'
                    : speakingEvaluated
                    ? similarityData?.score >= 50
                      ? '✓ Correct Pronunciation!'
                      : `✗ Evaluated — Target: ${currentWord.hanzi} (${currentWord.pinyin})`
                    : `Tap & Say "${currentWord.hanzi}" (${currentWord.pinyin})`}
                </span>
              </button>

              {similarityData && (
                <div
                  className={`rounded-2xl p-3.5 space-y-2 border ${
                    similarityData.score >= 50
                      ? 'bg-emerald-500/15 border-emerald-400/50 text-emerald-200'
                      : 'bg-rose-500/15 border-rose-400/50 text-rose-200'
                  }`}
                >
                  <div className="flex items-center justify-between text-xs">
                    <div>
                      <span className="font-semibold opacity-80">Recognized Speech: </span>
                      <span className="text-white font-black text-sm">
                        "{recognizedText || similarityData.matchedText}"
                      </span>
                    </div>
                    <span className="px-2.5 py-0.5 rounded-full font-black text-xs bg-black/30">
                      {similarityData.score}% Match
                    </span>
                  </div>
                  <p className="text-[11px] font-bold">
                    {similarityData.score >= 50
                      ? `✓ Correct! ${similarityData.feedback}`
                      : `✗ Incorrect — Correct answer: ${currentWord.hanzi} (${currentWord.pinyin})`}
                  </p>
                </div>
              )}

              {micErrorMsg && (
                <div className="rounded-xl bg-amber-500/15 border border-amber-500/30 p-2.5 text-[11px] text-amber-200">
                  {micErrorMsg}
                </div>
              )}

              {!speakingEvaluated && !speakingSkipped && (
                <div className="flex items-center justify-between pt-1">
                  <button
                    type="button"
                    onClick={handleSkipSpeaking}
                    className="game-btn-3d w-full py-2.5 px-3 rounded-xl bg-slate-900 hover:bg-slate-800 border border-slate-700 text-slate-300 hover:text-white text-xs font-bold flex items-center justify-center gap-1.5 transition-colors"
                  >
                    <SkipForward className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-amber-400" />
                    <span>Skip / Can't speak right now</span>
                  </button>
                </div>
              )}
            </div>
          )}
        </div>

        <div className="pt-1 text-center text-[11px] font-bold text-slate-400">
          {canAdvance
            ? 'Auto-advancing to next word...'
            : 'Complete the activity above to auto-advance'}
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

// ============================================================================
// DYNAMIC MULTI-MODALITY & SMART REVIEW SESSION MODAL
// Handles Single-Skill, Multi-Skill Mix, Full Gauntlet, Sentence Builder & Smart Review
// ============================================================================
function AdaptiveMixedSessionModal({
  sessionConfig,
  progress,
  wordNotes = {},
  onSaveWordNote,
  isSlowMode,
  onSetSlowMode,
  speakMandarin,
  speakingState,
  onRecordSkillEvent,
  onClose,
  onComplete
}) {
  const queue = sessionConfig?.queue || [];
  const [stepIdx, setStepIdx] = useState(0);

  // Gamified Combo & Floating XP states
  const [combo, setCombo] = useState(0);
  const [maxCombo, setMaxCombo] = useState(0);
  const [bonusXp, setBonusXp] = useState(0);
  const [xpPopupKey, setXpPopupKey] = useState(0);
  const [xpPopupAmount, setXpPopupAmount] = useState(15);
  const [stepCompleted, setStepCompleted] = useState(false);
  const [cardAnim, setCardAnim] = useState('idle'); // 'idle' | 'correct' | 'wrong'

  // Sub-state for 'recognition' modality (3D Flip + Meaning Check)
  const [isCardFlipped, setIsCardFlipped] = useState(false);
  const [recogAnswered, setRecogAnswered] = useState(false);
  const [recogSelectedOption, setRecogSelectedOption] = useState(null);

  // Sub-state for 'writing' modality (3-Stage Progressive HanziWriter: 1. Guided -> 2. Trace -> 3. Memory)
  const [charIndex, setCharIndex] = useState(0);
  const [charStage, setCharStage] = useState(1);
  const [scriptStatus, setScriptStatus] = useState('loading');
  const [writerModeStatus, setWriterModeStatus] = useState('quiz');
  const [strokeStats, setStrokeStats] = useState({
    completedStrokes: 0,
    totalStrokes: 0,
    mistakesOnCurrent: 0,
    totalMistakes: 0,
    hintTriggered: false
  });
  const [mixedCharStrokeMeta, setMixedCharStrokeMeta] = useState({
    strokeCount: null,
    radStrokes: []
  });
  const [completedCharsInWord, setCompletedCharsInWord] = useState([]);
  const [wordWritingXpAwarded, setWordWritingXpAwarded] = useState(false);
  const writerTargetRef = useRef(null);
  const writerInstanceRef = useRef(null);
  const writerStageTransitionTimerRef = useRef(null);
  const feedbackTimerRef = useRef(null);

  // Sub-state for 'listening' modality (Dictation)
  const [dictationSubMode, setDictationSubMode] = useState('pinyin');
  const [dictationInput, setDictationInput] = useState('');
  const [dictationResult, setDictationResult] = useState(null);

  // Sub-state for 'speaking' modality (SpeechRecognition)
  const [isRecording, setIsRecording] = useState(false);
  const [recognizedText, setRecognizedText] = useState('');
  const [similarityData, setSimilarityData] = useState(null);
  const [speakingSkipped, setSpeakingSkipped] = useState(false);
  const [micErrorMsg, setMicErrorMsg] = useState('');
  const recognitionRef = useRef(null);

  // Sub-state for 'sentence_builder' modality (Word Order Puzzle)
  const [selectedSentenceTiles, setSelectedSentenceTiles] = useState([]);
  const [sentenceCheckStatus, setSentenceCheckStatus] = useState(null); // null | 'correct' | 'wrong'

  // Sub-state for 'cloze' modality (Fill-in-the-Blank 选词填空)
  const [clozeSelectedId, setClozeSelectedId] = useState(null);

  const currentItem = queue[stepIdx] || queue[0];
  const currentWord = currentItem?.word || UNIT_1_WORDS[0];
  const currentModality = currentItem?.modality || 'recognition';
  const currentChar = currentWord.chars?.[charIndex] || currentWord.chars?.[0] || currentWord.hanzi[0];

  const clearAllSessionTimers = useCallback(() => {
    if (writerStageTransitionTimerRef.current) {
      clearTimeout(writerStageTransitionTimerRef.current);
      writerStageTransitionTimerRef.current = null;
    }
    if (feedbackTimerRef.current) {
      clearTimeout(feedbackTimerRef.current);
      feedbackTimerRef.current = null;
    }
  }, []);

  const sentencePuzzle = useMemo(
    () => buildSentenceBuilderPuzzle(currentWord),
    [currentWord]
  );

  const lessonWordPool = useMemo(() => {
    const map = new window.Map();
    queue.forEach((q) => {
      if (q.word?.hanzi) map.set(q.word.hanzi, q.word);
    });
    UNIT_1_WORDS.forEach((w) => {
      if (!map.has(w.hanzi)) map.set(w.hanzi, w);
    });
    return Array.from(map.values());
  }, [queue]);

  const finishMixedSession = useCallback(
    (finalBonusXp = bonusXp, finalMaxCombo = maxCombo) => {
      const uniqueWords = [];
      queue.forEach((item) => {
        if (!uniqueWords.some((w) => w.hanzi === item.word.hanzi)) {
          uniqueWords.push(item.word);
        }
      });
      onComplete(uniqueWords, finalBonusXp, finalMaxCombo);
    },
    [bonusXp, maxCombo, onComplete, queue]
  );

  const scheduleNextQueueItem = useCallback(
    (delayMs = 1400, nextBonusXp = bonusXp, nextMaxCombo = maxCombo) => {
      if (feedbackTimerRef.current) {
        clearTimeout(feedbackTimerRef.current);
      }
      feedbackTimerRef.current = setTimeout(() => {
        if (stepIdx < queue.length - 1) {
          setStepIdx((s) => s + 1);
        } else {
          finishMixedSession(nextBonusXp, nextMaxCombo);
        }
      }, delayMs);
    },
    [bonusXp, finishMixedSession, maxCombo, queue.length, stepIdx]
  );

  const awardComboXp = useCallback(
    (base = 15) => {
      const next = combo + 1;
      const nextMax = Math.max(maxCombo, next);
      setCombo(next);
      setMaxCombo(nextMax);
      soundFX.playCorrect(next);
      const gain = base + (next >= 2 ? next * 3 : 0);
      const nextBonus = bonusXp + gain;
      setBonusXp(nextBonus);
      setXpPopupAmount(gain);
      setXpPopupKey((k) => k + 1);
      return { nextBonus, nextMax };
    },
    [bonusXp, combo, maxCombo]
  );

  const registerMistakeFeedback = useCallback(() => {
    soundFX.playMistake();
    setCombo(0);
    setCardAnim('wrong');
    setTimeout(() => setCardAnim('idle'), 460);
  }, []);

  // Build 3 quick recognition options for the 'recognition' modality
  const recognitionOptions = useMemo(() => {
    const pool = [...UNIT_1_WORDS, ...queue.map((q) => q.word)];
    const uniqueDistractors = [];
    pool.forEach((w) => {
      if (
        w.hanzi !== currentWord.hanzi &&
        !uniqueDistractors.some((d) => d.hanzi === w.hanzi)
      ) {
        uniqueDistractors.push(w);
      }
    });
    while (uniqueDistractors.length < 2) {
      uniqueDistractors.push(UNIT_1_WORDS[uniqueDistractors.length % UNIT_1_WORDS.length]);
    }
    const rawOpts = [
      { id: 'correct', text: currentWord.meaning || currentWord.english, isCorrect: true },
      {
        id: 'd1',
        text: uniqueDistractors[0].meaning || uniqueDistractors[0].english,
        isCorrect: false
      },
      {
        id: 'd2',
        text: uniqueDistractors[1].meaning || uniqueDistractors[1].english,
        isCorrect: false
      }
    ];
    const shift = stepIdx % rawOpts.length;
    return rawOpts.map((_, idx) => rawOpts[(idx + shift) % rawOpts.length]);
  }, [currentWord, queue, stepIdx]);

  // Reset per-step state whenever stepIdx changes
  useEffect(() => {
    clearAllSessionTimers();
    setStepCompleted(false);
    setCardAnim('idle');
    setIsCardFlipped(false);
    setRecogAnswered(false);
    setRecogSelectedOption(null);

    setCharIndex(0);
    setCharStage(1);
    setCompletedCharsInWord([]);
    setWordWritingXpAwarded(false);
    setStrokeStats({
      completedStrokes: 0,
      totalStrokes: 0,
      mistakesOnCurrent: 0,
      totalMistakes: 0,
      hintTriggered: false
    });

    setDictationInput('');
    setDictationResult(null);

    setIsRecording(false);
    setRecognizedText('');
    setSimilarityData(null);
    setSpeakingSkipped(false);
    setMicErrorMsg('');

    setSelectedSentenceTiles([]);
    setSentenceCheckStatus(null);
    setClozeSelectedId(null);

    speakMandarin(
      currentModality === 'sentence_builder'
        ? sentencePuzzle.cleanSentence
        : currentWord.hanzi
    );

    return () => {
      clearAllSessionTimers();
      if (recognitionRef.current) {
        try {
          recognitionRef.current.abort();
        } catch (e) {
          // Ignore
        }
      }
    };
  }, [
    stepIdx,
    currentWord.hanzi,
    currentModality,
    sentencePuzzle.cleanSentence,
    speakMandarin,
    clearAllSessionTimers
  ]);

  // Load HanziWriter if any item in the queue uses 'writing'
  useEffect(() => {
    if (!queue.some((q) => q.modality === 'writing')) return;
    let mounted = true;
    loadHanziWriterScript()
      .then(() => {
        if (mounted) setScriptStatus('ready');
      })
      .catch(() => {
        if (mounted) setScriptStatus('error');
      });
    return () => {
      mounted = false;
      clearAllSessionTimers();
    };
  }, [queue, clearAllSessionTimers]);

  // Preload character stroke count & radical info via HanziWriter.loadCharacterData(char)
  useEffect(() => {
    if (
      currentModality !== 'writing' ||
      scriptStatus !== 'ready' ||
      !window.HanziWriter?.loadCharacterData ||
      !currentChar
    ) {
      return;
    }
    let active = true;
    window.HanziWriter.loadCharacterData(currentChar)
      .then((charData) => {
        if (!active || !charData) return;
        const radStrokes = Array.isArray(charData.radStrokes) ? charData.radStrokes : [];
        setMixedCharStrokeMeta({
          strokeCount: Array.isArray(charData.strokes) ? charData.strokes.length : null,
          radStrokes
        });
        if (writerTargetRef.current && (charStage === 1 || charStage === 2)) {
          highlightRadicalOutlineStrokes(writerTargetRef.current, radStrokes, true);
        }
      })
      .catch(() => {
        if (active) setMixedCharStrokeMeta({ strokeCount: null, radStrokes: [] });
      });
    return () => {
      active = false;
    };
  }, [currentModality, scriptStatus, currentChar, charStage]);

  // Initialize 3-Stage Progressive HanziWriter when currentModality === 'writing'
  const startWriterQuiz = useCallback(
    (writer, targetStage = charStage) => {
      const activeWriter = writer || writerInstanceRef.current;
      if (!activeWriter) return;

      clearAllSessionTimers();

      const activeStageMeta = getWritingStageMeta(targetStage);
      setWriterModeStatus('quiz');
      setStrokeStats((prev) => ({
        ...prev,
        completedStrokes: 0,
        mistakesOnCurrent: 0,
        totalMistakes: 0,
        hintTriggered: false
      }));

      activeWriter.hideCharacter();
      if (activeStageMeta.showOutline) {
        activeWriter.showOutline();
      } else {
        activeWriter.hideOutline();
      }

      activeWriter.quiz({
        showHintAfterMisses: activeStageMeta.showHintAfterMisses,
        highlightOnComplete: true,
        onMistake: (strokeData) => {
          registerMistakeFeedback();
          onRecordSkillEvent?.(currentWord, 'writing', { mistakesDelta: 1, completionsDelta: 0 });
          setStrokeStats((prev) => ({
            ...prev,
            mistakesOnCurrent: strokeData.mistakesOnStroke,
            totalMistakes: strokeData.totalMistakes,
            hintTriggered: strokeData.mistakesOnStroke >= activeStageMeta.showHintAfterMisses
          }));
        },
        onCorrectStroke: (strokeData) => {
          const doneCount = strokeData.strokeNum + 1;
          const total = doneCount + strokeData.strokesRemaining;
          soundFX.playCorrect(combo + 1);
          setStrokeStats({
            completedStrokes: doneCount,
            totalStrokes: total,
            mistakesOnCurrent: 0,
            totalMistakes: strokeData.totalMistakes,
            hintTriggered: false
          });

          // Stage 1 (Guided): Automatically flash the upcoming stroke before user draws it
          if (activeStageMeta.autoHighlightNextStroke && strokeData.strokesRemaining > 0) {
            try {
              activeWriter.highlightStroke(strokeData.strokeNum + 1);
            } catch (e) {
              // Ignore
            }
          }
        },
        onComplete: (summaryData) => {
          setCardAnim('correct');
          setTimeout(() => setCardAnim('idle'), 500);
          setStrokeStats((prev) => ({
            ...prev,
            completedStrokes: prev.totalStrokes || prev.completedStrokes,
            mistakesOnCurrent: 0,
            totalMistakes: summaryData.totalMistakes,
            hintTriggered: false
          }));

          if (targetStage < 3) {
            soundFX.playCorrect(combo + 1);
            setWriterModeStatus('stage_transition');
            writerStageTransitionTimerRef.current = setTimeout(() => {
              setCharStage(targetStage + 1);
            }, 600);
          } else {
            // Stage 3 (Memory Freehand) completed for this character!
            setWriterModeStatus('completed');
            const isFinalChar = charIndex >= (currentWord.chars?.length || 1) - 1;

            setCompletedCharsInWord((prev) => {
              const next = prev.includes(charIndex) ? prev : [...prev, charIndex];
              return next;
            });

            if (!isFinalChar) {
              soundFX.playCorrect(combo + 1);
              feedbackTimerRef.current = setTimeout(() => {
                setCharStage(1);
                setCharIndex((c) => c + 1);
              }, 850);
            } else {
              // Final character of the word completed Stage 3 -> show "Completed!" for 1200ms and auto-advance
              setStepCompleted(true);
              let updatedBonus = bonusXp;
              let updatedMax = maxCombo;
              if (!wordWritingXpAwarded) {
                setWordWritingXpAwarded(true);
                onRecordSkillEvent?.(currentWord, 'writing', {
                  mistakesDelta: 0,
                  completionsDelta: 1
