                });
                const res = awardComboXp(summaryData.totalMistakes === 0 ? 25 : 20);
                updatedBonus = res.nextBonus;
                updatedMax = res.nextMax;
              }
              scheduleNextQueueItem(1200, updatedBonus, updatedMax);
            }
          }
        }
      });

      // Stage 1 (Guided): Trigger highlightStroke(0) at start
      if (activeStageMeta.autoHighlightNextStroke) {
        setTimeout(() => {
          if (writerInstanceRef.current === activeWriter) {
            try {
              activeWriter.highlightStroke(0);
            } catch (e) {
              // Ignore
            }
          }
        }, 60);
      }
    },
    [
      awardComboXp,
      bonusXp,
      charIndex,
      charStage,
      clearAllSessionTimers,
      combo,
      currentWord,
      maxCombo,
      onRecordSkillEvent,
      registerMistakeFeedback,
      scheduleNextQueueItem,
      wordWritingXpAwarded
    ]
  );

  useEffect(() => {
    if (
      currentModality !== 'writing' ||
      scriptStatus !== 'ready' ||
      !writerTargetRef.current ||
      !window.HanziWriter
    ) {
      return;
    }

    const containerEl = writerTargetRef.current;
    if (writerInstanceRef.current?.cancelQuiz) {
      try {
        writerInstanceRef.current.cancelQuiz();
      } catch (e) {
        // Ignore
      }
    }
    // Explicitly clear container before every HanziWriter.create() call so SVG grids never duplicate
    containerEl.innerHTML = '';
    writerInstanceRef.current = null;

    const currentStageConfig = getWritingStageMeta(charStage);

    const writer = window.HanziWriter.create(containerEl, currentChar, {
      width: 240,
      height: 240,
      padding: 18,
      showOutline: currentStageConfig.showOutline,
      showCharacter: false,
      strokeAnimationSpeed: 1.1,
      delayBetweenStrokes: 150,
      strokeColor: '#f8fafc',
      radicalColor: '#dc2626',
      outlineColor: '#334155',
      drawingColor: '#f43f5e',
      drawingWidth: 22,
      highlightColor: '#38bdf8',
      showHintAfterMisses: currentStageConfig.showHintAfterMisses,
      highlightOnComplete: true,
      onLoadCharDataSuccess: (charData) => {
        const radStrokes = Array.isArray(charData.radStrokes) ? charData.radStrokes : [];
        setMixedCharStrokeMeta({
          strokeCount: charData.strokes.length,
          radStrokes
        });
        setStrokeStats({
          completedStrokes: 0,
          totalStrokes: charData.strokes.length,
          mistakesOnCurrent: 0,
          totalMistakes: 0,
          hintTriggered: false
        });
        if (writerInstanceRef.current === writer) {
          startWriterQuiz(writer, charStage);
          if (charStage === 1 || charStage === 2) {
            setTimeout(() => {
              highlightRadicalOutlineStrokes(containerEl, radStrokes, true);
            }, 40);
          }
        }
      }
    });

    writerInstanceRef.current = writer;

    return () => {
      if (writerStageTransitionTimerRef.current) {
        clearTimeout(writerStageTransitionTimerRef.current);
      }
      if (writerInstanceRef.current?.cancelQuiz) {
        try {
          writerInstanceRef.current.cancelQuiz();
        } catch (e) {
          // Ignore
        }
      }
      if (containerEl) containerEl.innerHTML = '';
      if (writerTargetRef.current) writerTargetRef.current.innerHTML = '';
      writerInstanceRef.current = null;
    };
  }, [currentModality, scriptStatus, stepIdx, charIndex, charStage, currentChar, startWriterQuiz]);

  const handleWatchWriterAnimation = () => {
    const writer = writerInstanceRef.current;
    if (!writer) return;
    soundFX.playPop();
    if (writerStageTransitionTimerRef.current) {
      clearTimeout(writerStageTransitionTimerRef.current);
      writerStageTransitionTimerRef.current = null;
    }
    setWriterModeStatus('animating');
    writer.cancelQuiz();
    writer.showOutline();
    writer.showCharacter();
    writer.animateCharacter({
      onComplete: () => {
        setTimeout(() => {
          if (writerInstanceRef.current === writer) {
            writer.hideCharacter();
            startWriterQuiz(writer, charStage);
          }
        }, 400);
      }
    });
  };

  // Handlers for Recognition step
  const handleSelectRecognitionOption = (opt) => {
    if (recogAnswered || stepCompleted) return;
    setRecogSelectedOption(opt.id);
    setRecogAnswered(true);
    setStepCompleted(true);

    if (opt.isCorrect) {
      setCardAnim('correct');
      setTimeout(() => setCardAnim('idle'), 500);
      onRecordSkillEvent?.(currentWord, 'recognition', { mistakesDelta: 0, completionsDelta: 1 });
      const { nextBonus, nextMax } = awardComboXp(15);
      speakMandarin(currentWord.hanzi);
      scheduleNextQueueItem(1400, nextBonus, nextMax);
    } else {
      registerMistakeFeedback();
      onRecordSkillEvent?.(currentWord, 'recognition', { mistakesDelta: 1, completionsDelta: 0 });
      scheduleNextQueueItem(1800, bonusXp, maxCombo);
    }
  };

  // Handlers for Listening step
  const handleCheckListeningDictation = (e) => {
    if (e) e.preventDefault();
    if (stepCompleted || !dictationInput.trim()) return;
    const res = validateDictationInput(dictationInput, currentWord, dictationSubMode);
    setDictationResult(res);
    setStepCompleted(true);

    if (res.isValid) {
      setCardAnim('correct');
      setTimeout(() => setCardAnim('idle'), 500);
      onRecordSkillEvent?.(currentWord, 'listening', { mistakesDelta: 0, completionsDelta: 1 });
      const { nextBonus, nextMax } = awardComboXp(15);
      speakMandarin(currentWord.hanzi);
      scheduleNextQueueItem(1400, nextBonus, nextMax);
    } else {
      registerMistakeFeedback();
      onRecordSkillEvent?.(currentWord, 'listening', { mistakesDelta: 1, completionsDelta: 0 });
      scheduleNextQueueItem(1800, bonusXp, maxCombo);
    }
  };

  // Handlers for Sentence Builder (Word Order Puzzle)
  const validateSentenceOrder = useCallback(
    (candidateTiles) => {
      if (stepCompleted) return;
      const builtString = candidateTiles.map((t) => t.text).join('');
      setStepCompleted(true);

      if (builtString === sentencePuzzle.cleanSentence) {
        setSentenceCheckStatus('correct');
        setCardAnim('correct');
        setTimeout(() => setCardAnim('idle'), 550);
        onRecordSkillEvent?.(currentWord, 'sentence_builder', {
          mistakesDelta: 0,
          completionsDelta: 1
        });
        const { nextBonus, nextMax } = awardComboXp(20);
        speakMandarin(sentencePuzzle.cleanSentence);
        scheduleNextQueueItem(1400, nextBonus, nextMax);
      } else {
        setSentenceCheckStatus('wrong');
        registerMistakeFeedback();
        onRecordSkillEvent?.(currentWord, 'sentence_builder', {
          mistakesDelta: 1,
          completionsDelta: 0
        });
        scheduleNextQueueItem(1800, bonusXp, maxCombo);
      }
    },
    [
      awardComboXp,
      bonusXp,
      currentWord,
      maxCombo,
      onRecordSkillEvent,
      registerMistakeFeedback,
      scheduleNextQueueItem,
      sentencePuzzle.cleanSentence,
      speakMandarin,
      stepCompleted
    ]
  );

  const handleTapAvailableTile = (tile) => {
    if (stepCompleted) return;
    soundFX.playPop();
    speakMandarin(tile.text);
    const nextSelected = [...selectedSentenceTiles, tile];
    setSelectedSentenceTiles(nextSelected);
    setSentenceCheckStatus(null);

    if (nextSelected.length === sentencePuzzle.shuffledTiles.length) {
      validateSentenceOrder(nextSelected);
    }
  };

  const handleRemovePlacedTile = (tileToRemove) => {
    if (stepCompleted) return;
    soundFX.playPop();
    setSelectedSentenceTiles((prev) =>
      prev.filter((t) => t.tileId !== tileToRemove.tileId)
    );
    setSentenceCheckStatus(null);
  };

  // Handlers for Speaking step
  const handleStartSpeakingCheck = () => {
    if (stepCompleted) return;
    setMicErrorMsg('');
    const SpeechRec =
      typeof window !== 'undefined' &&
      (window.SpeechRecognition || window.webkitSpeechRecognition);

    if (!SpeechRec) {
      setMicErrorMsg(
        'SpeechRecognition API is not supported in this browser. Tap "Skip / Can\'t speak right now" below.'
      );
      return;
    }

    try {
      const recognition = new SpeechRec();
      recognitionRef.current = recognition;
      recognition.lang = 'zh-CN';
      recognition.continuous = false;
      recognition.interimResults = false;

      setIsRecording(true);
      recognition.onresult = (event) => {
        setIsRecording(false);
        const transcript = event.results?.[0]?.[0]?.transcript || '';
        setRecognizedText(transcript);
        const computed = computeSpeechSimilarity(transcript, currentWord);
        setSimilarityData(computed);
        setStepCompleted(true);

        if (computed.score >= 50) {
          setCardAnim('correct');
          setTimeout(() => setCardAnim('idle'), 500);
          onRecordSkillEvent?.(currentWord, 'speaking', { mistakesDelta: 0, completionsDelta: 1 });
          const { nextBonus, nextMax } = awardComboXp(computed.score >= 85 ? 20 : 15);
          scheduleNextQueueItem(1400, nextBonus, nextMax);
        } else {
          registerMistakeFeedback();
          onRecordSkillEvent?.(currentWord, 'speaking', { mistakesDelta: 1, completionsDelta: 0 });
          scheduleNextQueueItem(1800, bonusXp, maxCombo);
        }
      };
      recognition.onerror = (event) => {
        setIsRecording(false);
        setMicErrorMsg(
          `Microphone unavailable (${event?.error || 'blocked'}). Tap "Skip / Can't speak right now" below.`
        );
      };
      recognition.onend = () => setIsRecording(false);
      recognition.start();
    } catch (e) {
      setIsRecording(false);
      setMicErrorMsg('Microphone blocked in iframe. Tap "Skip / Can\'t speak right now" below.');
    }
  };

  const modalityInfo = MODALITY_META[currentModality] || MODALITY_META.recognition;
  const masteryLv = getWordMasteryLevel(progress.mastery, currentWord);
  const currentWritingStageMeta = getWritingStageMeta(charStage);
  const isCurrentWritingCharDone =
    (charStage === 3 && writerModeStatus === 'completed') ||
    completedCharsInWord.includes(charIndex);

  return (
    <div className="fixed inset-0 z-50 bg-slate-950/95 backdrop-blur-md flex justify-center">
      <div className="w-full max-w-md bg-slate-900 min-h-screen flex flex-col justify-between p-4 overflow-y-auto">
        <div>
          <SessionGameTopHud
            title={sessionConfig.title}
            subtitle={`Step ${stepIdx + 1}/${queue.length} • ${modalityInfo.label} Mode`}
            totalSegments={queue.length}
            completedSegments={stepIdx + (stepCompleted ? 1 : 0)}
            combo={combo}
            xpPopupKey={xpPopupKey}
            xpPopupAmount={xpPopupAmount}
            onClose={() => {
              clearAllSessionTimers();
              onClose();
            }}
          />

          {/* Dynamic Modality Pill Strip showing current & upcoming modalities */}
          <div className="mt-2.5 flex items-center justify-between gap-2 bg-slate-950/90 border border-slate-800 rounded-2xl px-3 py-2">
            <div className="flex items-center gap-2 min-w-0">
              <span
                className={`text-[10px] font-black uppercase tracking-wider px-2.5 py-0.5 rounded-full border shrink-0 ${modalityInfo.badgeClass}`}
              >
                {modalityInfo.label}
              </span>
              <span className="text-[11px] text-slate-400 truncate">
                {currentItem?.reason || sessionConfig.badgeText}
              </span>
            </div>
            <span className="text-[10px] font-bold text-amber-300 shrink-0">
              Lv.{masteryLv}/5
            </span>
          </div>
        </div>

        {/* MODALITY 1: RECOGNITION (3D FLASHCARD + CHAO TONE CONTOURS + MEANING CHECK) */}
        {currentModality === 'recognition' && (
          <div className="my-auto space-y-3.5 py-3">
            <div
              onClick={() => {
                soundFX.playPop();
                setIsCardFlipped((f) => !f);
                speakMandarin(
                  !isCardFlipped
                    ? currentWord.exampleHanzi || currentWord.hanzi
                    : currentWord.hanzi
                );
              }}
              className={`cursor-pointer rounded-3xl bg-gradient-to-b from-slate-800 to-slate-900 border-2 p-5 text-center space-y-2.5 shadow-xl transition-all ${
                cardAnim === 'wrong'
                  ? 'border-rose-500 animate-game-shake'
                  : cardAnim === 'correct'
                  ? 'border-emerald-400 animate-game-green-pulse'
                  : isCardFlipped
                  ? 'border-emerald-500/50'
                  : 'border-slate-700 hover:border-rose-400/50'
              }`}
            >
              <div className="flex items-center justify-between gap-2">
                <span className="text-[10px] font-extrabold uppercase tracking-wider px-2.5 py-0.5 rounded-full bg-rose-500/15 text-rose-300 border border-rose-500/30">
                  {isCardFlipped ? 'Context & Example' : 'Tap Card to Flip'}
                </span>
                <MandarinAudioControls
                  text={currentWord.hanzi}
                  isSlowMode={isSlowMode}
                  onSetSlowMode={onSetSlowMode}
                  speakMandarin={speakMandarin}
                  speakingState={speakingState}
                  size="sm"
                />
              </div>

              <div className="text-5xl font-black text-white tracking-wider pt-1">
                {currentWord.hanzi}
              </div>
              <div className="text-lg font-extrabold text-rose-400">{currentWord.pinyin}</div>

              <ChaoToneVisualizer
                pinyin={currentWord.pinyin}
                hanzi={currentWord.hanzi}
                compact
              />

              {isCardFlipped && (
                <div
                  className="rounded-2xl bg-slate-950/80 border border-slate-800 p-3 text-left space-y-2 mt-2"
                  onClick={(e) => e.stopPropagation()}
                >
                  <div>
                    <p className="text-xs font-bold text-white">{currentWord.exampleHanzi}</p>
                    <p className="text-[11px] text-rose-300">{currentWord.examplePinyin}</p>
                    <p className="text-[11px] text-slate-300 italic">
                      "{currentWord.exampleEnglish}"
                    </p>
                  </div>
                  {onSaveWordNote && (
                    <div className="pt-1.5 border-t border-slate-800">
                      <label className="text-[10px] font-extrabold uppercase text-amber-400 block mb-1">
                        Personal Note / Mnemonic
                      </label>
                      <input
                        type="text"
                        value={wordNotes[currentWord.hanzi] || wordNotes[currentWord.id] || ''}
                        onChange={(e) => onSaveWordNote(currentWord, e.target.value)}
                        placeholder="Add a personal memory hook or note..."
                        className="w-full bg-slate-900 border border-slate-700 focus:border-amber-400 rounded-xl px-2.5 py-1.5 text-xs text-white placeholder:text-slate-500 outline-none"
                      />
                    </div>
                  )}
                </div>
              )}
            </div>

            <div className="space-y-2">
              <span className="text-[10px] font-extrabold uppercase tracking-wider text-slate-400 block">
                Select the matching English meaning:
              </span>
              {recognitionOptions.map((opt) => {
                const isSelected = recogSelectedOption === opt.id;
                let styleClass =
                  'bg-slate-800/90 hover:bg-slate-800 border-slate-700 text-slate-100';
                if (recogAnswered && opt.isCorrect) {
                  styleClass =
                    'bg-emerald-500/20 border-emerald-400 text-emerald-200 font-extrabold';
                } else if (recogAnswered && isSelected && !opt.isCorrect) {
                  styleClass = 'bg-rose-500/20 border-rose-500 text-rose-200 font-bold';
                } else if (recogAnswered) {
                  styleClass = 'bg-slate-900 border-slate-800 text-slate-500 opacity-60';
                }
                return (
                  <button
                    key={opt.id}
                    type="button"
                    disabled={recogAnswered}
                    onClick={() => handleSelectRecognitionOption(opt)}
                    className={`game-btn-3d w-full p-3.5 rounded-2xl border text-left text-xs font-bold flex items-center justify-between transition-all ${styleClass}`}
                  >
                    <span>{opt.text}</span>
                    {recogAnswered && opt.isCorrect && (
                      <Check className="w-4 h-4 text-emerald-400 shrink-0" />
                    )}
                    {recogAnswered && isSelected && !opt.isCorrect && (
                      <X className="w-4 h-4 text-rose-400 shrink-0" />
                    )}
                  </button>
                );
              })}

              {recogAnswered && (
                <div
                  className={`rounded-xl p-3 text-xs font-bold border ${
                    recognitionOptions.find((o) => o.id === recogSelectedOption)?.isCorrect
                      ? 'bg-emerald-500/15 border-emerald-400/50 text-emerald-200'
                      : 'bg-rose-500/15 border-rose-400/50 text-rose-200'
                  }`}
                >
                  {recognitionOptions.find((o) => o.id === recogSelectedOption)?.isCorrect
                    ? `✓ Correct! ${currentWord.hanzi} (${currentWord.pinyin}) — ${
                        currentWord.meaning || currentWord.english
                      }`
                    : `✗ Incorrect — Correct answer: ${currentWord.meaning || currentWord.english}`}
                </div>
              )}
            </div>
          </div>
        )}

        {/* MODALITY 2: WRITING (3-STAGE PROGRESSIVE STROKE ORDER MI ZI GE) */}
        {currentModality === 'writing' && (
          <div className="my-auto flex flex-col items-center space-y-2.5 py-2">
            <div className="w-full rounded-2xl bg-slate-800/80 border border-slate-700 p-3 flex items-center justify-between gap-2">
              <div className="min-w-0">
                <span className="text-[10px] font-bold uppercase text-amber-400 block truncate">
                  {currentWord.meaning || currentWord.english}
                </span>
                <div className="flex items-baseline gap-2">
                  <span className="text-xl font-black text-white">{currentWord.hanzi}</span>
                  <span className="text-xs font-bold text-rose-400">{currentWord.pinyin}</span>
                </div>
              </div>
              <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5 shrink-0">
                {(currentWord.chars || [currentWord.hanzi]).map((ch, idx) => {
                  const isDone = completedCharsInWord.includes(idx);
                  const isActive = idx === charIndex;
                  const isLocked =
                    idx > 0 && !completedCharsInWord.includes(idx - 1) && !isDone && !isActive;
                  return (
                    <button
                      key={idx}
                      type="button"
                      onClick={() => {
                        if (isLocked) {
                          soundFX.playMistake();
                          return;
                        }
                        soundFX.playPop();
                        setCharStage(isDone ? 3 : 1);
                        setCharIndex(idx);
                      }}
                      className={`w-9 h-9 rounded-xl font-black text-sm border flex items-center justify-center relative ${
                        isActive
                          ? 'bg-amber-500/20 border-amber-400 text-white'
                          : isDone
                          ? 'bg-emerald-500/20 border-emerald-400 text-emerald-300'
                          : isLocked
                          ? 'bg-slate-950 border-slate-800 text-slate-600 opacity-60'
                          : 'bg-slate-900 border-slate-700 text-slate-400'
                      }`}
                    >
                      <span>{ch}</span>
                      {isDone && (
                        <CheckCircle2 className="w-2.5 h-2.5 text-emerald-400 absolute -top-1 -right-1 bg-slate-900 rounded-full" />
                      )}
                    </button>
                  );
                })}
              </div>
            </div>

            {/* 3-Step Progressive Mastery Indicator Pill: 1. Guided -> 2. Trace -> 3. Memory */}
            <div className="w-full max-w-[244px]">
              <div className="grid grid-cols-3 gap-1 p-1 rounded-2xl bg-slate-950 border border-slate-800">
                {WRITING_MASTERY_STAGES.map((st) => {
                  const isCompletedStage =
                    isCurrentWritingCharDone || charStage > st.stage;
                  const isCurrentStage = charStage === st.stage && !isCurrentWritingCharDone;
                  return (
                    <button
                      key={st.stage}
                      type="button"
                      onClick={() => {
                        if (st.stage <= charStage || isCurrentWritingCharDone) {
                          soundFX.playPop();
                          setCharStage(st.stage);
                        }
                      }}
                      className={`py-1.5 px-1.5 rounded-xl text-[10px] font-black flex items-center justify-center gap-1 transition-all border ${
                        isCurrentStage
                          ? 'bg-gradient-to-r from-amber-500 to-rose-500 text-slate-950 border-amber-300 shadow'
                          : isCompletedStage
                          ? 'bg-emerald-500/20 border-emerald-500/40 text-emerald-300'
                          : 'bg-slate-900/70 border-transparent text-slate-500'
                      }`}
                    >
                      {isCompletedStage && <Check className="w-2.5 h-2.5 stroke-[3px] shrink-0" />}
                      <span className="truncate">{st.shortLabel}</span>
                    </button>
                  );
                })}
              </div>
            </div>

            <div className="w-full max-w-[244px] flex items-center justify-between">
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={handleWatchWriterAnimation}
                disabled={scriptStatus !== 'ready'}
                className="game-btn-3d px-3 py-1.5 rounded-xl bg-amber-500/15 border border-amber-400/40 text-amber-300 text-xs font-bold flex items-center gap-1"
              >
                <Eye className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
                <span>Replay Animation</span>
              </button>
              <MandarinAudioControls
                text={currentWord.hanzi}
                isSlowMode={isSlowMode}
                onSetSlowMode={onSetSlowMode}
                speakMandarin={speakMandarin}
                speakingState={speakingState}
                size="sm"
              />
            </div>

            {/* Stroke Count & Radical Info Strip */}
            <div className="w-full max-w-[244px] px-3 py-1.5 rounded-xl bg-slate-950/90 border border-slate-800 flex items-center justify-between gap-2 text-[11px]">
              <span className="font-extrabold text-slate-200">
                "{currentChar}" •{' '}
                {mixedCharStrokeMeta.strokeCount || strokeStats.totalStrokes || '?'}{' '}
                strokes
              </span>
              <span className="inline-flex items-center gap-1 text-[10px] font-bold text-red-300 truncate">
                <span
                  className="w-2 h-2 rounded-full shrink-0"
                  style={{ backgroundColor: '#dc2626' }}
                />
                <span>
                  {KNOWN_CHARACTER_INFO[currentChar]?.radical ||
                    (mixedCharStrokeMeta.radStrokes?.length > 0
                      ? `Radical (${mixedCharStrokeMeta.radStrokes.length} str)`
                      : 'Radical #dc2626')}
                </span>
              </span>
            </div>

            <div
              style={{ touchAction: 'none' }}
              className={`relative w-[244px] h-[244px] rounded-2xl bg-slate-950 border-2 shadow-2xl overflow-hidden select-none touch-none flex items-center justify-center ${
                cardAnim === 'wrong'
                  ? 'border-rose-500 animate-game-shake'
                  : cardAnim === 'correct'
                  ? 'border-emerald-400 animate-game-green-pulse'
                  : charStage === 3
                  ? 'border-emerald-500/50'
                  : charStage === 2
                  ? 'border-amber-500/50'
                  : 'border-sky-500/50'
              }`}
            >
              <svg
                viewBox="0 0 240 240"
                className="absolute inset-0 w-full h-full pointer-events-none opacity-30"
              >
                <rect x="6" y="6" width="228" height="228" fill="none" stroke="#f59e0b" strokeWidth="1.5" />
                <line x1="6" y1="120" x2="234" y2="120" stroke="#f59e0b" strokeWidth="1" strokeDasharray="6 5" />
                <line x1="120" y1="6" x2="120" y2="234" stroke="#f59e0b" strokeWidth="1" strokeDasharray="6 5" />
                <line x1="6" y1="6" x2="234" y2="234" stroke="#f59e0b" strokeWidth="1" strokeDasharray="6 6" />
                <line x1="6" y1="234" x2="234" y2="6" stroke="#f59e0b" strokeWidth="1" strokeDasharray="6 6" />
              </svg>

              {scriptStatus === 'error' && (
                <button
                  type="button"
                  onClick={() => {
                    if (charStage < 3) {
                      setCharStage((s) => s + 1);
                    } else {
                      const next = [...completedCharsInWord, charIndex];
                      setCompletedCharsInWord(next);
                      if (next.length >= (currentWord.chars?.length || 1)) {
                        setStepCompleted(true);
                        scheduleNextQueueItem(1200, bonusXp, maxCombo);
                      } else {
                        setCharStage(1);
                        setCharIndex((c) => c + 1);
                      }
                    }
                  }}
                  className="z-20 px-3 py-1.5 rounded-lg bg-amber-500 text-slate-950 font-bold text-xs"
                >
                  Mark "{currentChar}" Stage {charStage} Complete
                </button>
              )}

              {stepCompleted && (
                <div className="absolute inset-0 z-20 bg-slate-950/80 backdrop-blur-[2px] flex flex-col items-center justify-center gap-2 animate-game-green-pulse pointer-events-none">
                  <div className="w-14 h-14 rounded-full bg-emerald-500/25 border-2 border-emerald-400 flex items-center justify-center shadow-lg">
                    <CheckCircle2 className="w-8 h-8 text-emerald-400" />
                  </div>
                  <span className="text-sm font-black text-emerald-300">Completed! ✓</span>
                </div>
              )}

              <div
                ref={writerTargetRef}
                style={{ touchAction: 'none' }}
                className="relative z-10 w-[240px] h-[240px] touch-none select-none cursor-crosshair"
              />
            </div>

            <div className="w-full max-w-[244px] rounded-xl bg-slate-800/80 border border-slate-700/70 px-3 py-2 text-center">
              <p className="text-[11px] font-bold text-slate-200">
                {writerModeStatus === 'stage_transition'
                  ? `✓ ${currentWritingStageMeta.shortLabel} complete! Starting Stage ${charStage + 1}...`
                  : isCurrentWritingCharDone
                  ? `✓ Completed! Auto-advancing...`
                  : `${currentWritingStageMeta.shortLabel}: Strokes ${strokeStats.completedStrokes}/${
                      strokeStats.totalStrokes || '?'
                    }`}
              </p>
              <p className="text-[10px] text-slate-400 mt-0.5">
                {currentWritingStageMeta.instruction}
              </p>
            </div>
          </div>
        )}

        {/* MODALITY 3: LISTENING (DICTATION) */}
        {currentModality === 'listening' && (
          <div
            className={`my-auto rounded-3xl bg-slate-800/90 border p-4 space-y-3.5 shadow-xl ${
              cardAnim === 'wrong'
                ? 'border-rose-500 animate-game-shake'
                : cardAnim === 'correct'
                ? 'border-emerald-400 animate-game-green-pulse'
                : 'border-slate-700'
            }`}
          >
            <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
              <span className="text-[10px] font-extrabold uppercase text-emerald-300">
                Audio Dictation Challenge
              </span>
              <span className="text-xs font-bold text-slate-300">
                Meaning: <strong className="text-white">{(currentWord.meaning || currentWord.english).split(' / ')[0]}</strong>
              </span>
            </div>

            <div className="rounded-2xl bg-slate-950/80 border border-slate-800 p-3.5 flex items-center justify-between gap-2">
              <div>
                <p className="text-xs font-bold text-white">Listen & Transcribe</p>
                <p className="text-[11px] text-slate-400">
                  {stepCompleted
                    ? `${currentWord.hanzi} (${currentWord.pinyin})`
                    : 'Play native 1.0x Normal or 0.5x Slow audio'}
                </p>
              </div>
              <MandarinAudioControls
                text={currentWord.hanzi}
                isSlowMode={isSlowMode}
                onSetSlowMode={onSetSlowMode}
                speakMandarin={speakMandarin}
                speakingState={speakingState}
              />
            </div>

            <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-1.5 p-1 rounded-xl bg-slate-950 border border-slate-800">
              <button
                type="button"
                disabled={stepCompleted}
                onClick={() => {
                  setDictationSubMode('pinyin');
                  setDictationResult(null);
                }}
                className={`py-2 rounded-lg text-xs font-extrabold ${
                  dictationSubMode === 'pinyin'
                    ? 'bg-emerald-600 text-white'
                    : 'text-slate-400'
                }`}
              >
                Type Pinyin
              </button>
              <button
                type="button"
                disabled={stepCompleted}
                onClick={() => {
                  setDictationSubMode('hanzi');
                  setDictationResult(null);
                }}
                className={`py-2 rounded-lg text-xs font-extrabold ${
                  dictationSubMode === 'hanzi'
                    ? 'bg-emerald-600 text-white'
                    : 'text-slate-400'
                }`}
              >
                Type Hanzi
              </button>
            </div>

            <form onSubmit={handleCheckListeningDictation} className="flex gap-2">
              <input
                type="text"
                disabled={stepCompleted}
                value={dictationInput}
                onChange={(e) => setDictationInput(e.target.value)}
                placeholder={
                  dictationSubMode === 'pinyin'
                    ? 'e.g. nǐ hǎo, ni3 hao3, nihao'
                    : 'Type Hanzi (e.g. 你好)'
                }
                className={`flex-1 bg-slate-950 border rounded-xl px-3.5 py-2.5 text-xs font-bold text-white outline-none ${
                  stepCompleted
                    ? dictationResult?.isValid
                      ? 'border-emerald-400 bg-emerald-950/30 text-emerald-200'
                      : 'border-rose-500 bg-rose-950/30 text-rose-200'
                    : 'border-slate-700 focus:border-emerald-400'
                }`}
              />
              <button
                type="submit"
                disabled={stepCompleted || !dictationInput.trim()}
                className="game-btn-3d px-4 py-2.5 rounded-xl bg-emerald-600 disabled:bg-slate-800 disabled:text-slate-500 text-white font-extrabold text-xs"
              >
                Submit
              </button>
            </form>

            {dictationResult && (
              <div
                className={`rounded-xl p-3 border text-xs space-y-2 ${
                  dictationResult.isValid
                    ? 'bg-emerald-500/15 border-emerald-400/50 text-emerald-200'
                    : 'bg-rose-500/15 border-rose-400/50 text-rose-200'
                }`}
              >
                <div>
                  <p className="font-black">
                    {dictationResult.isValid
                      ? `✓ Correct! (${dictationResult.matchType})`
                      : `✗ Incorrect — Correct answer: ${currentWord.hanzi} (${currentWord.pinyin})`}
                  </p>
                  <p className="text-[11px] opacity-90 mt-0.5">{dictationResult.feedback}</p>
                </div>
                <div className="pt-1 border-t border-white/10">
                  <ChaoToneVisualizer
                    pinyin={currentWord.pinyin}
                    hanzi={currentWord.hanzi}
                    compact
                  />
                </div>
              </div>
            )}
          </div>
        )}

        {/* MODALITY 4: SPEAKING (PRONUNCIATION) */}
        {currentModality === 'speaking' && (
          <div className="my-auto rounded-3xl bg-slate-800/90 border border-slate-700 p-4 space-y-3.5 shadow-xl">
            <div className="flex items-center justify-between gap-2">
              <span className="text-[10px] font-extrabold uppercase text-sky-300">
                Oral Pronunciation Check
              </span>
              <MandarinAudioControls
                text={currentWord.hanzi}
                isSlowMode={isSlowMode}
                onSetSlowMode={onSetSlowMode}
                speakMandarin={speakMandarin}
                speakingState={speakingState}
                size="sm"
              />
            </div>

            <div className="rounded-2xl bg-slate-950/80 border border-slate-800 p-4 text-center space-y-2">
              <div className="text-4xl font-black text-white">{currentWord.hanzi}</div>
              <div className="text-sm font-bold text-rose-400">{currentWord.pinyin}</div>
              <ChaoToneVisualizer
                pinyin={currentWord.pinyin}
                hanzi={currentWord.hanzi}
                compact
              />
              <p className="text-xs text-slate-400">{currentWord.meaning || currentWord.english}</p>
            </div>

            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={handleStartSpeakingCheck}
              disabled={isRecording || stepCompleted}
              className={`game-btn-3d w-full py-3 rounded-2xl font-extrabold text-xs flex items-center justify-center gap-2 border ${
                isRecording
                  ? 'bg-rose-600 text-white border-rose-400 animate-pulse'
                  : similarityData && similarityData.score >= 50
                  ? 'bg-emerald-500/20 border-emerald-400 text-emerald-300'
                  : similarityData
                  ? 'bg-rose-500/20 border-rose-500 text-rose-200'
                  : 'bg-sky-600 hover:bg-sky-500 text-white border-sky-400'
              }`}
            >
              <Mic className="w-4 h-4" />
              <span>
                {isRecording
                  ? 'Listening (zh-CN)... Speak now!'
                  : stepCompleted
                  ? similarityData?.score >= 50 || speakingSkipped
                    ? '✓ Completed!'
                    : `✗ Evaluated — Target: ${currentWord.hanzi} (${currentWord.pinyin})`
                  : `Tap & Speak "${currentWord.hanzi}"`}
              </span>
            </button>

            {similarityData && (
              <div
                className={`rounded-xl p-3 text-xs space-y-1 border ${
                  similarityData.score >= 50
                    ? 'bg-emerald-500/15 border-emerald-400/50 text-emerald-200'
                    : 'bg-rose-500/15 border-rose-400/50 text-rose-200'
                }`}
              >
                <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
                  <span>
                    Heard: <strong className="text-white">"{recognizedText || similarityData.matchedText}"</strong>
                  </span>
                  <span className="font-black">{similarityData.score}% Match</span>
                </div>
                <p className="text-[11px] font-bold">
                  {similarityData.score >= 50
                    ? `✓ Correct! ${similarityData.feedback}`
                    : `✗ Incorrect — Correct answer: ${currentWord.hanzi} (${currentWord.pinyin})`}
                </p>
              </div>
            )}

            {micErrorMsg && (
              <div className="rounded-xl bg-amber-500/15 border border-amber-500/30 p-2.5 text-[11px] text-amber-200">
                {micErrorMsg}
              </div>
            )}

            {!speakingSkipped && !stepCompleted && (
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={() => {
                  soundFX.playPop();
                  setSpeakingSkipped(true);
                  setStepCompleted(true);
                  scheduleNextQueueItem(1400, bonusXp, maxCombo);
                }}
                className="w-full py-2.5 rounded-xl bg-slate-900 border border-slate-700 text-xs font-bold text-slate-300 flex items-center justify-center gap-1.5"
              >
                <SkipForward className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-amber-400" />
                <span>Skip / Can't speak right now</span>
              </button>
            )}
          </div>
        )}

        {/* MODALITY 5: SENTENCE BUILDER (WORD ORDER PUZZLE) */}
        {currentModality === 'sentence_builder' && (
          <div
            className={`my-auto rounded-3xl bg-slate-800/90 border p-4 space-y-3.5 shadow-xl transition-all ${
              cardAnim === 'wrong'
                ? 'border-rose-500 animate-game-shake'
                : cardAnim === 'correct'
                ? 'border-emerald-400 animate-game-green-pulse'
                : 'border-purple-500/40'
            }`}
          >
            <div className="flex items-center justify-between gap-2">
              <span className="text-[10px] font-extrabold uppercase tracking-wider px-2.5 py-1 rounded-full bg-purple-500/20 text-purple-300 border border-purple-500/40">
                Sentence Builder • Word Order
              </span>
              <MandarinAudioControls
                text={sentencePuzzle.cleanSentence}
                isSlowMode={isSlowMode}
                onSetSlowMode={onSetSlowMode}
                speakMandarin={speakMandarin}
                speakingState={speakingState}
                size="sm"
              />
            </div>

            {/* Target English Sentence Prompt */}
            <div className="rounded-2xl bg-slate-950/85 border border-slate-800 p-3.5 space-y-1">
              <span className="text-[10px] font-bold uppercase text-amber-400 block">
                Build this Mandarin sentence (Focus: {currentWord.hanzi} • {currentWord.pinyin}):
              </span>
              <p className="text-sm font-black text-white">
                "{sentencePuzzle.englishTranslation}"
              </p>
              {stepCompleted && (
                <p className="text-xs font-semibold text-rose-300 pt-0.5">
                  {sentencePuzzle.pinyinHint}
                </p>
              )}
            </div>

            {/* Answer Construction Tray */}
            <div className="min-h-[68px] rounded-2xl bg-slate-950 border-2 border-dashed border-slate-700 p-3 flex items-center flex-wrap gap-2">
              {selectedSentenceTiles.length === 0 ? (
                <span className="text-xs text-slate-500 font-semibold mx-auto">
                  Tap the shuffled Chinese word tiles below in the correct order...
                </span>
              ) : (
                selectedSentenceTiles.map((tile) => (
                  <button
                    key={tile.tileId}
                    type="button"
                    disabled={stepCompleted}
                    onClick={() => handleRemovePlacedTile(tile)}
                    className={`game-btn-3d px-3.5 py-2 rounded-xl font-black text-base border shadow transition-all ${
                      sentenceCheckStatus === 'correct'
                        ? 'bg-emerald-500/25 border-emerald-400 text-emerald-200'
                        : sentenceCheckStatus === 'wrong'
                        ? 'bg-rose-500/25 border-rose-400 text-rose-200'
                        : 'bg-purple-600 text-white border-purple-400'
                    }`}
                  >
                    {tile.text}
                  </button>
                ))
              )}
            </div>

            {/* Shuffled Tile Bank */}
            <div className="space-y-2">
              <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
                <span className="text-[10px] font-extrabold uppercase text-slate-400">
                  Available Word Tiles
                </span>
                {selectedSentenceTiles.length > 0 && !stepCompleted && (
                  <button
                    type="button"
                    onClick={() => {
                      soundFX.playPop();
                      setSelectedSentenceTiles([]);
                      setSentenceCheckStatus(null);
                    }}
                    className="text-[11px] font-bold text-amber-300 hover:underline flex items-center gap-1"
                  >
                    <RotateCcw className="w-3 h-3" />
                    <span>Clear Tray</span>
                  </button>
                )}
              </div>

              <div className="flex items-center justify-center flex-wrap gap-2 py-1">
                {sentencePuzzle.shuffledTiles.map((tile) => {
                  const isPlaced = selectedSentenceTiles.some(
                    (t) => t.tileId === tile.tileId
                  );
                  return (
                    <button
                      key={tile.tileId}
                      type="button"
                      disabled={isPlaced || stepCompleted}
                      onClick={() => handleTapAvailableTile(tile)}
                      className={`game-btn-3d px-4 py-2.5 rounded-2xl font-black text-lg border-b-4 transition-all ${
                        isPlaced
                          ? 'bg-slate-900 border-slate-950 text-slate-700 opacity-35 scale-95'
                          : 'bg-slate-900 hover:bg-slate-800 border-slate-950 text-white border border-slate-700 shadow-md'
                      }`}
                    >
                      {tile.text}
                    </button>
                  );
                })}
              </div>
            </div>

            {/* Instant Validation Feedback */}
            {sentenceCheckStatus === 'correct' && (
              <div className="rounded-xl bg-emerald-500/15 border border-emerald-400/50 p-3 text-xs text-emerald-200 flex items-center justify-between">
                <div>
                  <p className="font-black">✓ Correct! Perfect Mandarin Word Order</p>
                  <p className="text-[11px] text-emerald-100 mt-0.5">
                    {sentencePuzzle.targetHanziSentence}
                  </p>
                </div>
                <CheckCircle2 className="w-5 h-5 text-emerald-400 shrink-0" />
              </div>
            )}

            {sentenceCheckStatus === 'wrong' && (
              <div className="rounded-xl bg-rose-500/15 border border-rose-400/50 p-3 text-xs text-rose-200 flex items-center justify-between gap-2">
                <div>
                  <p className="font-black">
                    ✗ Incorrect — Correct answer: {sentencePuzzle.targetHanziSentence}
                  </p>
                  <p className="text-[11px] text-emerald-300 font-bold mt-0.5">
                    {sentencePuzzle.pinyinHint}
                  </p>
                </div>
                <X className="w-5 h-5 text-rose-400 shrink-0" />
              </div>
            )}
          </div>
        )}

        {/* MODALITY 6: FILL-IN-THE-BLANK (选词填空 CLOZE EXERCISE) */}
        {currentModality === 'cloze' && (
          <ClozeExerciseCard
            word={currentWord}
            lessonPool={lessonWordPool}
            seed={stepIdx}
            selectedOptionId={clozeSelectedId}
            isCompleted={stepCompleted}
            cardAnim={cardAnim}
            isSlowMode={isSlowMode}
            onSetSlowMode={onSetSlowMode}
            speakMandarin={speakMandarin}
            speakingState={speakingState}
            onSelectOption={(opt, clozeData) => {
              if (stepCompleted) return;
              setClozeSelectedId(opt.id);
              setStepCompleted(true);
              if (opt.isCorrect) {
                setCardAnim('correct');
                setTimeout(() => setCardAnim('idle'), 500);
                onRecordSkillEvent?.(currentWord, 'cloze', {
                  mistakesDelta: 0,
                  completionsDelta: 1
                });
                const { nextBonus, nextMax } = awardComboXp(20);
                speakMandarin(clozeData.mandarinSentence);
                scheduleNextQueueItem(1400, nextBonus, nextMax);
              } else {
                registerMistakeFeedback();
                onRecordSkillEvent?.(currentWord, 'cloze', {
                  mistakesDelta: 1,
                  completionsDelta: 0
                });
                scheduleNextQueueItem(1800, bonusXp, maxCombo);
              }
            }}
          />
        )}

        {/* Auto-Advance Status Footer */}
        <div className="pt-1 text-center text-[11px] font-bold text-slate-400">
          {stepCompleted
            ? 'Auto-advancing to next item...'
            : `Complete the ${modalityInfo.shortLabel} prompt above`}
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

// ============================================================================
// REUSABLE MANDARIN AUDIO CONTROLS (1.0x NORMAL & 0.5x SLOW NATIVE 'zh-CN')
// ============================================================================
function MandarinAudioControls({
  text,
  isSlowMode,
  onSetSlowMode,
  speakMandarin,
  speakingState,
  size = 'md'
}) {
  const effectiveSlow =
    typeof isSlowMode === 'boolean' ? isSlowMode : globalIsSlowMode;

  const isPlayingNormal =
    speakingState?.active &&
    speakingState?.text === text &&
    speakingState?.rate === NORMAL_SPEECH_RATE;
  const isPlayingSlow =
    speakingState?.active &&
    speakingState?.text === text &&
    speakingState?.rate === SLOW_SPEECH_RATE;

  const btnPadding = size === 'sm' ? 'px-2 py-1 text-[10px]' : 'px-2.5 py-1.5 text-xs';
  const iconClass = size === 'sm' ? 'w-3 h-3' : 'w-3.5 h-3.5';

  const handlePlayRate = (targetSlow) => {
    const rate = targetSlow ? SLOW_SPEECH_RATE : NORMAL_SPEECH_RATE;
    if (onSetSlowMode && targetSlow !== effectiveSlow) {
      // Updates global speed preference in localStorage and immediately plays `text` at the new rate
      onSetSlowMode(targetSlow, text);
    } else {
      speakMandarin(text, rate);
    }
  };

  return (
    <div className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5" onClick={(e) => e.stopPropagation()}>
      <button
        type="button"
        onClick={() => handlePlayRate(false)}
        title="Play 1.0x Normal Speed (zh-CN)"
        className={`rounded-xl font-black border flex items-center gap-1 transition-all active:scale-95 ${btnPadding} ${
          isPlayingNormal
            ? 'bg-rose-500 text-white border-rose-300 shadow-md shadow-rose-950/60 scale-105'
            : !effectiveSlow
            ? 'bg-rose-500/20 text-rose-200 border-rose-400/60'
            : 'bg-slate-900/90 hover:bg-slate-800 text-slate-400 border-slate-700'
        }`}
      >
        <Volume2 className={iconClass} />
        <span>1.0x Normal</span>
      </button>

      <button
        type="button"
        onClick={() => handlePlayRate(true)}
        title="Play 0.5x Slow Speed (zh-CN)"
        className={`rounded-xl font-black border flex items-center gap-1 transition-all active:scale-95 ${btnPadding} ${
          isPlayingSlow
            ? 'bg-sky-400 text-slate-950 border-sky-200 shadow-md shadow-sky-950/60 scale-105'
            : effectiveSlow
            ? 'bg-sky-500/25 text-sky-200 border-sky-400/70'
            : 'bg-slate-900/90 hover:bg-slate-800 text-slate-400 border-slate-700'
        }`}
      >
        <Turtle className={iconClass} />
        <span>0.5x Slow</span>
      </button>
    </div>
  );
}

// ============================================================================
// TAB 1: VISUAL ADVENTURE MAP VIEW (HETEROGENEOUS NODE BLUEPRINTS + WINDING PATH)
// ============================================================================
function ProgressionMapView({
  progress,
  courses,
  activeCourse,
  activeLesson,
  activeLessonIndex,
  activePattern,
  patternPreviewOverride,
  onChangePatternOverride,
  activeWords,
  dailyQuests = [],
  dailyQuestState = {},
  onJumpToQuest,
  onClaimQuestReward,
  onSelectCourseAndLesson,
  speakMandarin,
  onSelectStageNode,
  onOpenLibraryImporter
}) {
  const [selectedStagePreview, setSelectedStagePreview] = useState(null);
  const [questsExpanded, setQuestsExpanded] = useState(true);

  const lessonKey = `${activeCourse.id}__${activeLesson.id}`;
  const isDefaultUnit1 =
    activeCourse.id === 'course_default_unit1' && activeLesson.id === 'lesson_unit_1';

  const currentLessonProg = progress.lessonProgress?.[lessonKey] || {
    unlockedStage: isDefaultUnit1 ? progress.unlockedStage : 1,
    completedStages: isDefaultUnit1 ? progress.completedStages : []
  };

  const getStageIcon = (iconName) => {
    switch (iconName) {
      case 'Layers':
        return Layers;
      case 'PenTool':
        return PenTool;
      case 'Headphones':
        return Headphones;
      case 'Mic':
        return Mic;
      case 'Sparkles':
        return Sparkles;
      case 'Flame':
        return Flame;
      case 'Trophy':
        return Swords;
      default:
        return Star;
    }
  };

  // Winding S-curve horizontal offsets (% from left) for the 4 sequential nodes
  const nodeHorizontalPositions = [50, 24, 76, 50];

  // Calculate unit mastery percentage for ring progress on active node
  const unitMasteryRatio = useMemo(() => {
    const earned = activeWords.reduce((sum, w) => sum + getWordMasteryLevel(progress.mastery, w), 0);
    const max = Math.max(1, activeWords.length * 5);
    return Math.min(1, Math.max(0.15, earned / max));
  }, [activeWords, progress.mastery]);

  const stagesList = activePattern.stages;
  const activeStageObj =
    stagesList.find((s) => s.id === (selectedStagePreview || currentLessonProg.unlockedStage)) ||
    stagesList[0];

  const completedQuestsCount = dailyQuests.filter(
    (q) => dailyQuestState.completedIds?.includes(q.id)
  ).length;
  const unclaimedReadyCount = dailyQuests.filter(
    (q) =>
      dailyQuestState.completedIds?.includes(q.id) &&
      !dailyQuestState.claimedIds?.includes(q.id)
  ).length;

  return (
    <div className="p-4 space-y-4">
      {/* COMPACT COLLAPSIBLE DAILY QUESTS WIDGET (TOP OF MAP TAB) */}
      <div className="rounded-3xl bg-gradient-to-br from-slate-800/95 via-slate-900 to-slate-950 border border-amber-500/40 p-3.5 shadow-xl space-y-2.5">
        <button
          type="button"
          onClick={() => {
            soundFX.playPop();
            setQuestsExpanded((prev) => !prev);
          }}
          className="w-full flex items-center justify-between gap-2 text-left"
        >
          <div className="flex items-center gap-2 min-w-0">
            <div className="w-8 h-8 rounded-xl bg-amber-500/20 border border-amber-400/50 flex items-center justify-center text-amber-300 shrink-0">
              <Award className="w-4 h-4" />
            </div>
            <div className="min-w-0">
              <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5">
                <span className="text-xs font-black uppercase tracking-wider text-amber-300">
                  Daily Quests
                </span>
                {unclaimedReadyCount > 0 && (
                  <span className="px-2 py-0.5 rounded-full bg-emerald-500 text-slate-950 font-black text-[9px] uppercase animate-bounce">
                    {unclaimedReadyCount} Claim Ready!
                  </span>
                )}
              </div>
              <p className="text-[10px] text-slate-400 truncate">
                {completedQuestsCount}/3 Completed • Refreshes Daily ({dailyQuestState.date})
              </p>
            </div>
          </div>

          <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5 shrink-0">
            <span className="text-[10px] font-black px-2 py-0.5 rounded-lg bg-slate-950 border border-slate-800 text-amber-300">
              {completedQuestsCount}/3
            </span>
            <ChevronDown
              className={`w-4 h-4 text-slate-400 transition-transform ${
                questsExpanded ? 'rotate-180' : ''
              }`}
            />
          </div>
        </button>

        {questsExpanded && (
          <div className="space-y-2 pt-1 border-t border-slate-800/80">
            {dailyQuests.map((quest) => {
              const isDone = dailyQuestState.completedIds?.includes(quest.id);
              const isClaimed = dailyQuestState.claimedIds?.includes(quest.id);

              return (
                <div
                  key={quest.id}
                  onClick={() => {
                    if (!isDone && !isClaimed) {
                      onJumpToQuest?.(quest);
                    }
                  }}
                  className={`rounded-2xl p-2.5 border flex items-center justify-between gap-2.5 transition-all ${
                    isClaimed
                      ? 'bg-slate-950/60 border-slate-800/80 opacity-65'
                      : isDone
                      ? 'bg-emerald-950/40 border-emerald-400/60 shadow-md shadow-emerald-950/40'
                      : 'cursor-pointer bg-slate-900/90 hover:bg-slate-800/90 border-slate-700/80'
                  }`}
                >
                  <div className="flex items-center gap-2.5 min-w-0">
                    <div
                      className={`w-6 h-6 rounded-lg flex items-center justify-center text-xs font-black shrink-0 ${
                        isClaimed
                          ? 'bg-emerald-500/20 text-emerald-400'
                          : isDone
                          ? 'bg-emerald-500 text-slate-950'
                          : 'bg-slate-800 text-slate-400'
                      }`}
                    >
                      {isDone || isClaimed ? <Check className="w-3.5 h-3.5 stroke-[3px]" /> : '•'}
                    </div>
                    <div className="min-w-0">
                      <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5 flex-wrap">
                        <span className="text-xs font-extrabold text-white truncate">
                          {quest.title}
                        </span>
                      </div>
                      <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5 mt-0.5">
                        <span className="text-[9px] font-extrabold uppercase px-1.5 py-0.2 rounded bg-slate-800 text-slate-300">
                          {quest.badge}
                        </span>
                        <span className="text-[10px] font-black text-amber-300">
                          +{quest.xpReward} XP
                        </span>
                      </div>
                    </div>
                  </div>

                  {isClaimed ? (
                    <span className="px-2.5 py-1 rounded-xl bg-slate-900 border border-slate-800 text-[10px] font-black text-emerald-400 shrink-0">
                      Claimed ✓
                    </span>
                  ) : isDone ? (
                    <button
                      type="button"
                      onClick={(e) => {
                        e.stopPropagation();
                        onClaimQuestReward?.(quest);
                      }}
                      className="game-btn-3d px-3 py-1.5 rounded-xl bg-gradient-to-r from-amber-400 via-emerald-400 to-teal-400 text-slate-950 font-black text-[11px] shadow-lg shadow-emerald-500/30 border border-amber-200 animate-pulse shrink-0"
                    >
                      Claim +{quest.xpReward} XP
                    </button>
                  ) : (
                    <button
                      type="button"
                      onClick={(e) => {
                        e.stopPropagation();
                        onJumpToQuest?.(quest);
                      }}
                      className="game-btn-3d px-2.5 py-1.5 rounded-xl bg-slate-800 hover:bg-slate-700 border border-slate-600 text-[10px] font-extrabold text-amber-300 flex items-center gap-1 shrink-0"
                    >
                      <span>Go</span>
                      <ChevronRight className="w-3 h-3" />
                    </button>
                  )}
                </div>
              );
            })}
          </div>
        )}
      </div>

      {/* Course & Lesson Selector Card */}
      <div className="rounded-2xl bg-slate-800/90 border border-slate-700/80 p-3.5 space-y-2.5 shadow-lg">
        <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
          <span className="text-[10px] font-extrabold uppercase tracking-wider text-rose-400">
            Adventure World & Unit Selector
          </span>
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={onOpenLibraryImporter}
            className="text-[11px] font-bold text-sky-400 hover:text-sky-300 flex items-center gap-1"
          >
            <Upload className="w-3 h-3" />
            <span>+ Import HSK / JSON</span>
          </button>
        </div>

        <div className="grid grid-cols-1 gap-2">
          <div className="relative">
            <select
              value={activeCourse.id}
              onChange={(e) => onSelectCourseAndLesson(e.target.value, null)}
              className="w-full appearance-none bg-slate-950 border border-slate-700 focus:border-rose-500 rounded-xl px-3.5 py-2.5 pr-8 text-xs font-extrabold text-white outline-none"
            >
              {courses.map((course) => (
                <option key={course.id} value={course.id}>
                  {course.courseName} ({course.lessons?.length || 1} Unit
                  {(course.lessons?.length || 1) > 1 ? 's' : ''})
                </option>
              ))}
            </select>
            <ChevronDown className="w-4 h-4 text-slate-400 absolute right-3 top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 pointer-events-none" />
          </div>

          {activeCourse.lessons && activeCourse.lessons.length > 1 && (
            <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5 overflow-x-auto pb-1 pt-0.5">
              {activeCourse.lessons.map((lesson, lIdx) => {
                const isSelected = lesson.id === activeLesson.id;
                const patCode = HETEROGENEOUS_PATTERNS[lIdx % HETEROGENEOUS_PATTERNS.length].code;
                return (
                  <button
                    key={lesson.id}
                    type="button"
                    onClick={() => onSelectCourseAndLesson(activeCourse.id, lesson.id)}
                    className={`game-btn-3d px-3 py-1.5 rounded-xl text-xs font-bold shrink-0 border transition-all ${
                      isSelected
                        ? 'bg-rose-600 text-white border-rose-400 shadow'
                        : 'bg-slate-900 text-slate-400 border-slate-800 hover:text-slate-200'
                    }`}
                  >
                    {lesson.title} • {patCode}
                  </button>
                );
              })}
            </div>
          )}
        </div>

        {/* Heterogeneous Blueprint Inspector / Switcher */}
        <div className="pt-2 border-t border-slate-700/60 flex items-center justify-between gap-2">
          <span className="text-[10px] font-bold text-slate-400">
            Node Blueprint (Rotates by Unit #{activeLessonIndex + 1}):
          </span>
          <div className="flex items-center gap-1">
            {[
              { id: null, label: 'Auto' },
              { id: 'pattern_a', label: 'Pat A' },
              { id: 'pattern_b', label: 'Pat B' },
              { id: 'pattern_c', label: 'Pat C' }
            ].map((opt) => {
              const isSelected = patternPreviewOverride === opt.id;
              return (
                <button
                  key={opt.label}
                  type="button"
                  onClick={() => onChangePatternOverride(opt.id)}
                  className={`px-2 py-0.5 rounded-lg text-[10px] font-black border transition-all ${
                    isSelected
                      ? 'bg-amber-500/25 border-amber-400 text-amber-200'
                      : 'bg-slate-900 border-slate-800 text-slate-400 hover:text-slate-200'
                  }`}
                >
                  {opt.label}
                </button>
              );
            })}
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* Active Unit Adventure World Banner with Pattern Blueprint Tag */}
      <div className="rounded-3xl bg-gradient-to-br from-rose-600 via-red-700 to-amber-700 border border-rose-400/40 p-4 shadow-xl relative overflow-hidden">
        <div className="flex items-center justify-between gap-2 relative z-10 flex-wrap">
          <span className="text-[10px] font-black uppercase tracking-wider px-2.5 py-1 rounded-full bg-black/35 text-amber-200 border border-white/15">
            {activePattern.code} • {activePattern.name}
          </span>
          <span className="text-xs font-black text-amber-200 flex items-center gap-1 bg-black/25 px-2.5 py-1 rounded-full">
            <Crown className="w-3.5 h-3.5 fill-amber-400 text-amber-400" />
            <span>{currentLessonProg.completedStages.length} / 4 Mastered</span>
          </span>
        </div>
        <h1 className="text-lg font-black text-white mt-2 relative z-10">{activeLesson.title}</h1>
        <p className="text-[11px] text-rose-100/90 mt-0.5 relative z-10 font-semibold">
          {activePattern.tagline}
        </p>
      </div>

      {/* WINDING VERTICAL ADVENTURE PATH OF HETEROGENEOUS CIRCULAR GAME NODES */}
      <div className="rounded-3xl bg-gradient-to-b from-slate-900 via-slate-900/95 to-slate-950 border border-slate-800 p-5 relative overflow-hidden shadow-2xl">
        <div className="w-40 h-40 rounded-full bg-rose-500/5 blur-3xl absolute top-6 left-4 pointer-events-none" />
        <div className="w-44 h-44 rounded-full bg-amber-500/5 blur-3xl absolute bottom-10 right-4 pointer-events-none" />

        <div className="relative w-full h-[490px]">
          {/* SVG Winding Connecting Path */}
          <svg
            viewBox="0 0 100 460"
            preserveAspectRatio="none"
            className="absolute inset-0 w-full h-full pointer-events-none"
          >
            {stagesList.slice(0, -1).map((stage, idx) => {
              const x1 = nodeHorizontalPositions[idx];
              const y1 = 55 + idx * 116;
              const x2 = nodeHorizontalPositions[idx + 1];
              const y2 = 55 + (idx + 1) * 116;
              const midY = (y1 + y2) / 2;
              const pathD = `M ${x1} ${y1} C ${x1} ${midY}, ${x2} ${midY}, ${x2} ${y2}`;

              const isSegmentMastered = currentLessonProg.completedStages.includes(stage.id);

              return (
                <g key={`path-seg-${stage.id}`}>
                  <path
                    d={pathD}
                    fill="none"
                    stroke="#0f172a"
                    strokeWidth="4.5"
                    strokeLinecap="round"
                  />
                  <path
                    d={pathD}
                    fill="none"
                    stroke={isSegmentMastered ? '#fbbf24' : '#334155'}
                    strokeWidth="2.8"
                    strokeDasharray={isSegmentMastered ? 'none' : '4 4'}
                    strokeLinecap="round"
                  />
                </g>
              );
            })}
          </svg>

          {/* Render the 4 Heterogeneous Game Nodes along the winding path */}
          {stagesList.map((stage, idx) => {
            const StageIcon = getStageIcon(stage.iconName);
            const isMastered = currentLessonProg.completedStages.includes(stage.id);
            const isUnlocked = stage.id <= currentLessonProg.unlockedStage || isMastered;
            const isCurrent = stage.id === currentLessonProg.unlockedStage && !isMastered;

            const leftPercent = nodeHorizontalPositions[idx];
            const topPx = 14 + idx * 116;

            const radius = 38;
            const circumference = 2 * Math.PI * radius;
            const ringProgress = isMastered ? 1 : isCurrent ? Math.max(0.35, unitMasteryRatio) : 0;
            const dashOffset = circumference * (1 - ringProgress);

            const categoryBadgeStyle =
              stage.nodeCategory === 'gauntlet'
                ? 'bg-fuchsia-500/25 border-fuchsia-400/60 text-fuchsia-200'
                : stage.nodeCategory === 'mix'
                ? 'bg-emerald-500/20 border-emerald-400/50 text-emerald-200'
                : stage.nodeCategory === 'boss'
                ? 'bg-purple-500/25 border-purple-400/50 text-purple-200'
                : 'bg-sky-500/20 border-sky-400/40 text-sky-200';

            return (
              <div
                key={stage.id}
                style={{ left: `${leftPercent}%`, top: `${topPx}px` }}
                className="absolute -translate-x-1/2 flex flex-col items-center z-10"
              >
                {isCurrent && (
                  <div className="animate-float-bob -top-6 absolute whitespace-nowrap px-2.5 py-0.5 rounded-full bg-gradient-to-r from-rose-500 to-amber-500 text-slate-950 font-black text-[10px] uppercase tracking-wider shadow-lg border border-amber-200">
                    ★ Start Here
                  </div>
                )}

                <div className="relative flex items-center justify-center w-24 h-24">
                  {isCurrent && (
                    <div className="absolute inset-1 rounded-full border-2 border-rose-400/70 animate-node-ring pointer-events-none" />
                  )}

                  <svg className="w-24 h-24 -rotate-90 pointer-events-none" viewBox="0 0 88 88">
                    <circle
                      cx="44"
                      cy="44"
                      r={radius}
                      fill="none"
                      stroke={isMastered ? '#78350f' : isCurrent ? '#334155' : '#1e293b'}
                      strokeWidth="5"
                    />
                    {ringProgress > 0 && (
                      <circle
                        cx="44"
                        cy="44"
                        r={radius}
                        fill="none"
                        stroke={isMastered ? '#fbbf24' : '#f43f5e'}
                        strokeWidth="5.5"
                        strokeLinecap="round"
                        strokeDasharray={circumference}
                        strokeDashoffset={dashOffset}
                        className="transition-all duration-700"
                      />
                    )}
                  </svg>

                  <button
                    type="button"
                    onClick={() => {
                      setSelectedStagePreview(stage.id);
                      if (isUnlocked) {
                        onSelectStageNode(stage);
                      } else {
                        soundFX.playMistake();
                      }
                    }}
                    className={`game-btn-3d absolute w-16 h-16 rounded-full flex items-center justify-center border-b-4 shadow-xl transition-all ${
                      isMastered
                        ? 'bg-gradient-to-b from-amber-400 via-amber-500 to-yellow-600 border-amber-700 text-slate-950 shadow-amber-950/60'
                        : isCurrent
                        ? `bg-gradient-to-b ${stage.accentColor} border-rose-900 text-white shadow-rose-950/70 scale-105`
                        : 'bg-slate-800/90 border-slate-950 text-slate-500 opacity-65'
                    }`}
                  >
                    {!isUnlocked ? (
                      <Lock className="w-6 h-6 text-slate-500" />
                    ) : (
                      <StageIcon className="w-7 h-7 stroke-[2.4px]" />
                    )}
                  </button>

                  {isMastered && (
                    <div className="absolute -top-1 -right-1 w-7 h-7 rounded-full bg-amber-400 border-2 border-slate-950 flex items-center justify-center shadow-md">
                      <Crown className="w-3.5 h-3.5 fill-slate-950 text-slate-950" />
                    </div>
                  )}

                  {!isUnlocked && (
                    <div className="absolute -bottom-0.5 -right-0.5 w-6 h-6 rounded-full bg-slate-800 border border-slate-700 flex items-center justify-center">
                      <Lock className="w-3 h-3 text-slate-400" />
                    </div>
                  )}
                </div>

                {/* Node Title & Single-Skill / Multi-Skill Badge */}
                <div className="mt-0.5 flex flex-col items-center gap-0.5">
                  <span
                    className={`text-[11px] font-black px-2.5 py-0.5 rounded-full border whitespace-nowrap ${
                      isMastered
                        ? 'bg-amber-500/15 border-amber-400/40 text-amber-300'
                        : isCurrent
                        ? 'bg-rose-500/20 border-rose-400/50 text-white'
                        : 'bg-slate-900/90 border-slate-800 text-slate-400'
                    }`}
                  >
                    {stage.shortTitle}
                  </span>
                  <span
                    className={`text-[9px] font-black uppercase tracking-wider px-2 py-0.2 rounded-full border whitespace-nowrap ${categoryBadgeStyle}`}
                  >
                    {stage.badgeText}
                  </span>
                </div>
              </div>
            );
          })}
        </div>

        {/* Selected / Current Stage Quick Launch Footer Card */}
        {activeStageObj && (
          <div className="mt-2 rounded-2xl bg-slate-800/95 border border-slate-700 p-3.5 flex items-center justify-between gap-3">
            <div className="min-w-0">
              <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5 flex-wrap">
                <span className="text-[10px] font-black uppercase text-rose-400">
                  {activeStageObj.title}
                </span>
                <span className="text-[10px] font-black px-1.5 py-0.5 rounded bg-amber-500/20 text-amber-300">
                  +{activeStageObj.xpReward} XP
                </span>
              </div>
              <p className="text-xs font-bold text-white truncate mt-0.5">
                {activeStageObj.subtitle}
              </p>
              <p className="text-[11px] text-slate-400 line-clamp-1">
                {activeStageObj.description}
              </p>
            </div>

            <button
              type="button"
              disabled={
                activeStageObj.id > currentLessonProg.unlockedStage &&
                !currentLessonProg.completedStages.includes(activeStageObj.id)
              }
              onClick={() => onSelectStageNode(activeStageObj)}
              className="game-btn-3d px-4 py-2.5 rounded-xl bg-gradient-to-r from-rose-500 to-red-600 disabled:from-slate-800 disabled:to-slate-800 disabled:text-slate-500 text-white font-black text-xs shrink-0 border-b-4 border-rose-800 disabled:border-slate-900 flex items-center gap-1.5 shadow-lg"
            >
              <Play className="w-3.5 h-3.5 fill-current" />
              <span>
                {currentLessonProg.completedStages.includes(activeStageObj.id)
                  ? 'Replay'
                  : 'Play'}
              </span>
            </button>
          </div>
        )}
      </div>

      {/* Character Mastery Strip (Levels 0 to 5 persisted in localStorage) */}
      <div className="rounded-2xl bg-slate-800/75 border border-slate-700/80 p-3.5 space-y-2.5">
        <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
          <span className="text-[11px] font-extrabold uppercase tracking-wider text-slate-300">
            Unit Vocabulary Mastery (Lv 0–5)
          </span>
          <span className="text-[11px] font-bold text-emerald-400">Tap Card for Audio</span>
        </div>
        <div className="grid grid-cols-3 gap-2 max-h-56 overflow-y-auto pr-0.5">
          {activeWords.map((word) => {
            const level = getWordMasteryLevel(progress.mastery, word);
            return (
              <div
                key={word.id}
                onClick={() => {
                  soundFX.playPop();
                  speakMandarin(word.hanzi, progress.preferredSpeed);
                }}
                className="game-btn-3d cursor-pointer rounded-2xl bg-slate-900/90 hover:bg-slate-900 border border-slate-800 p-2.5 text-center transition-all"
              >
                <div className="text-lg font-black text-white truncate">{word.hanzi}</div>
                <div className="text-[11px] font-bold text-rose-400 truncate">{word.pinyin}</div>
                <div className="text-[10px] text-slate-400 truncate mt-0.5">
                  {(word.meaning || word.english).split(/[/;]/)[0]}
                </div>
                <div className="flex items-center justify-center gap-0.5 mt-1.5">
                  {[1, 2, 3, 4, 5].map((starIdx) => (
                    <Star
                      key={starIdx}
                      className={`w-2.5 h-2.5 ${
                        starIdx <= level
                          ? 'fill-amber-400 text-amber-400'
                          : 'text-slate-700'
                      }`}
                    />
                  ))}
                </div>
                <div className="text-[10px] font-bold text-emerald-400 mt-0.5">
                  Lv.{level}/5
                </div>
              </div>
            );
          })}
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

// ============================================================================
// STAGE 1 MODAL: 3D FLIPPABLE FLASHCARDS WITH CHAO TONE VISUALIZER, COMBO & FLOATING XP
// ============================================================================
function FlashcardStageModal({
  words = UNIT_1_WORDS,
  progress,
  wordNotes = {},
  onSaveWordNote,
  isSlowMode,
  onSetSlowMode,
  speakMandarin,
  speakingState,
  onRecordSkillEvent,
  onClose,
  onComplete
}) {
  const [cardIndex, setCardIndex] = useState(0);
  const [isFlipped, setIsFlipped] = useState(false);
  const [combo, setCombo] = useState(1);
  const [maxCombo, setMaxCombo] = useState(1);
  const [bonusXp, setBonusXp] = useState(0);
  const [xpPopupKey, setXpPopupKey] = useState(0);
  const [flippedCardsSet, setFlippedCardsSet] = useState(new Set());

  const currentWord = words[cardIndex] || words[0];
  const masteryLevel = getWordMasteryLevel(progress.mastery, currentWord);

  useEffect(() => {
    speakMandarin(currentWord.hanzi);
  }, [cardIndex, currentWord.hanzi, speakMandarin]);

  const handleFlipCard = () => {
    const nextFlipped = !isFlipped;
    setIsFlipped(nextFlipped);
    soundFX.playPop();

    if (nextFlipped && !flippedCardsSet.has(cardIndex)) {
      const nextSet = new Set(flippedCardsSet);
      nextSet.add(cardIndex);
      setFlippedCardsSet(nextSet);

      onRecordSkillEvent?.(currentWord, 'recognition', { mistakesDelta: 0, completionsDelta: 1 });
      setCombo((prev) => {
        const nextCombo = prev + 1;
        setMaxCombo((m) => Math.max(m, nextCombo));
        soundFX.playCorrect(nextCombo);
        return nextCombo;
      });
      setBonusXp((prev) => prev + 15);
      setXpPopupKey((prev) => prev + 1);
    }

    if (nextFlipped) {
      speakMandarin(currentWord.exampleHanzi || currentWord.hanzi);
    } else {
      speakMandarin(currentWord.hanzi);
    }
  };

  const handleNextCard = () => {
    if (!flippedCardsSet.has(cardIndex)) {
      soundFX.playCorrect(combo);
      setBonusXp((prev) => prev + 15);
      setXpPopupKey((prev) => prev + 1);
    } else {
      soundFX.playPop();
    }

    if (cardIndex < words.length - 1) {
      setCardIndex((prev) => prev + 1);
      setIsFlipped(false);
    } else {
      onComplete(bonusXp, maxCombo);
    }
  };

  const handlePrevCard = () => {
    if (cardIndex > 0) {
      soundFX.playPop();
      setCardIndex((prev) => prev - 1);
      setIsFlipped(false);
    }
  };

  return (
    <div className="fixed inset-0 z-50 bg-slate-950/95 backdrop-blur-md flex justify-center">
