      <div className="w-full max-w-md bg-slate-900 min-h-screen flex flex-col justify-between p-4 overflow-y-auto">
        <SessionGameTopHud
          title="Stage 1 • Flashcards"
          subtitle={`Card ${cardIndex + 1} of ${words.length} • Mastery Lv.${masteryLevel}/5`}
          totalSegments={words.length}
          completedSegments={cardIndex + (isFlipped ? 1 : 0)}
          combo={combo}
          xpPopupKey={xpPopupKey}
          xpPopupAmount={15}
          onClose={onClose}
        />

        {/* Smooth 3D Perspective Card Flip Container */}
        <div className="my-auto py-4" style={{ perspective: '1200px' }}>
          <div
            onClick={handleFlipCard}
            style={{
              transformStyle: 'preserve-3d',
              transform: isFlipped ? 'rotateY(180deg)' : 'rotateY(0deg)'
            }}
            className={`cursor-pointer relative w-full min-h-[370px] transition-transform duration-500 select-none ${
              isFlipped ? 'animate-game-green-pulse' : ''
            }`}
          >
            {/* FRONT FACE */}
            <div
              style={{ backfaceVisibility: 'hidden' }}
              className="absolute inset-0 rounded-3xl bg-gradient-to-b from-slate-800 to-slate-900 border-2 border-slate-700 hover:border-rose-500/60 p-5 shadow-2xl flex flex-col justify-between"
            >
              <div className="flex items-center justify-between gap-2">
                <span className="text-[10px] font-extrabold uppercase tracking-wider px-2.5 py-1 rounded-full bg-rose-500/15 text-rose-300 border border-rose-500/30">
                  Front • Hanzi & Pinyin
                </span>
                <MandarinAudioControls
                  text={currentWord.hanzi}
                  isSlowMode={isSlowMode}
                  onSetSlowMode={onSetSlowMode}
                  speakMandarin={speakMandarin}
                  speakingState={speakingState}
                  size="sm"
                />
              </div>

              <div className="my-auto text-center space-y-2.5 py-3">
                <div className="text-6xl font-black text-white tracking-widest">
                  {currentWord.hanzi}
                </div>
                <div className="text-2xl font-extrabold text-rose-400">{currentWord.pinyin}</div>

                {/* Chao Tone Contour Visualizer & Tone Sandhi Detector */}
                <ChaoToneVisualizer
                  pinyin={currentWord.pinyin}
                  hanzi={currentWord.hanzi}
                />

                <p className="text-[11px] text-slate-400">{currentWord.toneNote}</p>
              </div>

              <div className="text-center pt-2 border-t border-slate-800/80">
                <span className="text-xs font-bold text-amber-300 inline-flex items-center gap-1.5">
                  <RefreshCw className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
                  <span>Tap card to 3D flip (+15 XP)</span>
                </span>
              </div>
            </div>

            {/* BACK FACE (Rotated 180deg) */}
            <div
              style={{
                backfaceVisibility: 'hidden',
                transform: 'rotateY(180deg)'
              }}
              className="absolute inset-0 rounded-3xl bg-gradient-to-b from-slate-800 via-slate-900 to-slate-950 border-2 border-emerald-500/50 p-5 shadow-2xl flex flex-col justify-between"
            >
              <div className="flex items-center justify-between gap-2">
                <span className="text-[10px] font-extrabold uppercase tracking-wider px-2.5 py-1 rounded-full bg-emerald-500/15 text-emerald-300 border border-emerald-500/30">
                  Back • Meaning & Context
                </span>
                <MandarinAudioControls
                  text={currentWord.hanzi}
                  isSlowMode={isSlowMode}
                  onSetSlowMode={onSetSlowMode}
                  speakMandarin={speakMandarin}
                  speakingState={speakingState}
                  size="sm"
                />
              </div>

              <div className="my-auto space-y-3 py-1">
                <div className="text-center border-b border-slate-800 pb-2.5">
                  <div className="flex items-center justify-center gap-2">
                    <span className="text-3xl font-black text-white">{currentWord.hanzi}</span>
                    <span className="text-lg font-bold text-rose-400">({currentWord.pinyin})</span>
                  </div>
                  <div className="text-base font-extrabold text-emerald-400 mt-0.5">
                    {currentWord.meaning || currentWord.english}
                  </div>
                  <p className="text-[11px] text-slate-400 mt-0.5">{currentWord.literal}</p>
                </div>

                <div className="rounded-2xl bg-slate-950/80 border border-slate-800 p-3 space-y-1.5">
                  <div className="flex items-center justify-between gap-1">
                    <span className="text-[10px] font-bold uppercase text-amber-400">
                      Example Sentence
                    </span>
                    <MandarinAudioControls
                      text={currentWord.exampleHanzi || currentWord.hanzi}
                      isSlowMode={isSlowMode}
                      onSetSlowMode={onSetSlowMode}
                      speakMandarin={speakMandarin}
                      speakingState={speakingState}
                      size="sm"
                    />
                  </div>
                  <p className="text-sm font-bold text-white">{currentWord.exampleHanzi}</p>
                  <p className="text-xs font-semibold text-rose-300">
                    {currentWord.examplePinyin}
                  </p>
                  <p className="text-xs text-slate-300 italic">"{currentWord.exampleEnglish}"</p>
                </div>

                {/* Compact Editable Personal Note / Mnemonic saved in localStorage per word */}
                <div
                  onClick={(e) => e.stopPropagation()}
                  className="rounded-2xl bg-slate-950/90 border border-amber-500/30 p-2.5 space-y-1"
                >
                  <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
                    <label className="text-[10px] font-extrabold uppercase tracking-wider text-amber-400 flex items-center gap-1">
                      <PenTool className="w-3 h-3" />
                      <span>Personal Note / Mnemonic</span>
                    </label>
                    <span className="text-[9px] font-bold text-emerald-400">Auto-saved</span>
                  </div>
                  <input
                    type="text"
                    value={wordNotes[currentWord.hanzi] || wordNotes[currentWord.id] || ''}
                    onChange={(e) => onSaveWordNote?.(currentWord, e.target.value)}
                    placeholder={`Add a mnemonic or note for ${currentWord.hanzi}...`}
                    className="w-full bg-slate-900 border border-slate-700 focus:border-amber-400 rounded-xl px-3 py-1.5 text-xs font-semibold text-white placeholder:text-slate-500 outline-none"
                  />
                </div>

                <p className="text-[10px] text-slate-400 text-center">{currentWord.usageTip}</p>
              </div>

              <div className="text-center pt-2 border-t border-slate-800/80">
                <span className="text-xs font-bold text-slate-400 inline-flex items-center gap-1.5">
                  <RefreshCw className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-emerald-400" />
                  <span>Tap card to flip back to Front</span>
                </span>
              </div>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>

        <div className="flex items-center gap-2.5">
          <button
            type="button"
            disabled={cardIndex === 0}
            onClick={handlePrevCard}
            className="game-btn-3d px-4 py-3.5 rounded-xl bg-slate-800 hover:bg-slate-700 disabled:opacity-40 text-xs font-bold text-slate-200 border-b-4 border-slate-950"
          >
            Previous
          </button>

          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={handleNextCard}
            className="game-btn-3d flex-1 py-3.5 rounded-xl bg-gradient-to-r from-rose-500 to-red-600 hover:from-rose-400 hover:to-red-500 text-white font-extrabold text-xs shadow-lg border-b-4 border-rose-800 flex items-center justify-center gap-1.5"
          >
            <span>
              {cardIndex === words.length - 1
                ? `Finish Flashcards (+${45 + bonusXp} XP)`
                : 'Next Flashcard'}
            </span>
            <ChevronRight className="w-4 h-4" />
          </button>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

// ============================================================================
// STAGE 4 MODAL: UNIT BOSS BATTLE (BOSS AVATAR + HP BAR + 3 HEARTS + 15s TIMER + COMBO/SFX)
// ============================================================================
function BossChallengeModal({
  words = UNIT_1_WORDS,
  isPracticeMode = false,
  isSlowMode,
  onSetSlowMode,
  speakMandarin,
  speakingState,
  onRecordSkillEvent,
  onClose,
  onComplete
}) {
  const questions = useMemo(() => buildBossQuestionsForWords(words), [words]);
  const [questionIndex, setQuestionIndex] = useState(0);
  const [hearts, setHearts] = useState(3);
  const [bossHp, setBossHp] = useState(questions.length);
  const [timeLeft, setTimeLeft] = useState(15);
  const [selectedOptionId, setSelectedOptionId] = useState(null);
  const [isAnswered, setIsAnswered] = useState(false);
  const [correctWordIds, setCorrectWordIds] = useState([]);
  const [status, setStatus] = useState('playing'); // 'playing' | 'lost' | 'won'

  // Gamified Combo, Boss Hit Animation & Floating XP
  const [combo, setCombo] = useState(0);
  const [maxCombo, setMaxCombo] = useState(0);
  const [bonusXp, setBonusXp] = useState(0);
  const [xpPopupKey, setXpPopupKey] = useState(0);
  const [xpPopupAmount, setXpPopupAmount] = useState(15);
  const [bossDamageAnim, setBossDamageAnim] = useState(false);
  const [playerDamageAnim, setPlayerDamageAnim] = useState(false);

  const feedbackTimerRef = useRef(null);
  const currentQuestion = questions[questionIndex] || questions[0];
  const maxBossHp = questions.length;
  const bossHpPercent = Math.max(0, Math.round((bossHp / Math.max(1, maxBossHp)) * 100));

  const clearFeedbackTimer = useCallback(() => {
    if (feedbackTimerRef.current) {
      clearTimeout(feedbackTimerRef.current);
      feedbackTimerRef.current = null;
    }
  }, []);

  useEffect(() => {
    return () => clearFeedbackTimer();
  }, [clearFeedbackTimer]);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (status === 'playing' && currentQuestion) {
      speakMandarin(currentQuestion.speechText);
    }
  }, [questionIndex, status, currentQuestion, speakMandarin]);

  const scheduleBossTurnAdvance = useCallback(
    (delayMs, nextHeartsValue) => {
      clearFeedbackTimer();
      feedbackTimerRef.current = setTimeout(() => {
        if (nextHeartsValue <= 0) {
          setStatus('lost');
          return;
        }
        if (questionIndex < questions.length - 1) {
          setQuestionIndex((prev) => prev + 1);
          setSelectedOptionId(null);
          setIsAnswered(false);
          setTimeLeft(15);
        } else {
          setStatus('won');
        }
      }, delayMs);
    },
    [clearFeedbackTimer, questionIndex, questions.length]
  );

  useEffect(() => {
    if (status !== 'playing' || isAnswered) return;

    if (timeLeft <= 0) {
      soundFX.playMistake();
      setCombo(0);
      setPlayerDamageAnim(true);
      setTimeout(() => setPlayerDamageAnim(false), 500);
      setIsAnswered(true);
      setSelectedOptionId('timeout');
      const nextHearts = hearts - 1;
      setHearts(nextHearts);
      scheduleBossTurnAdvance(1800, nextHearts);
      return;
    }

    const timerId = setTimeout(() => {
      setTimeLeft((prev) => prev - 1);
    }, 1000);

    return () => clearTimeout(timerId);
  }, [timeLeft, status, isAnswered, hearts, scheduleBossTurnAdvance]);

  const handleSelectOption = (option) => {
    if (isAnswered || status !== 'playing') return;

    setSelectedOptionId(option.id);
    setIsAnswered(true);

    if (option.isCorrect) {
      speakMandarin(currentQuestion.speechText, 1.0);
      setBossHp((prev) => Math.max(0, prev - 1));
      setBossDamageAnim(true);
      setTimeout(() => setBossDamageAnim(false), 550);
      onRecordSkillEvent?.(
        { id: currentQuestion.wordId, hanzi: currentQuestion.speechText },
        'recognition',
        { mistakesDelta: 0, completionsDelta: 1 }
      );

      setCombo((prev) => {
        const nextCombo = prev + 1;
        setMaxCombo((m) => Math.max(m, nextCombo));
        soundFX.playCorrect(nextCombo);
        const earned = 15 + (nextCombo >= 2 ? nextCombo * 5 : 0);
        setBonusXp((b) => b + earned);
        setXpPopupAmount(earned);
        setXpPopupKey((k) => k + 1);
        return nextCombo;
      });

      setCorrectWordIds((prev) =>
        prev.includes(currentQuestion.wordId) ? prev : [...prev, currentQuestion.wordId]
      );
      scheduleBossTurnAdvance(1400, hearts);
    } else {
      soundFX.playMistake();
      setCombo(0);
      setPlayerDamageAnim(true);
      setTimeout(() => setPlayerDamageAnim(false), 500);
      onRecordSkillEvent?.(
        { id: currentQuestion.wordId, hanzi: currentQuestion.speechText },
        'recognition',
        { mistakesDelta: 1, completionsDelta: 0 }
      );

      const nextHearts = hearts - 1;
      setHearts(nextHearts);
      scheduleBossTurnAdvance(1800, nextHearts);
    }
  };

  const handleRetryBoss = () => {
    clearFeedbackTimer();
    soundFX.playPop();
    setQuestionIndex(0);
    setHearts(3);
    setBossHp(questions.length);
    setCombo(0);
    setBonusXp(0);
    setTimeLeft(15);
    setSelectedOptionId(null);
    setIsAnswered(false);
    setCorrectWordIds([]);
    setStatus('playing');
  };

  const correctOptionObj = currentQuestion?.options?.find((o) => o.isCorrect);
  const selectedWasCorrect = currentQuestion?.options?.find(
    (o) => o.id === selectedOptionId
  )?.isCorrect;

  return (
    <div className="fixed inset-0 z-50 bg-slate-950/95 backdrop-blur-md flex justify-center">
      <div className="w-full max-w-md bg-slate-900 min-h-screen flex flex-col justify-between p-4 overflow-y-auto">
        <div>
          <SessionGameTopHud
            title={isPracticeMode ? 'Practice Arena Quiz' : 'Stage 4 • Boss Battle'}
            subtitle={`Turn ${questionIndex + 1} of ${questions.length}`}
            totalSegments={questions.length}
            completedSegments={questionIndex + (isAnswered ? 1 : 0)}
            combo={combo}
            xpPopupKey={xpPopupKey}
            xpPopupAmount={xpPopupAmount}
            onClose={() => {
              clearFeedbackTimer();
              onClose();
            }}
          />

          {/* BOSS AVATAR & HP BAR VS PLAYER HEARTS & 15s TIMER */}
          <div className="mt-3 rounded-2xl bg-slate-950/90 border border-purple-500/40 p-3 space-y-2.5 shadow-lg">
            {/* Boss Row */}
            <div className="flex items-center justify-between gap-3">
              <div className="flex items-center gap-2.5 min-w-0">
                <div
                  className={`w-11 h-11 rounded-2xl flex items-center justify-center text-xl font-black border shadow-md shrink-0 transition-transform ${
                    bossDamageAnim
                      ? 'bg-rose-600 border-rose-300 text-white animate-game-shake scale-110'
                      : 'bg-gradient-to-br from-purple-600 to-indigo-900 border-purple-400/50 text-amber-300'
                  }`}
                >
                  龙
                </div>
                <div className="min-w-0">
                  <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5">
                    <span className="text-xs font-black text-white truncate">
                      Jade Dragon Guardian
                    </span>
                    {bossDamageAnim && (
                      <span className="text-[10px] font-black text-rose-400 animate-bounce">
                        -1 HP!
                      </span>
                    )}
                  </div>
                  <div className="w-36 h-2.5 bg-slate-800 rounded-full overflow-hidden border border-slate-700 mt-1">
                    <div
                      className="h-full bg-gradient-to-r from-rose-500 via-amber-500 to-purple-500 transition-all duration-500"
                      style={{ width: `${bossHpPercent}%` }}
                    />
                  </div>
                </div>
              </div>

              <div className="text-right shrink-0">
                <span className="text-[10px] font-black uppercase text-purple-300 block">
                  Boss HP
                </span>
                <span className="text-xs font-black text-white">
                  {bossHp} / {maxBossHp}
                </span>
              </div>
            </div>

            {/* Player Hearts & 15s Turn Timer Bar */}
            <div className="flex items-center justify-between pt-2 border-t border-slate-800/90">
              <div
                className={`flex items-center gap-1.5 px-2.5 py-1 rounded-full bg-rose-500/15 border border-rose-500/30 ${
                  playerDamageAnim ? 'animate-game-shake bg-rose-600/40' : ''
                }`}
              >
                <Shield className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-rose-400" />
                {[1, 2, 3].map((heartIdx) => (
                  <Heart
                    key={heartIdx}
                    className={`w-4 h-4 transition-all ${
                      heartIdx <= hearts
                        ? 'fill-rose-500 text-rose-500 scale-100'
                        : 'text-slate-700 scale-90'
                    }`}
                  />
                ))}
              </div>

              <div
                className={`flex items-center gap-1 px-3 py-1 rounded-full text-xs font-black border ${
                  timeLeft <= 5
                    ? 'bg-rose-600/30 border-rose-400 text-rose-200 animate-pulse'
                    : 'bg-slate-800 border-slate-700 text-amber-300'
                }`}
              >
                <Clock className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
                <span>{timeLeft}s</span>
              </div>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>

        {status === 'playing' && (
          <div className="my-auto space-y-4 py-3">
            <div
              className={`rounded-3xl bg-gradient-to-b from-purple-950/60 to-slate-800/90 border border-purple-500/40 p-5 text-center space-y-2 shadow-xl ${
                playerDamageAnim ? 'animate-game-shake border-rose-500' : ''
              }`}
            >
              <div className="flex items-center justify-between gap-2">
                <span className="text-[10px] font-extrabold uppercase tracking-wider px-2 py-0.5 rounded bg-purple-500/20 text-purple-300 border border-purple-400/30">
                  Attack Turn {questionIndex + 1}/{questions.length}
                </span>
                <MandarinAudioControls
                  text={currentQuestion.speechText}
                  isSlowMode={isSlowMode}
                  onSetSlowMode={onSetSlowMode}
                  speakMandarin={speakMandarin}
                  speakingState={speakingState}
                  size="sm"
                />
              </div>

              <p className="text-xs font-bold text-slate-300 pt-1">{currentQuestion.prompt}</p>
              <div className="text-3xl font-black text-white py-1">
                {currentQuestion.focusHanzi}
              </div>
              <div className="text-xs font-semibold text-rose-300">
                {currentQuestion.focusPinyin}
              </div>
            </div>

            <div className="space-y-2.5">
              {currentQuestion.options.map((opt) => {
                const isSelected = selectedOptionId === opt.id;
                let btnStyle =
                  'bg-slate-800/90 hover:bg-slate-800 border-slate-700 text-slate-100';

                if (isAnswered) {
                  if (opt.isCorrect) {
                    btnStyle =
                      'bg-emerald-500/20 border-emerald-400 text-emerald-200 font-extrabold animate-game-green-pulse';
                  } else if (isSelected && !opt.isCorrect) {
                    btnStyle =
                      'bg-rose-500/20 border-rose-500 text-rose-200 animate-game-shake';
                  } else {
                    btnStyle = 'bg-slate-900 border-slate-800 text-slate-500 opacity-60';
                  }
                }

                return (
                  <button
                    key={opt.id}
                    type="button"
                    disabled={isAnswered}
                    onClick={() => handleSelectOption(opt)}
                    className={`game-btn-3d w-full p-3.5 rounded-2xl border text-left text-xs font-bold flex items-center justify-between transition-all ${btnStyle}`}
                  >
                    <span>{opt.text}</span>
                    {isAnswered && opt.isCorrect && (
                      <Check className="w-4 h-4 text-emerald-400 shrink-0" />
                    )}
                    {isAnswered && isSelected && !opt.isCorrect && (
                      <X className="w-4 h-4 text-rose-400 shrink-0" />
                    )}
                  </button>
                );
              })}
            </div>

            {isAnswered && (
              <div
                className={`rounded-2xl p-3 text-xs border ${
                  selectedWasCorrect
                    ? 'bg-emerald-500/15 border-emerald-400/50 text-emerald-200'
                    : 'bg-rose-500/15 border-rose-400/50 text-rose-200'
                }`}
              >
                <p className="font-extrabold">
                  {selectedOptionId === 'timeout'
                    ? `⏰ Time ran out! — Correct answer: ${correctOptionObj?.text}`
                    : selectedWasCorrect
                    ? '✓ Correct! Direct hit on the Boss (-1 HP)'
                    : `✗ Incorrect — Correct answer: ${correctOptionObj?.text}`}
                </p>
                <p className="text-[11px] opacity-90 mt-0.5">{currentQuestion.explanation}</p>
              </div>
            )}
          </div>
        )}

        {status === 'lost' && (
          <div className="my-auto rounded-3xl bg-slate-800/90 border border-rose-500/40 p-6 text-center space-y-4 animate-game-shake">
            <ShieldAlert className="w-12 h-12 text-rose-400 mx-auto" />
            <h2 className="text-xl font-black text-white">Out of Hearts!</h2>
            <p className="text-xs text-slate-300">
              The Jade Dragon Guardian held strong with {bossHp} HP left. Regroup and challenge the boss again!
            </p>
            <div className="flex gap-2.5 pt-2">
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={() => {
                  clearFeedbackTimer();
                  onClose();
                }}
                className="game-btn-3d flex-1 py-3 rounded-xl bg-slate-900 border border-slate-700 text-xs font-bold text-slate-300"
              >
                Return to Map
              </button>
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={handleRetryBoss}
                className="game-btn-3d flex-1 py-3 rounded-xl bg-rose-600 hover:bg-rose-500 text-xs font-extrabold text-white border-b-4 border-rose-800"
              >
                Try Again (3 Hearts)
              </button>
            </div>
          </div>
        )}

        {status === 'won' && (
          <div className="my-auto rounded-3xl bg-gradient-to-b from-purple-900/50 to-slate-800 border border-amber-400/50 p-6 text-center space-y-4 animate-game-green-pulse">
            <Trophy className="w-12 h-12 text-amber-400 mx-auto" />
            <h2 className="text-xl font-black text-white">Guardian Boss Defeated!</h2>
            <p className="text-xs text-slate-300">
              You depleted the Boss HP to 0 with{' '}
              <span className="text-rose-400 font-bold">{hearts} heart(s)</span> remaining and a{' '}
              <span className="text-amber-300 font-bold">{maxCombo}x max combo</span>!
            </p>
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={() => onComplete(correctWordIds, hearts, bonusXp, maxCombo)}
              className="game-btn-3d w-full py-3.5 rounded-xl bg-gradient-to-r from-amber-500 to-rose-600 text-white font-extrabold text-xs shadow-lg border-b-4 border-rose-800"
            >
              Claim Victory Rewards & Bonus XP
            </button>
          </div>
        )}

        {status === 'playing' && (
          <div className="pt-1 text-center text-[11px] font-bold text-slate-400">
            {isAnswered
              ? 'Auto-advancing to next turn...'
              : 'Select an answer before the 15s timer expires!'}
          </div>
        )}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

// ============================================================================
// 60-SECOND BLITZ MATCH MINI-GAME MODAL (4 HANZI VS 4 SHUFFLED MEANING/PINYIN CARDS)
// ============================================================================
const SUPPLEMENTAL_BLITZ_WORDS = [
  ...UNIT_1_WORDS,
  normalizeWordEntry({
    id: 'blitz_pengyou',
    hanzi: '朋友',
    pinyin: 'péngyou',
    meaning: 'Friend / Companion'
  })
];

function BlitzMatchModal({ words = UNIT_1_WORDS, speakMandarin, onClose, onComplete }) {
  const [timeLeft, setTimeLeft] = useState(60);
  const [timeDeltaBanner, setTimeDeltaBanner] = useState(null); // { id, text: '+2s' | '-2s', positive: boolean }
  const [combo, setCombo] = useState(0);
  const [maxCombo, setMaxCombo] = useState(0);
  const [scoreXp, setScoreXp] = useState(0);
  const [totalPairsMatched, setTotalPairsMatched] = useState(0);
  const [matchedWordsHistory, setMatchedWordsHistory] = useState([]);
  const [gameEnded, setGameEnded] = useState(false);

  const [leftCards, setLeftCards] = useState([]);
  const [rightCards, setRightCards] = useState([]);
  const [clearedPairIds, setClearedPairIds] = useState([]);
  const [selectedLeftId, setSelectedLeftId] = useState(null);
  const [selectedRightId, setSelectedRightId] = useState(null);
  const [mismatchPair, setMismatchPair] = useState(null); // { leftId, rightId }

  // Ensure we always have at least 4 distinct vocabulary items for the 4x4 board
  const fullPool = useMemo(() => {
    const map = new window.Map();
    words.forEach((w) => {
      if (w?.hanzi) map.set(w.hanzi, w);
    });
    SUPPLEMENTAL_BLITZ_WORDS.forEach((w) => {
      if (w?.hanzi && !map.has(w.hanzi)) map.set(w.hanzi, w);
    });
    return Array.from(map.values());
  }, [words]);

  const dealFourPairsBoard = useCallback(() => {
    const shuffledPool = [...fullPool].sort(() => Math.random() - 0.5);
    const chosenFour = shuffledPool.slice(0, 4);

    const left = chosenFour
      .map((w, idx) => ({
        pairId: `pair_${w.hanzi}_${idx}`,
        word: w,
        hanzi: w.hanzi
      }))
      .sort(() => Math.random() - 0.5);

    const right = left
      .map((item) => ({
        pairId: item.pairId,
        word: item.word,
        pinyin: item.word.pinyin,
        meaningShort: (item.word.meaning || item.word.english || '').split(/[;/]/)[0].trim()
      }))
      .sort(() => Math.random() - 0.5);

    setLeftCards(left);
    setRightCards(right);
    setClearedPairIds([]);
    setSelectedLeftId(null);
    setSelectedRightId(null);
    setMismatchPair(null);
  }, [fullPool]);

  useEffect(() => {
    dealFourPairsBoard();
  }, [dealFourPairsBoard]);

  // 60-Second Countdown Timer
  useEffect(() => {
    if (gameEnded) return;
    if (timeLeft <= 0) {
      setGameEnded(true);
      soundFX.playVictory();
      return;
    }
    const timer = setTimeout(() => {
      setTimeLeft((prev) => Math.max(0, prev - 1));
    }, 1000);
    return () => clearTimeout(timer);
  }, [timeLeft, gameEnded]);

  const evaluateSelection = useCallback(
    (leftId, rightId) => {
      if (!leftId || !rightId) return;

      if (leftId === rightId) {
        // Correct Match! +2 seconds, +XP, clear pair
        const matchedItem = leftCards.find((c) => c.pairId === leftId);
        const nextCombo = combo + 1;
        setCombo(nextCombo);
        setMaxCombo((m) => Math.max(m, nextCombo));
        soundFX.playCorrect(nextCombo);

        if (matchedItem?.word) {
          speakMandarin(matchedItem.word.hanzi);
          setMatchedWordsHistory((prev) =>
            prev.some((w) => w.hanzi === matchedItem.word.hanzi)
              ? prev
              : [...prev, matchedItem.word]
          );
        }

        const earnedXp = 15 + (nextCombo >= 3 ? 5 : 0);
        setScoreXp((x) => x + earnedXp);
        setTotalPairsMatched((c) => c + 1);
        setTimeLeft((t) => t + 2);
        setTimeDeltaBanner({ id: Date.now(), text: '+2s', positive: true });

        const nextCleared = [...clearedPairIds, leftId];
        setClearedPairIds(nextCleared);
        setSelectedLeftId(null);
        setSelectedRightId(null);

        if (nextCleared.length >= leftCards.length) {
          setTimeout(() => {
            dealFourPairsBoard();
          }, 320);
        }
      } else {
        // Wrong Match! Shake + subtract 2 seconds
        soundFX.playMistake();
        setCombo(0);
        setTimeLeft((t) => Math.max(0, t - 2));
        setTimeDeltaBanner({ id: Date.now(), text: '-2s', positive: false });
        setMismatchPair({ leftId, rightId });

        setTimeout(() => {
          setMismatchPair(null);
          setSelectedLeftId(null);
          setSelectedRightId(null);
        }, 460);
      }
    },
    [clearedPairIds, combo, dealFourPairsBoard, leftCards, speakMandarin]
  );

  const handleSelectLeft = (pairId) => {
    if (gameEnded || clearedPairIds.includes(pairId) || mismatchPair) return;
    soundFX.playPop();
    const card = leftCards.find((c) => c.pairId === pairId);
    if (card) speakMandarin(card.hanzi);
    setSelectedLeftId(pairId);
    if (selectedRightId) {
      evaluateSelection(pairId, selectedRightId);
    }
  };

  const handleSelectRight = (pairId) => {
    if (gameEnded || clearedPairIds.includes(pairId) || mismatchPair) return;
    soundFX.playPop();
    setSelectedRightId(pairId);
    if (selectedLeftId) {
      evaluateSelection(selectedLeftId, pairId);
    }
  };

  return (
    <div className="fixed inset-0 z-50 bg-slate-950/95 backdrop-blur-md flex justify-center">
      <div className="w-full max-w-md bg-slate-900 min-h-screen flex flex-col justify-between p-4 overflow-y-auto">
        {/* Blitz Top Header */}
        <div className="space-y-3 border-b border-slate-800 pb-3">
          <div className="flex items-center justify-between gap-2">
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={onClose}
              className="game-btn-3d p-2 rounded-xl bg-slate-800 hover:bg-slate-700 text-slate-300 flex items-center gap-1 text-xs font-bold"
            >
              <ArrowLeft className="w-4 h-4" />
              <span>Exit</span>
            </button>

            <div className="text-center">
              <span className="text-[11px] font-black uppercase tracking-wider text-amber-400 block">
                60s Blitz Match
              </span>
              <span className="text-[10px] text-slate-400">
                Match Pair = +2s • Miss = -2s
              </span>
            </div>

            <div className="px-2.5 py-1 rounded-full bg-amber-500/15 border border-amber-400/40 text-amber-300 text-xs font-black flex items-center gap-1">
              <Flame className="w-3.5 h-3.5 fill-amber-400" />
              <span>{combo}x</span>
            </div>
          </div>

          {/* Timer Bar & Live Score Counters */}
          <div className="rounded-2xl bg-slate-950 border border-slate-800 p-3 flex items-center justify-between">
            <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
              <div
                className={`px-3 py-1 rounded-xl font-black text-sm flex items-center gap-1.5 border ${
                  timeLeft <= 10
                    ? 'bg-rose-600/30 border-rose-400 text-rose-200 animate-pulse'
                    : 'bg-emerald-500/15 border-emerald-400/40 text-emerald-300'
                }`}
              >
                <Clock className="w-4 h-4" />
                <span>{timeLeft}s</span>
              </div>

              {timeDeltaBanner && (
                <span
                  key={timeDeltaBanner.id}
                  className={`text-xs font-black px-2 py-0.5 rounded-full animate-bounce ${
                    timeDeltaBanner.positive
                      ? 'bg-emerald-500/20 text-emerald-300 border border-emerald-400/40'
                      : 'bg-rose-500/20 text-rose-300 border border-rose-400/40'
                  }`}
                >
                  {timeDeltaBanner.text}
                </span>
              )}
            </div>

            <div className="flex items-center gap-3 text-xs font-black">
              <span className="text-slate-300">
                Pairs: <strong className="text-white">{totalPairsMatched}</strong>
              </span>
              <span className="text-amber-300">+{scoreXp} XP</span>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>

        {/* 4x4 Matching Grid (Left: 4 Hanzi Cards, Right: 4 Shuffled Meaning/Pinyin Cards) */}
        {!gameEnded ? (
          <div className="my-auto py-4">
            <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-3">
              {/* Left Column: 4 Hanzi Cards */}
              <div className="space-y-2.5">
                <span className="text-[10px] font-black uppercase tracking-wider text-rose-400 block text-center">
                  Hanzi
                </span>
                {leftCards.map((card) => {
                  const isCleared = clearedPairIds.includes(card.pairId);
                  const isSelected = selectedLeftId === card.pairId;
                  const isWrong = mismatchPair?.leftId === card.pairId;

                  return (
                    <button
                      key={`left_${card.pairId}`}
                      type="button"
                      disabled={isCleared}
                      onClick={() => handleSelectLeft(card.pairId)}
                      className={`game-btn-3d w-full h-24 rounded-2xl border-2 font-black text-2xl flex items-center justify-center transition-all ${
                        isCleared
                          ? 'bg-emerald-500/10 border-emerald-500/20 text-emerald-400/30 opacity-30 scale-95'
                          : isWrong
                          ? 'bg-rose-600/30 border-rose-500 text-rose-200 animate-game-shake'
                          : isSelected
                          ? 'bg-rose-500/25 border-rose-400 text-white scale-[1.03] shadow-lg shadow-rose-950/60'
                          : 'bg-slate-800/90 hover:bg-slate-800 border-slate-700 text-white'
                      }`}
                    >
                      {isCleared ? <Check className="w-6 h-6 text-emerald-400" /> : card.hanzi}
                    </button>
                  );
                })}
              </div>

              {/* Right Column: 4 Shuffled Meaning / Pinyin Cards */}
              <div className="space-y-2.5">
                <span className="text-[10px] font-black uppercase tracking-wider text-sky-400 block text-center">
                  Meaning & Pinyin
                </span>
                {rightCards.map((card) => {
                  const isCleared = clearedPairIds.includes(card.pairId);
                  const isSelected = selectedRightId === card.pairId;
                  const isWrong = mismatchPair?.rightId === card.pairId;

                  return (
                    <button
                      key={`right_${card.pairId}`}
                      type="button"
                      disabled={isCleared}
                      onClick={() => handleSelectRight(card.pairId)}
                      className={`game-btn-3d w-full h-24 rounded-2xl border-2 p-2.5 flex flex-col items-center justify-center text-center transition-all ${
                        isCleared
                          ? 'bg-emerald-500/10 border-emerald-500/20 text-emerald-400/30 opacity-30 scale-95'
                          : isWrong
                          ? 'bg-rose-600/30 border-rose-500 text-rose-200 animate-game-shake'
                          : isSelected
                          ? 'bg-sky-500/25 border-sky-400 text-white scale-[1.03] shadow-lg shadow-sky-950/60'
                          : 'bg-slate-800/90 hover:bg-slate-800 border-slate-700 text-slate-100'
                      }`}
                    >
                      {isCleared ? (
                        <Check className="w-6 h-6 text-emerald-400" />
                      ) : (
                        <>
                          <span className="text-xs font-extrabold text-rose-300">
                            {card.pinyin}
                          </span>
                          <span className="text-xs font-bold text-white line-clamp-2 mt-0.5">
                            {card.meaningShort}
                          </span>
                        </>
                      )}
                    </button>
                  );
                })}
              </div>
            </div>
          </div>
        ) : (
          <div className="my-auto rounded-3xl bg-gradient-to-b from-amber-900/40 to-slate-800 border border-amber-400/50 p-6 text-center space-y-4 animate-game-green-pulse">
            <Trophy className="w-12 h-12 text-amber-400 mx-auto" />
            <h2 className="text-xl font-black text-white">60s Blitz Complete!</h2>
            <p className="text-xs text-slate-300">
              You matched <strong className="text-emerald-400">{totalPairsMatched} pairs</strong> with a{' '}
              <strong className="text-amber-300">{maxCombo}x max combo</strong>!
            </p>
            <div className="flex gap-2.5 pt-2">
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={() => {
                  setTimeLeft(60);
                  setCombo(0);
                  setScoreXp(0);
                  setTotalPairsMatched(0);
                  setGameEnded(false);
                  dealFourPairsBoard();
                }}
                className="game-btn-3d flex-1 py-3 rounded-xl bg-slate-900 border border-slate-700 text-xs font-bold text-slate-200"
              >
                Play Again
              </button>
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={() =>
                  onComplete(matchedWordsHistory, Math.max(40, scoreXp), maxCombo)
                }
                className="game-btn-3d flex-1 py-3 rounded-xl bg-gradient-to-r from-amber-500 to-rose-600 text-white font-black text-xs border-b-4 border-rose-800"
              >
                Claim +{Math.max(40, scoreXp)} XP
              </button>
            </div>
          </div>
        )}

        {!gameEnded && (
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={() => setGameEnded(true)}
            className="game-btn-3d w-full py-3 rounded-xl bg-slate-800 hover:bg-slate-700 border border-slate-700 text-xs font-bold text-slate-300"
          >
            Finish Blitz & Claim XP ({scoreXp} XP)
          </button>
        )}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

// ============================================================================
// TAB 2: SMART REVIEW ARENA (ADAPTIVE SPACED-REPETITION & WEAKEST MODALITY HUB)
// ============================================================================
function SmartReviewArenaView({
  progress,
  courses,
  activeCourse,
  activeLesson,
  activeWords,
  customDecks,
  unlockedWordsCount = 3,
  speakMandarin,
  speakingState,
  onLaunchSmartReviewSession,
  onLaunchSentenceBuilderDrill,
  onLaunchClozeDrill,
  onLaunchToneSandhiDrill,
  onLaunchHskMockExam,
  onLaunchBlitzMatch,
  onLaunchActivity,
  triggerToast
}) {
  const [reviewFilter, setReviewFilter] = useState('all_weak'); // 'all_weak' | 'writing_audio'

  const isSentenceBuilderUnlocked =
    unlockedWordsCount >= ADVANCED_MODE_UNLOCK_THRESHOLDS.sentence_builder;
  const isToneDrillUnlocked =
    unlockedWordsCount >= ADVANCED_MODE_UNLOCK_THRESHOLDS.tone_sandhi_drill;
  const isClozeUnlocked =
    unlockedWordsCount >= ADVANCED_MODE_UNLOCK_THRESHOLDS.cloze;
  const isBlitzUnlocked =
    unlockedWordsCount >= ADVANCED_MODE_UNLOCK_THRESHOLDS.blitz_match;
  const isHskMockUnlocked =
    unlockedWordsCount >= ADVANCED_MODE_UNLOCK_THRESHOLDS.hsk_mock_exam;

  // Scan all lessons in the active course (and custom decks) that the user has started or unlocked
  const scannedPool = useMemo(() => {
    const wordMap = new window.Map();

    // Always include activeLesson words
    activeWords.forEach((w, i) => {
      const norm = normalizeWordEntry(w, i);
      if (norm.hanzi) wordMap.set(norm.hanzi, norm);
    });

    // Scan all lessons in activeCourse that are started/unlocked
    (activeCourse?.lessons || []).forEach((lesson, lIdx) => {
      const lessonKey = `${activeCourse.id}__${lesson.id}`;
      const lp = progress.lessonProgress?.[lessonKey];
      const isStartedOrUnlocked =
        lIdx === 0 ||
        lesson.id === activeLesson?.id ||
        (lp && (lp.unlockedStage > 1 || (lp.completedStages && lp.completedStages.length > 0)));

      if (isStartedOrUnlocked) {
        (lesson.words || []).forEach((w, wIdx) => {
          const norm = normalizeWordEntry(w, wIdx);
          if (norm.hanzi && !wordMap.has(norm.hanzi)) {
            wordMap.set(norm.hanzi, norm);
          }
        });
      }
    });

    // Also include custom decks words
    customDecks.forEach((deck) => {
      (deck.words || []).forEach((w, wIdx) => {
        const norm = normalizeWordEntry(w, wIdx);
        if (norm.hanzi && !wordMap.has(norm.hanzi)) {
          wordMap.set(norm.hanzi, norm);
        }
      });
    });

    return Array.from(wordMap.values());
  }, [activeCourse, activeLesson, activeWords, customDecks, progress.lessonProgress]);

  // Compute Words to Review (Lv 0-2), Consolidating (Lv 3), Mastered (Lv 4-5), and Modality Mistake Totals
  const reviewAnalytics = useMemo(() => {
    const wordsToReview = []; // Lv 0-2
    const consolidatingWords = []; // Lv 3
    const masteredWords = []; // Lv 4-5

    const modalitySummary = {
      recognition: { key: 'recognition', label: 'Recognition', mistakes: 0, completions: 0 },
      writing: { key: 'writing', label: 'Writing (Strokes)', mistakes: 0, completions: 0 },
      listening: { key: 'listening', label: 'Listening (Dictation)', mistakes: 0, completions: 0 },
      speaking: { key: 'speaking', label: 'Speaking (Tones)', mistakes: 0, completions: 0 },
      sentence_builder: {
        key: 'sentence_builder',
        label: 'Sentence Builder',
        mistakes: 0,
        completions: 0
      },
      cloze: {
        key: 'cloze',
        label: 'Fill-in-the-Blank (Cloze)',
        mistakes: 0,
        completions: 0
      }
    };

    const enrichedWords = scannedPool.map((word) => {
      const mastery = getWordMasteryLevel(progress.mastery, word);
      const telemetry = getWordSkillTelemetry(progress.skillTelemetry, word);

      ['recognition', 'writing', 'listening', 'speaking', 'sentence_builder', 'cloze'].forEach((mKey) => {
        modalitySummary[mKey].mistakes += telemetry[mKey].mistakes;
        modalitySummary[mKey].completions += telemetry[mKey].completions;
      });

      // Rank each modality by highest mistakes, then fewest completions
      const rankModalities = (allowedKeys) => {
        return [...allowedKeys].sort((a, b) => {
          const mDiff = telemetry[b].mistakes - telemetry[a].mistakes;
          if (mDiff !== 0) return mDiff;
          return telemetry[a].completions - telemetry[b].completions;
        });
      };

      const unlockedDynamicModalities = [
        'writing',
        'listening',
        ...(isClozeUnlocked ? ['cloze'] : []),
        ...(isSentenceBuilderUnlocked ? ['sentence_builder'] : []),
        'speaking',
        'recognition'
      ];

      const weakestAllModality = rankModalities(unlockedDynamicModalities)[0];
      const weakestWritingAudioModality = rankModalities([
        'writing',
        'listening',
        'speaking',
        ...(isClozeUnlocked ? ['cloze'] : []),
        ...(isSentenceBuilderUnlocked ? ['sentence_builder'] : [])
      ])[0];

      const totalMistakes =
        telemetry.recognition.mistakes +
        telemetry.writing.mistakes +
        telemetry.listening.mistakes +
        telemetry.speaking.mistakes +
        telemetry.sentence_builder.mistakes +
        telemetry.cloze.mistakes;

      const enriched = {
        word,
        mastery,
        telemetry,
        totalMistakes,
        weakestAllModality,
        weakestWritingAudioModality
      };

      if (mastery <= 2) wordsToReview.push(enriched);
      else if (mastery === 3) consolidatingWords.push(enriched);
      else masteredWords.push(enriched);

      return enriched;
    });

    // Determine overall weakest modality across the learner's pool
    const sortedModalities = Object.values(modalitySummary).sort((a, b) => {
      if (b.mistakes !== a.mistakes) return b.mistakes - a.mistakes;
      return a.completions - b.completions;
    });

    const weakestGlobalModality = sortedModalities[0];

    return {
      enrichedWords,
      wordsToReview,
      consolidatingWords,
      masteredWords,
      modalitySummary,
      sortedModalities,
      weakestGlobalModality
    };
  }, [scannedPool, progress.mastery, progress.skillTelemetry]);

  // Build Adaptive 10-Step Smart Review Queue prioritized by lowest mastery (0 -> 1 -> 2 -> 3)
  // and testing each word in its weakest / least-completed modality
  const adaptiveSmartQueue = useMemo(() => {
    const allowedModes =
      reviewFilter === 'writing_audio'
        ? [
            'writing',
            'listening',
            'speaking',
            ...(isClozeUnlocked ? ['cloze'] : []),
            ...(isSentenceBuilderUnlocked ? ['sentence_builder'] : [])
          ]
        : [
            'recognition',
            'writing',
            'listening',
            'speaking',
            ...(isClozeUnlocked ? ['cloze'] : []),
            ...(isSentenceBuilderUnlocked ? ['sentence_builder'] : [])
          ];

    // Sort candidates: lowest mastery first (0 -> 1 -> 2 -> 3), then highest mistakes in allowed modes
    const sortedCandidates = [...reviewAnalytics.enrichedWords].sort((a, b) => {
      if (a.mastery !== b.mastery) return a.mastery - b.mastery;
      const aMistakes = allowedModes.reduce((sum, k) => sum + a.telemetry[k].mistakes, 0);
      const bMistakes = allowedModes.reduce((sum, k) => sum + b.telemetry[k].mistakes, 0);
      return bMistakes - aMistakes;
    });

    // Build up to 10 adaptive review items (cycling through prioritized weak words if pool < 10)
    const targetCount = Math.min(10, Math.max(sortedCandidates.length * 2, 6));
    const queue = [];

    for (let i = 0; i < targetCount; i++) {
      const candidate = sortedCandidates[i % sortedCandidates.length];
      if (!candidate) break;

      // Rank candidate's modalities by highest mistakes -> fewest completions
      const rankedModes = [...allowedModes].sort((mA, mB) => {
        const diffMistakes = candidate.telemetry[mB].mistakes - candidate.telemetry[mA].mistakes;
        if (diffMistakes !== 0) return diffMistakes;
        return candidate.telemetry[mA].completions - candidate.telemetry[mB].completions;
      });

      const prev1 = queue.length >= 1 ? queue[queue.length - 1].modality : null;
      const prev2 = queue.length >= 2 ? queue[queue.length - 2].modality : null;
      const lastModeForSameWord =
        queue
          .slice()
          .reverse()
          .find((q) => q.word.hanzi === candidate.word.hanzi)?.modality || null;

      let chosenMode =
        rankedModes.find(
          (m) => !(m === prev1 && m === prev2) && m !== lastModeForSameWord
        ) ||
        rankedModes.find((m) => !(m === prev1 && m === prev2)) ||
        rankedModes[0];

      const modeTelemetry = candidate.telemetry[chosenMode];
      const reasonText =
        modeTelemetry.mistakes > 0
          ? `Targeting ${modeTelemetry.mistakes} past mistake${
              modeTelemetry.mistakes > 1 ? 's' : ''
            } in ${MODALITY_META[chosenMode]?.label}`
          : `Low completions (${modeTelemetry.completions}) • Mastery Lv.${candidate.mastery}/5`;

      queue.push({
        stepId: `smart_${candidate.word.id}_${chosenMode}_${i}`,
        word: candidate.word,
        modality: chosenMode,
        mastery: candidate.mastery,
        reason: reasonText
      });
    }

    return queue;
  }, [reviewAnalytics.enrichedWords, reviewFilter]);

  return (
    <div className="p-4 space-y-4">
      {/* SMART REVIEW OVERVIEW DASHBOARD */}
      <div className="rounded-3xl bg-gradient-to-br from-slate-800 via-slate-900 to-slate-950 border border-rose-500/30 p-4 space-y-3.5 shadow-xl">
        <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
          <span className="text-[10px] font-black uppercase tracking-wider px-2.5 py-1 rounded-full bg-rose-500/20 text-rose-300 border border-rose-500/30 flex items-center gap-1">
            <Sparkles className="w-3 h-3" />
            <span>Adaptive Spaced Repetition</span>
          </span>
          <span className="text-[11px] font-bold text-amber-300">
            {unlockedWordsCount} Unlocked Words
          </span>
        </div>

        <div>
          <h1 className="text-lg font-black text-white">Smart Review Arena</h1>
          <p className="text-xs text-slate-300 mt-0.5">
            Automatically prioritizes your lowest-mastery characters and unlocks advanced workbook modes as your unlocked vocabulary grows.
          </p>
        </div>

        {/* 1. Count of Words to Review (Lv 0-2) vs Consolidating (Lv 3) vs Mastered (Lv 4-5) */}
        <div className="grid grid-cols-3 gap-2">
          <div className="rounded-2xl bg-rose-500/15 border border-rose-500/40 p-3 text-center">
            <div className="text-2xl font-black text-rose-300">
              {reviewAnalytics.wordsToReview.length}
            </div>
            <div className="text-[10px] font-black uppercase text-rose-200 mt-0.5">
              Words to Review
            </div>
            <div className="text-[9px] text-rose-300/80">Mastery Lv. 0–2</div>
          </div>

          <div className="rounded-2xl bg-amber-500/15 border border-amber-500/40 p-3 text-center">
            <div className="text-2xl font-black text-amber-300">
              {reviewAnalytics.consolidatingWords.length}
            </div>
            <div className="text-[10px] font-black uppercase text-amber-200 mt-0.5">
              Consolidating
            </div>
            <div className="text-[9px] text-amber-300/80">Mastery Lv. 3</div>
          </div>

          <div className="rounded-2xl bg-emerald-500/15 border border-emerald-500/40 p-3 text-center">
            <div className="text-2xl font-black text-emerald-300">
              {reviewAnalytics.masteredWords.length}
            </div>
            <div className="text-[10px] font-black uppercase text-emerald-200 mt-0.5">
              Mastered
            </div>
            <div className="text-[9px] text-emerald-300/80">Mastery Lv. 4–5</div>
          </div>
        </div>

        {/* 2. Visual Weakest Modality Indicator */}
        <div className="rounded-2xl bg-slate-950/90 border border-slate-800 p-3.5 space-y-2.5">
          <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
            <span className="text-[10px] font-black uppercase tracking-wider text-amber-400 flex items-center gap-1">
              <Target className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
              <span>Weakest Modality Indicator</span>
            </span>
            <span className="text-[11px] font-black text-rose-300">
              Focus: {reviewAnalytics.weakestGlobalModality?.label}
            </span>
          </div>

          <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-2">
            {reviewAnalytics.sortedModalities.map((mod, idx) => {
              const isWeakest = idx === 0;
              return (
                <div
                  key={mod.key}
                  className={`rounded-xl p-2.5 border ${
                    isWeakest
                      ? 'bg-rose-500/15 border-rose-500/50'
                      : 'bg-slate-900/90 border-slate-800'
                  }`}
                >
                  <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
                    <span className="text-[11px] font-extrabold text-white truncate">
                      {mod.label}
                    </span>
                    {isWeakest && (
                      <span className="text-[9px] font-black uppercase px-1.5 py-0.5 rounded bg-rose-500 text-white">
                        Weakest
                      </span>
                    )}
                  </div>
                  <div className="flex items-center justify-between text-[10px] text-slate-400 mt-1">
                    <span className="text-rose-300 font-bold">{mod.mistakes} mistakes</span>
                    <span className="text-emerald-300 font-bold">{mod.completions} done</span>
                  </div>
                </div>
              );
            })}
          </div>
        </div>

        {/* Quick Filter Toggle: All Weak Words vs Struggling Writing/Audio */}
        <div className="space-y-1.5">
          <span className="text-[10px] font-extrabold uppercase tracking-wider text-slate-400 block">
            Smart Review Filter Mode
          </span>
          <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-1.5 p-1 rounded-2xl bg-slate-950 border border-slate-800">
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={() => {
                soundFX.playPop();
                setReviewFilter('all_weak');
              }}
              className={`game-btn-3d py-2 px-2 rounded-xl text-xs font-extrabold transition-all ${
                reviewFilter === 'all_weak'
                  ? 'bg-rose-600 text-white shadow'
                  : 'text-slate-400 hover:text-slate-200'
              }`}
            >
              All Weak Words
            </button>
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={() => {
                soundFX.playPop();
                setReviewFilter('writing_audio');
              }}
              className={`game-btn-3d py-2 px-2 rounded-xl text-xs font-extrabold transition-all ${
                reviewFilter === 'writing_audio'
                  ? 'bg-amber-500 text-slate-950 shadow'
                  : 'text-slate-400 hover:text-slate-200'
              }`}
            >
              Struggling Writing/Audio
            </button>
          </div>
        </div>

        {/* Primary Adaptive Session Launcher Button */}
        <button
          type="button"
          onClick={() =>
            onLaunchSmartReviewSession(
              adaptiveSmartQueue,
              reviewFilter === 'writing_audio'
                ? 'Struggling Writing/Audio'
                : 'All Weak Words'
            )
          }
          className="game-btn-3d w-full py-4 rounded-2xl bg-gradient-to-r from-rose-500 via-amber-500 to-emerald-500 text-slate-950 font-black text-sm shadow-xl border-b-4 border-amber-700 flex items-center justify-center gap-2"
        >
          <Zap className="w-4 h-4 fill-slate-950" />
          <span>Start Smart Review ({adaptiveSmartQueue.length} Words)</span>
        </button>

        {/* Progressive Mode Unlocking Header */}
        <div className="flex items-center justify-between pt-2 border-t border-slate-800">
          <span className="text-[10px] font-extrabold uppercase tracking-wider text-slate-400">
            Progressive Practice Modes
          </span>
          <span className="text-[10px] font-bold text-amber-300">
            Milestones: 12 • 25 • 40 Words
          </span>
        </div>

        {/* Workbook Practice Cards with Progressive Word-Count Milestones */}
        <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-2.5">
          {/* 12+ Words: Sentence Builder */}
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={() => {
              if (!isSentenceBuilderUnlocked) {
                soundFX.playMistake();
                triggerToast?.(
                  'Mode Locked (12+ Words Required)',
                  `Unlocks at 12 words — ${unlockedWordsCount}/12 unlocked. Complete Lesson 1 or import more words!`
                );
                return;
              }
              onLaunchSentenceBuilderDrill();
            }}
            className={`game-btn-3d p-3 rounded-2xl border text-left flex flex-col justify-between transition-all ${
              isSentenceBuilderUnlocked
                ? 'bg-gradient-to-br from-purple-500/20 to-indigo-500/20 hover:from-purple-500/30 hover:to-indigo-500/30 border-purple-400/50'
                : 'bg-slate-900/75 border-slate-800 opacity-75'
            }`}
          >
            <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
              <span
                className={`text-[10px] font-black uppercase ${
                  isSentenceBuilderUnlocked ? 'text-purple-300' : 'text-slate-500'
                }`}
              >
                Word Order Puzzle
              </span>
              {isSentenceBuilderUnlocked ? (
                <Sparkles className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-purple-400" />
              ) : (
                <Lock className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-amber-400" />
              )}
            </div>
            <div className="mt-1">
              <div className="text-xs font-black text-white">Sentence Builder</div>
              {isSentenceBuilderUnlocked ? (
                <div className="text-[10px] text-slate-300">Reconstruct Examples</div>
              ) : (
                <div className="text-[10px] font-bold text-amber-300 mt-0.5">
                  Unlocks at 12 words — {unlockedWordsCount}/12
                </div>
              )}
            </div>
          </button>

          {/* 12+ Words: Tone & Sandhi Drill */}
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={() => {
              if (!isToneDrillUnlocked) {
                soundFX.playMistake();
                triggerToast?.(
                  'Mode Locked (12+ Words Required)',
                  `Unlocks at 12 words — ${unlockedWordsCount}/12 unlocked. Complete Lesson 1 or unlock more units!`
                );
                return;
              }
              onLaunchToneSandhiDrill(scannedPool);
            }}
            className={`game-btn-3d p-3 rounded-2xl border text-left flex flex-col justify-between transition-all ${
              isToneDrillUnlocked
                ? 'bg-gradient-to-br from-emerald-500/20 to-amber-500/20 hover:from-emerald-500/30 hover:to-amber-500/30 border-emerald-400/50'
                : 'bg-slate-900/75 border-slate-800 opacity-75'
            }`}
          >
            <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
              <span
                className={`text-[10px] font-black uppercase ${
                  isToneDrillUnlocked ? 'text-emerald-300' : 'text-slate-500'
                }`}
              >
                声调辨析 • Ear Drill
              </span>
              {isToneDrillUnlocked ? (
                <Headphones className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-emerald-400" />
              ) : (
                <Lock className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-amber-400" />
              )}
            </div>
            <div className="mt-1">
              <div className="text-xs font-black text-white">Tone & Sandhi Drill</div>
              {isToneDrillUnlocked ? (
                <div className="text-[10px] text-slate-300">3+3 → 2+3 & 不/一 Sandhi</div>

                  Unlocks at 12 words — {unlockedWordsCount}/12

              )}
            </div>
          </button>

          {/* 25+ Words: Fill-in-the-Blank (Cloze) */}
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={() => {
              if (!isClozeUnlocked) {
                soundFX.playMistake();

              isBlitzUnlocked
                ? 'bg-gradient-to-br from-amber-500/20 to-rose-500/20 hover:from-amber-500/30 hover:to-rose-500/30 border-amber-400/50'
                : 'bg-slate-900/75 border-slate-800 opacity-75'
            }`}
          >
            <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
              <span
                className={`text-[10px] font-black uppercase ${
                  isBlitzUnlocked ? 'text-amber-300' : 'text-slate-500'
                }`}
              >
                Arcade Mini-Game
              </span>

                <Clock className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-amber-400" />
              ) : (
                <Lock className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-amber-400" />
              )}
            </div>
            <div className="mt-1">
              <div className="text-xs font-black text-white">60s Blitz Match</div>
              {isBlitzUnlocked ? (
                <div className="text-[10px] text-slate-300">4x4 Pairs • +2s / -2s</div>
              ) : (
                <div className="text-[10px] font-bold text-amber-300 mt-0.5">
                  Unlocks at 25 words — {unlockedWordsCount}/25
                </div>
              )}
            </div>
          </button>
        </div>

        {/* 40+ Words: HSK Mock Exam Mode (模拟测试 • 20 Questions • 15-Minute Timer) */}
        <button
          type="button"

            if (!isHskMockUnlocked) {

          }}

            isHskMockUnlocked
              ? 'bg-gradient-to-r from-indigo-600/30 via-purple-600/30 to-amber-500/25 hover:from-indigo-600/40 hover:to-amber-500/35 border-amber-400/50'
              : 'bg-slate-900/80 border-slate-800 opacity-75'
          }`}
        >
          <div className="flex items-center gap-3 min-w-0 text-left">
            <div
              className={`w-10 h-10 rounded-xl border flex items-center justify-center shrink-0 font-black text-sm ${
                isHskMockUnlocked
                  ? 'bg-amber-500/20 border-amber-400/50 text-amber-300'
                  : 'bg-slate-950 border-slate-800 text-slate-500'
              }`}
            >
              {isHskMockUnlocked ? 'HSK' : <Lock className="w-4 h-4 text-amber-400" />}
            </div>
            <div className="min-w-0">
              <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5 flex-wrap">
                <span className="text-xs font-black text-white">
                  HSK Mock Test (20 Questions)
                </span>
                <span className="text-[9px] font-black uppercase px-2 py-0.5 rounded-full bg-amber-500/20 text-amber-300 border border-amber-400/40">
                  模拟测试
                </span>
              </div>
              {isHskMockUnlocked ? (
                <p className="text-[10px] text-slate-300 truncate mt-0.5">
                  10 Listening + 10 Reading/Cloze • 15m Timer • Pass: 12/20 (60%)
                </p>
              ) : (
                <p className="text-[10px] font-bold text-amber-300 mt-0.5">
                  Unlocks at 40 words — {unlockedWordsCount}/40 (Complete Lesson 3 or import HSK deck)
                </p>
              )}
            </div>
          </div>
          {isHskMockUnlocked ? (
            <ChevronRight className="w-4 h-4 text-amber-300 shrink-0" />
          ) : (
            <Lock className="w-4 h-4 text-slate-500 shrink-0" />
          )}
        </button>
      </div>

      {/* UPCOMING SMART REVIEW QUEUE PREVIEW (SHOWS PRIORITY ORDER & ASSIGNED MODALITY) */}
      <div className="rounded-2xl bg-slate-800/75 border border-slate-700/80 p-4 space-y-2.5">
        <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
          <h2 className="text-xs font-extrabold uppercase tracking-wider text-slate-300">
            Adaptive Queue Preview (Lowest Mastery First)
          </h2>
          <span className="text-[11px] font-bold text-amber-300">
            {adaptiveSmartQueue.length} Steps Queued
          </span>
        </div>

        <div className="space-y-2 max-h-60 overflow-y-auto pr-1">
          {adaptiveSmartQueue.map((item, idx) => {
            const modMeta = MODALITY_META[item.modality] || MODALITY_META.recognition;
            return (
              <div
                key={item.stepId}
                className="p-2.5 rounded-xl bg-slate-900/95 border border-slate-800 flex items-center justify-between gap-2"
              >
                <div className="flex items-center gap-2.5 min-w-0">
                  <span className="w-6 h-6 rounded-lg bg-slate-800 text-slate-400 font-black text-[11px] flex items-center justify-center shrink-0">
                    {idx + 1}
                  </span>
                  <div className="min-w-0">
                    <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5">
                      <span className="text-sm font-black text-white">{item.word.hanzi}</span>
                      <span className="text-xs font-bold text-rose-400">{item.word.pinyin}</span>
                      <span className="text-[10px] px-1.5 py-0.2 rounded bg-slate-800 text-amber-300 font-bold">
                        Lv.{item.mastery}/5
                      </span>
                    </div>
                    <p className="text-[10px] text-slate-400 truncate">{item.reason}</p>
                  </div>
                </div>

                <span
                  className={`text-[10px] font-black uppercase px-2.5 py-1 rounded-full border shrink-0 ${modMeta.badgeClass}`}
                >
                  {modMeta.shortLabel}
                </span>
              </div>
            );
          })}
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* Direct Single-Skill Drills & Custom Decks Access */}
      <div className="rounded-2xl bg-slate-800/60 border border-slate-700/80 p-4 space-y-2.5">
        <h3 className="text-xs font-extrabold uppercase tracking-wider text-slate-300">
          Focused Single-Skill Drills ({activeLesson?.title || 'Active Unit'})
        </h3>
        <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-2">
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={() => onLaunchActivity('writing')}
            className="game-btn-3d p-3 rounded-xl bg-slate-900 hover:bg-slate-800 border border-amber-500/30 text-left"
          >
            <span className="text-[10px] font-black uppercase text-amber-400 block">
              Calligraphy
            </span>
            <span className="text-xs font-extrabold text-white">Stroke Order (米字格)</span>
          </button>

          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={() => onLaunchActivity('dictation_only')}
            className="game-btn-3d p-3 rounded-xl bg-slate-900 hover:bg-slate-800 border border-emerald-500/30 text-left"
          >
            <span className="text-[10px] font-black uppercase text-emerald-400 block">
              Ear Training
            </span>
            <span className="text-xs font-extrabold text-white">Pinyin / Hanzi Dictation</span>
          </button>

          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={() => onLaunchActivity('speaking_only')}
            className="game-btn-3d p-3 rounded-xl bg-slate-900 hover:bg-slate-800 border border-sky-500/30 text-left"
          >
            <span className="text-[10px] font-black uppercase text-sky-400 block">
              Oral Check
            </span>
            <span className="text-xs font-extrabold text-white">Speech Recognition</span>
          </button>

          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={() => onLaunchActivity('flashcards')}
            className="game-btn-3d p-3 rounded-xl bg-slate-900 hover:bg-slate-800 border border-rose-500/30 text-left"
          >
            <span className="text-[10px] font-black uppercase text-rose-400 block">
              Discovery
            </span>
            <span className="text-xs font-extrabold text-white">3D Flashcards</span>
          </button>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

// ============================================================================
// STROKE ORDER RULES DOJO: INLINE EXAMPLE ANIMATION PLAYER & 2-STEP UNSEEN QUIZ MODAL
// ============================================================================
function RuleExampleMiniPlayer({ rule, speakMandarin }) {
  const [selectedIdx, setSelectedIdx] = useState(0);
  const [scriptStatus, setScriptStatus] = useState('loading');
  const [strokeCount, setStrokeCount] = useState(null);
  const containerRef = useRef(null);
  const writerRef = useRef(null);

  const currentExample = rule.learnedChars[selectedIdx] || rule.learnedChars[0];

  useEffect(() => {
    let mounted = true;
    loadHanziWriterScript()
      .then(() => {
        if (mounted) setScriptStatus('ready');
      })
      .catch(() => {
        if (mounted) setScriptStatus('error');
      });
    return () => {
      mounted = false;
    };
  }, []);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (scriptStatus !== 'ready' || !containerRef.current || !window.HanziWriter) return;
    const containerEl = containerRef.current;
    containerEl.innerHTML = '';
    writerRef.current = null;

    speakMandarin(currentExample.char);

    const writer = window.HanziWriter.create(containerEl, currentExample.char, {
      width: 164,
      height: 164,
      padding: 14,
      showOutline: true,
      showCharacter: true,
      strokeAnimationSpeed: 1.05,
      delayBetweenStrokes: 200,
      strokeColor: '#f8fafc',
      radicalColor: '#dc2626',
      outlineColor: '#334155',
      onLoadCharDataSuccess: (data) => {
        setStrokeCount(Array.isArray(data.strokes) ? data.strokes.length : null);
        setTimeout(() => {
          if (writerRef.current === writer) {
            writer.animateCharacter();
          }
        }, 140);
      }
    });

    writerRef.current = writer;

    return () => {
      if (containerEl) containerEl.innerHTML = '';
      writerRef.current = null;
    };
  }, [scriptStatus, currentExample.char, speakMandarin]);

  return (
    <div className="rounded-2xl bg-slate-950/90 border border-amber-500/30 p-3 space-y-2.5">
      <div className="flex items-center justify-between gap-2">
        <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5">
          {rule.learnedChars.map((item, idx) => (
            <button
              key={item.char}
              type="button"
              onClick={() => {
                soundFX.playPop();
                setSelectedIdx(idx);
              }}
              className={`px-2.5 py-1 rounded-xl text-xs font-black border transition-all ${
                selectedIdx === idx
                  ? 'bg-amber-500 text-slate-950 border-amber-300 shadow'
                  : 'bg-slate-900 border-slate-700 text-slate-300 hover:text-white'
              }`}
            >
              {item.char} ({item.pinyin})
            </button>
          ))}
        </div>

        <button
          type="button"
          onClick={() => {
            soundFX.playPop();
            writerRef.current?.animateCharacter();
          }}
          className="px-2.5 py-1 rounded-xl bg-amber-500/20 hover:bg-amber-500/30 border border-amber-400/40 text-amber-300 text-[11px] font-bold flex items-center gap-1"
        >
          <Play className="w-3 h-3 fill-current" />
          <span>Replay</span>
        </button>
      </div>

      <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
        <div className="relative w-[168px] h-[168px] rounded-2xl bg-slate-900 border border-amber-500/40 flex items-center justify-center shrink-0 overflow-hidden">
          <svg
            viewBox="0 0 168 168"
            className="absolute inset-0 w-full h-full pointer-events-none opacity-25"
          >
            <rect x="4" y="4" width="160" height="160" fill="none" stroke="#f59e0b" strokeWidth="1.5" />
            <line x1="4" y1="84" x2="164" y2="84" stroke="#f59e0b" strokeWidth="1" strokeDasharray="4 4" />
            <line x1="84" y1="4" x2="84" y2="164" stroke="#f59e0b" strokeWidth="1" strokeDasharray="4 4" />
            <line x1="4" y1="4" x2="164" y2="164" stroke="#f59e0b" strokeWidth="1" strokeDasharray="4 4" />
            <line x1="4" y1="164" x2="164" y2="4" stroke="#f59e0b" strokeWidth="1" strokeDasharray="4 4" />
          </svg>
          <div ref={containerRef} className="relative z-10 w-[164px] h-[164px]" />
        </div>

        <div className="space-y-1.5 text-xs min-w-0">
          <div className="flex items-baseline gap-1.5">
            <span className="text-2xl font-black text-white">{currentExample.char}</span>
            <span className="font-bold text-rose-400">{currentExample.pinyin}</span>
          </div>
          <p className="text-emerald-300 font-bold">{currentExample.meaning}</p>
          <p className="text-[11px] text-slate-400">
            {strokeCount ? `${strokeCount} strokes total` : 'Loading strokes...'}
          </p>
          <p className="text-[10px] text-amber-300/90 leading-snug">
            Notice how <strong>{rule.chineseRule}</strong> guides the stroke sequence.
          </p>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

function StrokeRuleIntuitionModal({
  rule,
  isSlowMode,
  onSetSlowMode,
  speakMandarin,
  speakingState,
  onClose,
  onCompleteRule
}) {
  const unseenList = rule?.unseenChars || [];
  const [charIdx, setCharIdx] = useState(0);
  // 2-Step Unseen Intuition Quiz: Step 1 = Ghost Trace (showOutline: true), Step 2 = Blind Memory (showOutline: false)
  const [quizStep, setQuizStep] = useState(1); // 1 | 2
  const [scriptStatus, setScriptStatus] = useState('loading');
  const [modeStatus, setModeStatus] = useState('quiz'); // 'quiz' | 'animating' | 'step_transition' | 'char_done'
  const [strokeStats, setStrokeStats] = useState({
    completedStrokes: 0,
    totalStrokes: 0,
    mistakesOnCurrent: 0,
    totalMistakes: 0,
    hintTriggered: false
  });
  const [completedCharIndices, setCompletedCharIndices] = useState([]);
  const [boardFeedback, setBoardFeedback] = useState('idle');

  const writerTargetRef = useRef(null);
  const writerInstanceRef = useRef(null);
  const transitionTimerRef = useRef(null);

  const currentChallenge = unseenList[charIdx] || unseenList[0];
  const currentChar = currentChallenge?.char || '干';
  const showOutlineForStep = quizStep === 1;
  const hintMissThreshold = quizStep === 1 ? 2 : 3;

  useEffect(() => {
    let mounted = true;
    loadHanziWriterScript()
      .then(() => {
        if (mounted) setScriptStatus('ready');
      })
      .catch(() => {
        if (mounted) setScriptStatus('error');
      });
    return () => {
      mounted = false;
      if (transitionTimerRef.current) clearTimeout(transitionTimerRef.current);
    };
  }, []);

  useEffect(() => {
    speakMandarin(currentChar);
  }, [charIdx, currentChar, speakMandarin]);

  const startTwoStepQuiz = useCallback(
    (writer, targetStep = quizStep) => {
      const activeWriter = writer || writerInstanceRef.current;
      if (!activeWriter) return;

      if (transitionTimerRef.current) {
        clearTimeout(transitionTimerRef.current);
        transitionTimerRef.current = null;
      }

      const isStep1Trace = targetStep === 1;
      const missesAllowed = isStep1Trace ? 2 : 3;

      setModeStatus('quiz');
      setStrokeStats((prev) => ({
        ...prev,
        completedStrokes: 0,
        mistakesOnCurrent: 0,
        totalMistakes: 0,
        hintTriggered: false
      }));

      activeWriter.hideCharacter();
      if (isStep1Trace) {
        activeWriter.showOutline();
      } else {
        activeWriter.hideOutline();
      }

      activeWriter.quiz({
        showHintAfterMisses: missesAllowed,
        highlightOnComplete: true,
        onMistake: (strokeData) => {
          soundFX.playMistake();
          setBoardFeedback('wrong');
          setTimeout(() => setBoardFeedback('idle'), 450);
          setStrokeStats((prev) => ({
            ...prev,
            mistakesOnCurrent: strokeData.mistakesOnStroke,
            totalMistakes: strokeData.totalMistakes,
            hintTriggered: strokeData.mistakesOnStroke >= missesAllowed
          }));
        },
        onCorrectStroke: (strokeData) => {
          const done = strokeData.strokeNum + 1;
          const total = done + strokeData.strokesRemaining;
          soundFX.playCorrect(done);
          setStrokeStats({
            completedStrokes: done,
            totalStrokes: total,
            mistakesOnCurrent: 0,
            totalMistakes: strokeData.totalMistakes,
            hintTriggered: false
          });
        },
        onComplete: (summaryData) => {
          setBoardFeedback('correct');
          setTimeout(() => setBoardFeedback('idle'), 500);
          setStrokeStats((prev) => ({
            ...prev,
            completedStrokes: prev.totalStrokes || prev.completedStrokes,
            mistakesOnCurrent: 0,
            totalMistakes: summaryData.totalMistakes,
            hintTriggered: false
          }));

          if (targetStep === 1) {
            soundFX.playCorrect(3);
            setModeStatus('step_transition');
            transitionTimerRef.current = setTimeout(() => {
              setQuizStep(2);
            }, 620);
          } else {
            soundFX.playVictory();
            setModeStatus('char_done');
            setCompletedCharIndices((prev) =>
              prev.includes(charIdx) ? prev : [...prev, charIdx]
            );
          }
        }
      });
    },
