            <div className="flex items-center justify-between border-b border-slate-800 pb-3">
              <div>
                <span className="text-[10px] font-extrabold uppercase text-rose-400">
                  Quick Add Vocabulary
                </span>
                <h3 className="text-base font-black text-white">Add Single Word</h3>
              </div>
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={() => setQuickAddOpen(false)}
                className="p-1.5 rounded-xl bg-slate-800 text-slate-400 hover:text-white"
              >
                <X className="w-4 h-4" />
              </button>
            </div>

            <form onSubmit={handleQuickAddSubmit} className="space-y-3">
              <div>
                <label className="text-xs font-bold text-slate-300 block mb-1">
                  Hanzi <span className="text-rose-400">*</span>
                </label>
                <input
                  type="text"
                  required
                  value={singleHanzi}
                  onChange={(e) => setSingleHanzi(e.target.value)}
                  placeholder="e.g. 朋友"
                  className="w-full bg-slate-950 border border-slate-700 focus:border-rose-500 rounded-xl px-3.5 py-2.5 text-sm font-bold text-white outline-none"
                />
              </div>

              <div>
                <label className="text-xs font-bold text-slate-300 block mb-1">
                  Pinyin <span className="text-rose-400">*</span>
                </label>
                <input
                  type="text"
                  required
                  value={singlePinyin}
                  onChange={(e) => setSinglePinyin(e.target.value)}
                  placeholder="e.g. péngyou or peng2 you5"
                  className="w-full bg-slate-950 border border-slate-700 focus:border-rose-500 rounded-xl px-3.5 py-2.5 text-xs font-bold text-white outline-none"
                />
              </div>

              <div>
                <label className="text-xs font-bold text-slate-300 block mb-1">
                  English Meaning <span className="text-rose-400">*</span>
                </label>
                <input
                  type="text"
                  required
                  value={singleMeaning}
                  onChange={(e) => setSingleMeaning(e.target.value)}
                  placeholder="e.g. friend / companion"
                  className="w-full bg-slate-950 border border-slate-700 focus:border-rose-500 rounded-xl px-3.5 py-2.5 text-xs font-bold text-white outline-none"
                />
              </div>

              <div>
                <label className="text-xs font-bold text-slate-300 block mb-1">
                  Example / Notes <span className="text-slate-500">(optional)</span>
                </label>
                <input
                  type="text"
                  value={singleExample}
                  onChange={(e) => setSingleExample(e.target.value)}
                  placeholder="e.g. 他是我的好朋友。"
                  className="w-full bg-slate-950 border border-slate-700 focus:border-rose-500 rounded-xl px-3.5 py-2.5 text-xs text-white outline-none"
                />
              </div>

              <div>
                <label className="text-xs font-bold text-slate-300 block mb-1">
                  Target Deck
                </label>
                <select
                  value={singleDeckName}
                  onChange={(e) => setSingleDeckName(e.target.value)}
                  className="w-full bg-slate-950 border border-slate-700 rounded-xl px-3.5 py-2.5 text-xs font-bold text-white outline-none"
                >
                  {customDecks.map((d) => (
                    <option key={d.id} value={d.name}>
                      {d.name}
                    </option>
                  ))}
                  <option value="__NEW__">+ Create New Deck...</option>
                </select>
              </div>

              {singleDeckName === '__NEW__' && (
                <div>
                  <input
                    type="text"
                    value={customNewDeckName}
                    onChange={(e) => setCustomNewDeckName(e.target.value)}
                    placeholder="Enter new deck name..."
                    className="w-full bg-slate-950 border border-amber-500/60 rounded-xl px-3.5 py-2 text-xs font-bold text-white outline-none"
                  />
                </div>
              )}

              <div className="flex gap-2 pt-2">
                <button
                  type="button"
                  onClick={() => setQuickAddOpen(false)}
                  className="flex-1 py-3 rounded-xl bg-slate-800 text-xs font-bold text-slate-300"
                >
                  Cancel
                </button>
                <button
                  type="submit"
                  className="flex-1 py-3 rounded-xl bg-gradient-to-r from-rose-500 to-red-600 text-xs font-extrabold text-white shadow-lg"
                >
                  Save Word
                </button>
              </div>
            </form>
          </div>
        </div>
      )}

      {/* MODAL 2: RAW COMPLETE-HSK-VOCABULARY LEVEL TAG SELECTOR MODAL */}
      {rawHskDataPending && (
        <div className="fixed inset-0 z-50 bg-slate-950/90 backdrop-blur-sm flex items-center justify-center p-4">
          <div className="w-full max-w-sm rounded-3xl bg-slate-900 border border-emerald-500/40 p-5 space-y-4 shadow-2xl">
            <div className="flex items-center justify-between border-b border-slate-800 pb-3">
              <div>
                <span className="text-[10px] font-extrabold uppercase text-emerald-400">
                  complete-hsk-vocabulary Detected
                </span>
                <h3 className="text-base font-black text-white">Select HSK Level Tag</h3>
              </div>
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={() => setRawHskDataPending(null)}
                className="p-1.5 rounded-xl bg-slate-800 text-slate-400 hover:text-white"
              >
                <X className="w-4 h-4" />
              </button>
            </div>

            <p className="text-xs text-slate-300">
              Choose which HSK level tag to extract. Matching words will be automatically mapped and chunked into sequential lessons of <strong>20 words each</strong> (Unit 1, Unit 2...) on the Map!
            </p>

            {/* Level Tag Grid */}
            <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-2 max-h-48 overflow-y-auto pr-1">
              {availableHskLevels.map((item) => {
                const isSelected = selectedHskTag === item.tag;
                const unitCount = Math.ceil(item.count / 20);
                return (
                  <button
                    key={item.tag}
                    type="button"
                    onClick={() => {
                      setSelectedHskTag(item.tag);
                      setCustomHskCourseTitle(`HSK (${item.tag.toUpperCase()})`);
                    }}
                    className={`p-2.5 rounded-xl border text-left transition-all ${
                      isSelected
                        ? 'bg-emerald-500/20 border-emerald-400 text-white shadow'
                        : 'bg-slate-950 border-slate-800 text-slate-400 hover:text-slate-200'
                    }`}
                  >
                    <div className="text-xs font-black uppercase text-emerald-300">
                      {item.tag}
                    </div>
                    <div className="text-[11px] font-bold text-white mt-0.5">
                      {item.count} words
                    </div>
                    <div className="text-[10px] text-slate-400">
                      → {unitCount} Unit{unitCount > 1 ? 's' : ''} (20/unit)
                    </div>
                  </button>
                );
              })}
            </div>

            <div>
              <label className="text-xs font-bold text-slate-300 block mb-1">
                Course Title for Map Selector
              </label>
              <input
                type="text"
                value={customHskCourseTitle}
                onChange={(e) => setCustomHskCourseTitle(e.target.value)}
                className="w-full bg-slate-950 border border-slate-700 rounded-xl px-3.5 py-2 text-xs font-bold text-white outline-none"
              />
            </div>

            <div className="flex gap-2 pt-1">
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={() => setRawHskDataPending(null)}
                className="flex-1 py-3 rounded-xl bg-slate-800 text-xs font-bold text-slate-300"
              >
                Cancel
              </button>
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={handleConfirmHskLevelExtraction}
                className="flex-1 py-3 rounded-xl bg-gradient-to-r from-emerald-500 to-teal-600 text-xs font-extrabold text-white shadow-lg"
              >
                Extract & Add to Map
              </button>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
      )}

      {/* MODAL 3: CHARACTER MATRIX QUICK-INSPECTION MODAL */}
      {selectedCharEntry && (
        <CharacterInspectModal
          charEntry={selectedCharEntry}
          isSlowMode={isSlowMode}
          onSetSlowMode={onSetSlowMode}
          speakMandarin={speakMandarin}
          speakingState={speakingState}
          onClose={() => setSelectedCharEntry(null)}
        />
      )}

      {/* MODAL 4: STROKE ORDER RULES DOJO — 2-STEP UNSEEN CHARACTER INTUITION QUIZ */}
      {activeRuleChallenge && (
        <StrokeRuleIntuitionModal
          rule={activeRuleChallenge}
          isSlowMode={isSlowMode}
          onSetSlowMode={onSetSlowMode}
          speakMandarin={speakMandarin}
          speakingState={speakingState}
          onClose={() => setActiveRuleChallenge(null)}
          onCompleteRule={(completedRule) => {
            setActiveRuleChallenge(null);
            onCompleteStrokeRuleDojo?.(completedRule);
          }}
        />
      )}
    </div>
  );
}

// ============================================================================
// TAB 4: PROFILE VIEW (ACHIEVEMENTS / BADGES GRID + WEAKEST WORDS REVIEW + STATS + DAILY GOAL)
// ============================================================================
function ProfileView({
  progress,
  globalStats,
  allUniqueWordsMap,
  courses,
  customDecks,
  currentLevel,
  dailyGoalXp = 30,
  todayXpEarned = 0,
  onSetDailyGoalXp,
  onToggleSpeed,
  onExportAllData,
  onImportBackupData,
  confirmResetOpen,
  setConfirmResetOpen,
  onConfirmReset,
  onLaunchWeakWordsReview
}) {
  const profileBackupInputRef = useRef(null);

  const handleProfileBackupFile = (e) => {
    const file = e.target.files?.[0];
    if (!file) return;
    const reader = new FileReader();
    reader.onload = (event) => {
      try {
        const parsed = JSON.parse(String(event.target?.result || ''));
        onImportBackupData(parsed);
      } catch (err) {
        // Handled by parent toast
      }
    };
    reader.readAsText(file);
    e.target.value = '';
  };

  const trackedWordsArray = useMemo(() => {
    return Array.from(allUniqueWordsMap.values());
  }, [allUniqueWordsMap]);

  // Identify Weakest Words (Mastery Level 0 to 2)
  const weakWordsList = useMemo(() => {
    const list = trackedWordsArray.filter(
      (word) => getWordMasteryLevel(progress.mastery, word) <= 2
    );
    return list;
  }, [trackedWordsArray, progress.mastery]);

  // Automatic Achievements / Badges Computed from LocalStorage Stats
  const achievements = useMemo(() => {
    const completedStagesTotal =
      (progress.completedStages?.length || 0) +
      Object.values(progress.lessonProgress || {}).reduce(
        (acc, lp) => acc + (lp.completedStages?.length || 0),
        0
      );

    const hasWrittenStage =
      (progress.stats?.writingStagesCompleted || 0) > 0 ||
      (progress.completedStages || []).includes(2);

    const hasPerfectBoss =
      (progress.stats?.perfectBossWins || 0) > 0 ||
      (progress.completedStages || []).includes(4);

    return [
      {
        id: 'first_steps',
        title: 'First Steps',
        desc: 'Complete your first study stage or earn 50+ XP',
        icon: Sparkles,
        unlocked: completedStagesTotal >= 1 || progress.xp >= 50,
        progressText: completedStagesTotal >= 1 || progress.xp >= 50 ? 'Unlocked!' : `${progress.xp}/50 XP`
      },
      {
        id: 'stroke_master',
        title: 'Stroke Master',
        desc: 'Complete a Hanzi Stroke Order Writing stage',
        icon: PenTool,
        unlocked: hasWrittenStage,
        progressText: hasWrittenStage ? 'Unlocked!' : '0/1 Stage'
      },
      {
        id: 'perfect_boss',
        title: 'Perfect Boss',
        desc: 'Defeat the Unit Boss Challenge',
        icon: Swords,
        unlocked: hasPerfectBoss,
        progressText: hasPerfectBoss ? 'Unlocked!' : 'Defeat Boss'
      },
      {
        id: 'fifty_words_club',
        title: '50 Words Club',
        desc: 'Build a library of 50+ words or master 3+ core words',
        icon: BookOpen,
        unlocked:
          globalStats.totalTrackedWords >= 50 || globalStats.masteredWordsCount >= 3,
        progressText:
          globalStats.totalTrackedWords >= 50
            ? `${globalStats.totalTrackedWords}/50 Words`
            : `${globalStats.masteredWordsCount}/3 Mastered`
      },
      {
        id: 'combo_virtuoso',
        title: 'Combo Virtuoso',
        desc: 'Reach a 3x+ Combo streak or 3-day study streak',
        icon: Flame,
        unlocked: (progress.stats?.highestCombo || 0) >= 3 || progress.streak >= 3,
        progressText: `${Math.max(progress.stats?.highestCombo || 0, progress.streak)}/3 Streak`
      },
      {
        id: 'jade_scholar',
        title: 'Jade Scholar',
        desc: 'Reach Player Level 3 (200+ Total XP)',
        icon: Crown,
        unlocked: currentLevel >= 3,
        progressText: `Lv.${currentLevel}/3`
      }
    ];
  }, [progress, globalStats, currentLevel]);

  const unlockedCount = achievements.filter((a) => a.unlocked).length;

  return (
    <div className="p-4 space-y-4">
      {/* Learner Profile Banner */}
      <div className="rounded-3xl bg-slate-800/90 border border-slate-700/80 p-5 flex items-center gap-4 shadow-xl">
        <div className="w-16 h-16 rounded-2xl bg-gradient-to-br from-rose-500 via-red-600 to-amber-500 flex items-center justify-center text-2xl font-black text-white shadow-lg shrink-0 border border-amber-300/30">
          学
        </div>
        <div className="min-w-0 flex-1">
          <div className="flex items-center gap-2 flex-wrap">
            <h1 className="text-base font-black text-white">
              Lv. {currentLevel} {getPlayerTitle(currentLevel)}
            </h1>
            <span className="text-[10px] font-black px-2 py-0.5 rounded-full bg-amber-500/20 text-amber-300 border border-amber-500/30">
              {unlockedCount}/{achievements.length} Badges
            </span>
          </div>
          <p className="text-xs text-slate-400 mt-0.5">
            {courses.length} Course(s) • {customDecks.length} Custom Deck(s) •{' '}
            {globalStats.totalTrackedWords} Total Words
          </p>
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* Real Core Gamification Metrics: Mastered Words (Lv 4-5), Accumulated XP, Daily Streak */}
      <div className="grid grid-cols-3 gap-2.5">
        <div className="rounded-2xl bg-slate-800/75 border border-emerald-500/30 p-3.5 text-center">
          <Trophy className="w-5 h-5 text-emerald-400 mx-auto" />
          <div className="text-xl font-black text-white mt-1">
            {globalStats.masteredWordsCount}
          </div>
          <div className="text-[10px] font-bold uppercase text-emerald-300">
            Mastered (Lv 4-5)
          </div>
        </div>
        <div className="rounded-2xl bg-slate-800/75 border border-rose-500/30 p-3.5 text-center">
          <Zap className="w-5 h-5 text-rose-400 fill-rose-400 mx-auto" />
          <div className="text-xl font-black text-white mt-1">{progress.xp}</div>
          <div className="text-[10px] font-bold uppercase text-rose-300">Accumulated XP</div>
        </div>
        <div className="rounded-2xl bg-slate-800/75 border border-amber-500/30 p-3.5 text-center">
          <Flame className="w-5 h-5 text-amber-400 fill-amber-400 mx-auto" />
          <div className="text-xl font-black text-white mt-1">{progress.streak}</div>
          <div className="text-[10px] font-bold uppercase text-amber-300">Daily Streak</div>
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* WEAKEST WORDS REVIEW CALL-TO-ACTION CARD (LEVEL 0-2 CHARACTERS) */}
      <div className="rounded-3xl bg-gradient-to-br from-rose-950/80 via-slate-800 to-slate-900 border border-rose-500/40 p-4 space-y-3 shadow-lg">
        <div className="flex items-start justify-between gap-3">
          <div>
            <span className="text-[10px] font-black uppercase tracking-wider px-2 py-0.5 rounded-full bg-rose-500/20 text-rose-300 border border-rose-500/30 inline-flex items-center gap-1">
              <Target className="w-3 h-3" />
              <span>Targeted Mastery Booster</span>
            </span>
            <h2 className="text-sm font-black text-white mt-1.5">
              Weakest Words Review (Level 0–2)
            </h2>
            <p className="text-xs text-slate-300 mt-0.5">
              {weakWordsList.length > 0
                ? `You have ${weakWordsList.length} character(s) at Mastery Level 0–2 (${weakWordsList
                    .slice(0, 4)
                    .map((w) => w.hanzi)
                    .join(', ')}${weakWordsList.length > 4 ? '...' : ''}). Re-train them now!`
                : 'All tracked characters are above Level 2! Click below for a quick refresher.'}
            </p>
          </div>
        </div>

        <button
          type="button"
          onClick={() =>
            onLaunchWeakWordsReview(
              weakWordsList.length > 0 ? weakWordsList : trackedWordsArray.slice(0, 5)
            )
          }
          className="game-btn-3d w-full py-3 rounded-xl bg-gradient-to-r from-rose-500 to-amber-500 text-slate-950 font-black text-xs shadow-lg border-b-4 border-rose-800 flex items-center justify-center gap-2"
        >
          <RotateCcw className="w-4 h-4 stroke-[2.5px]" />
          <span>
            Review {weakWordsList.length > 0 ? `${weakWordsList.length} Weakest Words` : 'Core Words'} Now
          </span>
        </button>
      </div>

      {/* ACHIEVEMENTS / BADGES GRID */}
      <div className="rounded-2xl bg-slate-800/75 border border-slate-700/80 p-4 space-y-3">
        <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
          <h2 className="text-xs font-extrabold uppercase tracking-wider text-amber-400 flex items-center gap-1.5">
            <Award className="w-4 h-4" />
            <span>Achievements & Badges</span>
          </h2>
          <span className="text-xs font-black text-slate-300">
            {unlockedCount} / {achievements.length} Unlocked
          </span>
        </div>

        <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-2.5">
          {achievements.map((badge) => {
            const BadgeIcon = badge.icon;
            return (
              <div
                key={badge.id}
                className={`rounded-2xl p-3 border transition-all flex flex-col justify-between ${
                  badge.unlocked
                    ? 'bg-gradient-to-b from-amber-500/15 to-slate-900 border-amber-400/50 shadow-md'
                    : 'bg-slate-900/60 border-slate-800 opacity-65'
                }`}
              >
                <div className="flex items-start justify-between gap-2">
                  <div
                    className={`w-9 h-9 rounded-xl flex items-center justify-center shrink-0 ${
                      badge.unlocked
                        ? 'bg-gradient-to-br from-amber-400 to-orange-500 text-slate-950 shadow'
                        : 'bg-slate-800 text-slate-500'
                    }`}
                  >
                    <BadgeIcon className="w-5 h-5" />
                  </div>
                  <span
                    className={`text-[10px] font-black px-2 py-0.5 rounded-full ${
                      badge.unlocked
                        ? 'bg-emerald-500/20 text-emerald-300 border border-emerald-500/30'
                        : 'bg-slate-800 text-slate-400'
                    }`}
                  >
                    {badge.progressText}
                  </span>
                </div>
                <div className="mt-2">
                  <h3 className="text-xs font-black text-white">{badge.title}</h3>
                  <p className="text-[10px] text-slate-400 mt-0.5 leading-snug">{badge.desc}</p>
                </div>
              </div>
            );
          })}
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* Secondary Mastery Summary */}
      <div className="rounded-2xl bg-slate-800/75 border border-slate-700/80 p-4 space-y-3">
        <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
          <h2 className="text-xs font-extrabold uppercase tracking-wider text-slate-300">
            Vocabulary Mastery Distribution
          </h2>
          <span className="text-xs font-bold text-amber-300">
            {globalStats.totalStarsEarned} / {globalStats.maxStarsPossible} ★
          </span>
        </div>

        <div className="grid grid-cols-3 gap-2 text-center">
          <div className="p-2.5 rounded-xl bg-slate-900 border border-slate-800">
            <div className="text-sm font-black text-emerald-400">
              {globalStats.masteredWordsCount}
            </div>
            <div className="text-[10px] text-slate-400 font-bold">Mastered (Lv 4–5)</div>
          </div>
          <div className="p-2.5 rounded-xl bg-slate-900 border border-slate-800">
            <div className="text-sm font-black text-amber-400">
              {globalStats.learningWordsCount}
            </div>
            <div className="text-[10px] text-slate-400 font-bold">In Progress (Lv 1–3)</div>
          </div>
          <div className="p-2.5 rounded-xl bg-slate-900 border border-slate-800">
            <div className="text-sm font-black text-slate-300">
              {Math.max(
                0,
                globalStats.totalTrackedWords -
                  globalStats.masteredWordsCount -
                  globalStats.learningWordsCount
              )}
            </div>
            <div className="text-[10px] text-slate-400 font-bold">New (Lv 0)</div>
          </div>
        </div>

        {/* Word-by-Word Mastery Bars */}
        <div className="space-y-2.5 pt-2 max-h-52 overflow-y-auto pr-1">
          {trackedWordsArray.slice(0, 20).map((word) => {
            const lv = getWordMasteryLevel(progress.mastery, word);
            return (
              <div key={word.id} className="space-y-1">
                <div className="flex items-center justify-between text-xs">
                  <span className="font-bold text-white truncate max-w-[210px]">
                    {word.hanzi} ({word.pinyin}) — {(word.meaning || word.english).split(/[/;]/)[0]}
                  </span>
                  <span
                    className={`font-extrabold ${
                      lv >= 4 ? 'text-emerald-400' : lv >= 1 ? 'text-amber-300' : 'text-slate-500'
                    }`}
                  >
                    Lv.{lv}/5 {lv >= 4 ? '★' : ''}
                  </span>
                </div>
                <div className="w-full h-2 bg-slate-900 rounded-full overflow-hidden">
                  <div
                    className={`h-full transition-all duration-500 ${
                      lv >= 4
                        ? 'bg-gradient-to-r from-emerald-500 to-teal-400'
                        : 'bg-gradient-to-r from-amber-500 to-rose-500'
                    }`}
                    style={{ width: `${(lv / 5) * 100}%` }}
                  />
                </div>
              </div>
            );
          })}
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* Full Data Backup & Settings */}
      <div className="rounded-2xl bg-slate-800/75 border border-slate-700/80 p-4 space-y-3">
        <h2 className="text-xs font-extrabold uppercase tracking-wider text-slate-300">
          Daily XP Goal, Backup & Study Settings
        </h2>

        {/* Configurable Daily XP Goal Setting (Default: 30 XP/day) */}
        <div className="rounded-2xl bg-slate-900/90 border border-slate-800 p-3 space-y-2">
          <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
            <div>
              <p className="text-xs font-extrabold text-white">Daily XP Target</p>
              <p className="text-[11px] text-slate-400">
                Today's Progress:{' '}
                <strong className="text-emerald-400">
                  {todayXpEarned} / {dailyGoalXp} XP
                </strong>
              </p>
            </div>
            <span
              className={`px-2.5 py-0.5 rounded-full text-[10px] font-black border ${
                todayXpEarned >= dailyGoalXp
                  ? 'bg-emerald-500/20 border-emerald-400 text-emerald-300'
                  : 'bg-amber-500/15 border-amber-400/40 text-amber-300'
              }`}
            >
              {todayXpEarned >= dailyGoalXp ? '★ Goal Complete!' : 'In Progress'}
            </span>
          </div>

          <div className="grid grid-cols-4 gap-1.5">
            {[20, 30, 50, 100].map((targetVal) => {
              const isSelected = dailyGoalXp === targetVal;
              return (
                <button
                  key={targetVal}
                  type="button"
                  onClick={() => {
                    soundFX.playPop();
                    onSetDailyGoalXp?.(targetVal);
                  }}
                  className={`game-btn-3d py-1.5 rounded-xl text-xs font-black border transition-all ${
                    isSelected
                      ? 'bg-emerald-500 text-slate-950 border-emerald-300 shadow'
                      : 'bg-slate-950 border-slate-800 text-slate-400 hover:text-white'
                  }`}
                >
                  {targetVal} XP
                </button>
              );
            })}
          </div>
        </div>

        <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-2">
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={onExportAllData}
            className="game-btn-3d py-2.5 px-3 rounded-xl bg-emerald-600/20 hover:bg-emerald-600/30 border border-emerald-500/40 text-emerald-300 text-xs font-extrabold flex items-center justify-center gap-1.5"
          >
            <Download className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
            <span>Export All Data</span>
          </button>

          <input
            ref={profileBackupInputRef}
            type="file"
            accept=".json,application/json"
            onChange={handleProfileBackupFile}
            className="hidden"
          />
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={() => profileBackupInputRef.current?.click()}
            className="game-btn-3d py-2.5 px-3 rounded-xl bg-amber-500/20 hover:bg-amber-500/30 border border-amber-500/40 text-amber-300 text-xs font-extrabold flex items-center justify-center gap-1.5"
          >
            <Upload className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
            <span>Import Backup</span>
          </button>
        </div>

        <div className="flex items-center justify-between pt-2 border-t border-slate-700/70">
          <div>
            <p className="text-xs font-bold text-white">Default Speech Synthesis Speed</p>
            <p className="text-[11px] text-slate-400">Forced to native Mandarin (zh-CN • 1.0x vs 0.5x)</p>
          </div>
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={() => onToggleSpeed('你好')}
            className={`game-btn-3d px-3 py-1.5 rounded-xl border text-xs font-black ${
              progress.isSlowMode || progress.preferredSpeed === 0.5
                ? 'bg-sky-500/20 border-sky-400 text-sky-300'
                : 'bg-slate-900 border-slate-700 text-amber-300'
            }`}
          >
            {progress.isSlowMode || progress.preferredSpeed === 0.5 ? '0.5x Slow' : '1.0x Normal'}
          </button>
        </div>

        {/* Inline Reset Study Progress Confirmation */}
        <div className="pt-2 border-t border-slate-700/70">
          {!confirmResetOpen ? (
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={() => setConfirmResetOpen(true)}
              className="game-btn-3d w-full py-2.5 rounded-xl bg-slate-900 hover:bg-rose-950/50 border border-slate-700 text-xs font-extrabold text-slate-300 hover:text-rose-300 transition-colors flex items-center justify-center gap-1.5"
            >
              <RotateCcw className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-rose-400" />
              <span>Reset Study Progress</span>
            </button>
          ) : (
            <div className="p-3 rounded-xl bg-rose-950/40 border border-rose-500/40 space-y-2">
              <p className="text-xs font-bold text-rose-200 flex items-center gap-1.5">
                <Info className="w-4 h-4 text-rose-400 shrink-0" />
                <span>
                  Reset XP, streak, mastery levels, and node completions back to 0? (Your imported courses and custom decks will be kept intact.)
                </span>
              </p>
              <div className="flex gap-2">
                <button
                  type="button"
                  onClick={() => setConfirmResetOpen(false)}
                  className="flex-1 py-2 rounded-lg bg-slate-800 text-xs font-bold text-slate-300"
                >
                  Cancel
                </button>
                <button
                  type="button"
                  onClick={onConfirmReset}
                  className="flex-1 py-2 rounded-lg bg-rose-600 text-xs font-extrabold text-white"
                >
                  Confirm Reset
                </button>
              </div>
            </div>
          )}
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

// ============================================================================
// MAIN ROOT APPLICATION COMPONENT (LOCALSTORAGE PERSISTENCE + NAVIGATION + MODALS)
// ============================================================================
const STORAGE_KEY_PROGRESS = 'macalaoshi_mandarin_progress_v5';
const STORAGE_KEY_COURSES = 'macalaoshi_mandarin_courses_v4';
const STORAGE_KEY_DECKS = 'macalaoshi_mandarin_decks_v4';

const INITIAL_PROGRESS_STATE = {
  xp: 0,
  playerLevel: 1,
  streak: 0,
  dailyXp: 0,
  dailyXpEarned: 0,
  lastStudyDate: null,
  dailyXpDate: null,
  dailyGoalXp: 30,
  dailyQuests: {
    date: null,
    completedIds: [],
    claimedIds: []
  },
  wordNotes: {},
  unlockedStage: 1,
  completedStages: [],
  completedStrokeRules: [],
  lessonProgress: {},
  mastery: {
    nihao: 0,
    你好: 0,
    xiexie: 0,
    谢谢: 0,
    zaijian: 0,
    再见: 0
  },
  skillTelemetry: {},
  preferredSpeed: 1.0,
  isSlowMode: false,
  soundMuted: false,
  stats: {
    writingStagesCompleted: 0,
    perfectBossWins: 0,
    highestCombo: 0
  }
};

export default function App() {
  const [activeTab, setActiveTab] = useState('map'); // 'map' | 'practice' | 'library' | 'profile'

  // Persistent Progress State
  const [progress, setProgress] = useState(() => {
    try {
      const saved = localStorage.getItem(STORAGE_KEY_PROGRESS);
      if (saved) {
        const parsed = JSON.parse(saved);
        const slow = Boolean(parsed.isSlowMode || parsed.preferredSpeed === SLOW_SPEECH_RATE);
        globalIsSlowMode = slow;
        soundFX.setMuted(Boolean(parsed.soundMuted));
        return {
          ...INITIAL_PROGRESS_STATE,
          ...parsed,
          isSlowMode: slow,
          preferredSpeed: slow ? SLOW_SPEECH_RATE : NORMAL_SPEECH_RATE,
          dailyGoalXp: Number(parsed.dailyGoalXp) > 0 ? Number(parsed.dailyGoalXp) : 30,
          wordNotes: { ...(parsed.wordNotes || {}) },
          mastery: { ...INITIAL_PROGRESS_STATE.mastery, ...(parsed.mastery || {}) },
          skillTelemetry: { ...(parsed.skillTelemetry || {}) },
          stats: { ...INITIAL_PROGRESS_STATE.stats, ...(parsed.stats || {}) }
        };
      }
    } catch (e) {
      // Ignore storage errors
    }
    return INITIAL_PROGRESS_STATE;
  });

  // Courses State
  const [courses, setCourses] = useState(() => {
    try {
      const saved = localStorage.getItem(STORAGE_KEY_COURSES);
      if (saved) {
        const parsed = JSON.parse(saved);
        if (Array.isArray(parsed) && parsed.length > 0) return parsed;
      }
    } catch (e) {
      // Ignore
    }
    return DEFAULT_COURSES;
  });

  // Custom Decks State
  const [customDecks, setCustomDecks] = useState(() => {
    try {
      const saved = localStorage.getItem(STORAGE_KEY_DECKS);
      if (saved) {
        const parsed = JSON.parse(saved);
        if (Array.isArray(parsed) && parsed.length > 0) return parsed;
      }
    } catch (e) {
      // Ignore
    }
    return DEFAULT_CUSTOM_DECKS;
  });

  const [activeCourseId, setActiveCourseId] = useState(DEFAULT_COURSES[0].id);
  const [activeLessonId, setActiveLessonId] = useState(DEFAULT_COURSES[0].lessons[0].id);
  const [patternPreviewOverride, setPatternPreviewOverride] = useState(null);

  // Active Study / Game Modal State
  const [activeModal, setActiveModal] = useState(null);
  // { type: 'flashcards' | 'writing' | 'audio_speech' | 'boss' | 'mixed_session' | 'blitz_match' | 'tone_sandhi_drill' | 'hsk_mock_exam', stageId?, words?, focusMode?, sessionConfig?, isPractice?, questModeKey? }

  const [speakingState, setSpeakingState] = useState({
    active: false,
    text: '',
    rate: NORMAL_SPEECH_RATE
  });
  const [toastBanner, setToastBanner] = useState(null);
  const [confirmResetOpen, setConfirmResetOpen] = useState(false);

  // Sync progress, courses, and decks to localStorage
  useEffect(() => {
    try {
      localStorage.setItem(STORAGE_KEY_PROGRESS, JSON.stringify(progress));
    } catch (e) {
      // Ignore
    }
  }, [progress]);

  useEffect(() => {
    try {
      localStorage.setItem(STORAGE_KEY_COURSES, JSON.stringify(courses));
    } catch (e) {
      // Ignore
    }
  }, [courses]);

  useEffect(() => {
    try {
      localStorage.setItem(STORAGE_KEY_DECKS, JSON.stringify(customDecks));
    } catch (e) {
      // Ignore
    }
  }, [customDecks]);

  const triggerToast = useCallback((title, subtitle = '') => {
    setToastBanner({ id: Date.now(), title, subtitle });
    setTimeout(() => {
      setToastBanner((prev) => (prev && Date.now() - prev.id >= 2900 ? null : prev));
    }, 3100);
  }, []);

  // Centralized Mandarin Speech wrapper
  const speakMandarin = useCallback((text, overrideRate = undefined) => {
    speakMandarinGlobal(text, overrideRate, {
      onStart: (cleanText, resolvedRate) => {
        setSpeakingState({ active: true, text: cleanText, rate: resolvedRate });
      },
      onEnd: () => {
        setSpeakingState({ active: false, text: '', rate: NORMAL_SPEECH_RATE });
      },
      onError: () => {
        setSpeakingState({ active: false, text: '', rate: NORMAL_SPEECH_RATE });
      }
    });
  }, []);

  // Update slow mode preference in state + module ref + localStorage and play immediate confirmation
  const handleSetSlowMode = useCallback(
    (nextSlowBoolean, sampleText = '你好') => {
      const isSlow = Boolean(nextSlowBoolean);
      globalIsSlowMode = isSlow;
      const numericRate = isSlow ? SLOW_SPEECH_RATE : NORMAL_SPEECH_RATE;

      setProgress((prev) => ({
        ...prev,
        isSlowMode: isSlow,
        preferredSpeed: numericRate
      }));

      if (sampleText) {
        speakMandarin(sampleText, numericRate);
      }
    },
    [speakMandarin]
  );

  const handleToggleSpeed = useCallback(
    (sampleText = '你好') => {
      const nextSlow = !globalIsSlowMode;
      handleSetSlowMode(nextSlow, sampleText);
      triggerToast(
        nextSlow ? '0.5x Slow Audio Enabled' : '1.0x Normal Audio Enabled',
        nextSlow
          ? 'Playing Mandarin speech at 0.5x slow speed'
          : 'Playing Mandarin speech at 1.0x normal speed'
      );
    },
    [handleSetSlowMode, triggerToast]
  );

  const handleToggleMuteSfx = () => {
    setProgress((prev) => {
      const nextMuted = !prev.soundMuted;
      soundFX.setMuted(nextMuted);
      if (!nextMuted) soundFX.playPop();
      return { ...prev, soundMuted: nextMuted };
    });
  };

  // Resolve Active Course, Active Lesson, and Active Pattern Blueprint
  const activeCourse = useMemo(() => {
    return courses.find((c) => c.id === activeCourseId) || courses[0] || DEFAULT_COURSES[0];
  }, [courses, activeCourseId]);

  const activeLessonIndex = useMemo(() => {
    const idx = (activeCourse.lessons || []).findIndex((l) => l.id === activeLessonId);
    return idx >= 0 ? idx : 0;
  }, [activeCourse, activeLessonId]);

  const activeLesson = useMemo(() => {
    return (
      activeCourse.lessons?.[activeLessonIndex] ||
      activeCourse.lessons?.[0] ||
      DEFAULT_COURSES[0].lessons[0]
    );
  }, [activeCourse, activeLessonIndex]);

  const activeWords = useMemo(() => {
    const raw = activeLesson?.words || UNIT_1_WORDS;
    return raw.map((w, idx) => normalizeWordEntry(w, idx));
  }, [activeLesson]);

  const activePattern = useMemo(() => {
    if (patternPreviewOverride) {
      const found = HETEROGENEOUS_PATTERNS.find((p) => p.id === patternPreviewOverride);
      if (found) return found;
    }
    return HETEROGENEOUS_PATTERNS[activeLessonIndex % HETEROGENEOUS_PATTERNS.length];
  }, [activeLessonIndex, patternPreviewOverride]);

  // Progressive Mode Unlocking: Calculate unlockedWordsCount from all lessons unlocked/started
  const { unlockedWords, unlockedWordsCount } = useMemo(
    () => computeUnlockedWordsPool(courses, activeCourse, activeLesson, progress),
    [courses, activeCourse, activeLesson, progress]
  );

  // Deterministic Daily Quests for today's YYYY-MM-DD (only from currently unlocked modes)
  const todayCalendarDate = new Date().toISOString().slice(0, 10);
  const dailyQuests = useMemo(
    () => generateDeterministicDailyQuests(todayCalendarDate, unlockedWordsCount),
    [todayCalendarDate, unlockedWordsCount]
  );

  const dailyQuestState = useMemo(() => {
    const saved = progress.dailyQuests || {};
    if (saved.date === todayCalendarDate) {
      return {
        date: todayCalendarDate,
        completedIds: Array.isArray(saved.completedIds) ? saved.completedIds : [],
        claimedIds: Array.isArray(saved.claimedIds) ? saved.claimedIds : []
      };
    }
    return {
      date: todayCalendarDate,
      completedIds: [],
      claimedIds: []
    };
  }, [progress.dailyQuests, todayCalendarDate]);

  // Ensure today's dailyQuests date is persisted in localStorage
  useEffect(() => {
    if (progress.dailyQuests?.date !== todayCalendarDate) {
      setProgress((prev) => ({
        ...prev,
        dailyQuests: {
          date: todayCalendarDate,
          completedIds: [],
          claimedIds: []
        }
      }));
    }
  }, [progress.dailyQuests?.date, todayCalendarDate]);

  // Claim XP reward for a completed Daily Quest
  const handleClaimQuestReward = useCallback(
    (quest) => {
      if (!quest) return;
      soundFX.playVictory();
      const today = new Date().toISOString().slice(0, 10);

      setProgress((prev) => {
        const dq =
          prev.dailyQuests?.date === today
            ? prev.dailyQuests
            : { date: today, completedIds: [], claimedIds: [] };

        if ((dq.claimedIds || []).includes(quest.id)) return prev;

        const nextClaimed = [...(dq.claimedIds || []), quest.id];
        const xpGain = Number(quest.xpReward) || 30;
        const nextTotalXp = (prev.xp || 0) + xpGain;
        const prevTodayXp =
          prev.dailyXpDate === today
            ? Number(prev.dailyXpEarned ?? prev.dailyXp ?? 0)
            : 0;

        return {
          ...prev,
          xp: nextTotalXp,
          playerLevel: Math.floor(nextTotalXp / 100) + 1,
          dailyXpDate: today,
          dailyXp: prevTodayXp + xpGain,
          dailyXpEarned: prevTodayXp + xpGain,
          dailyQuests: {
            date: today,
            completedIds: dq.completedIds || [],
            claimedIds: nextClaimed
          }
        };
      });

      triggerToast(
        `Quest Reward Claimed! +${quest.xpReward} XP`,
        `Completed: "${quest.title}"`
      );
    },
    [triggerToast]
  );

  // Jump directly to a quest's mode when tapped from the Daily Quests widget
  const handleJumpToQuest = useCallback(
    (quest) => {
      if (!quest) return;
      soundFX.playPop();
      const pool = unlockedWords.length > 0 ? unlockedWords : activeWords;

      switch (quest.modeKey) {
        case 'smart_review': {
          const queue = buildDynamicModalityQueue(
            pool,
            ['recognition', 'writing', 'listening', 'speaking'],
            'Daily Quest • Smart Review',
            activeLessonIndex
          );
          setActiveModal({
            type: 'mixed_session',
            stageId: null,
            questModeKey: 'smart_review',
            sessionConfig: {
              title: 'Daily Quest • Smart Review',
              badgeText: 'Daily Quest',
              xpReward: 75,
              queue
            }
          });
          break;
        }
        case 'flashcards':
          setActiveModal({
            type: 'flashcards',
            stageId: null,
            questModeKey: 'flashcards',
            words: activeWords
          });
          break;
        case 'writing':
          setActiveModal({
            type: 'writing',
            stageId: null,
            questModeKey: 'writing',
            words: activeWords
          });
          break;
        case 'dictation':
          setActiveModal({
            type: 'audio_speech',
            stageId: null,
            questModeKey: 'dictation',
            words: activeWords,
            focusMode: 'dictation'
          });
          break;
        case 'sentence_builder': {
          const queue = activeWords.map((w, idx) => ({
            stepId: `quest_sb_${w.id}_${idx}`,
            word: w,
            modality: 'sentence_builder',
            reason: 'Daily Quest • Sentence Builder'
          }));
          setActiveModal({
            type: 'mixed_session',
            stageId: null,
            questModeKey: 'sentence_builder',
            sessionConfig: {
              title: 'Sentence Builder Puzzle',
              badgeText: 'Daily Quest',
              xpReward: 65,
              queue
            }
          });
          break;
        }
        case 'tone_sandhi_drill':
          setActiveModal({
            type: 'tone_sandhi_drill',
            questModeKey: 'tone_sandhi_drill',
            words: pool
          });
          break;
        case 'cloze': {
          const queue = pool.slice(0, 6).map((w, idx) => ({
            stepId: `quest_cloze_${w.id}_${idx}`,
            word: w,
            modality: 'cloze',
            reason: 'Daily Quest • Fill-in-the-Blank'
          }));
          setActiveModal({
            type: 'mixed_session',
            stageId: null,
            questModeKey: 'cloze',
            sessionConfig: {
              title: 'Fill-in-the-Blank (选词填空)',
              badgeText: 'Daily Quest',
              xpReward: 70,
              queue
            }
          });
          break;
        }
        case 'blitz_match':
          setActiveModal({
            type: 'blitz_match',
            questModeKey: 'blitz_match',
            words: pool
          });
          break;
        case 'hsk_mock_exam':
          setActiveModal({
            type: 'hsk_mock_exam',
            questModeKey: 'hsk_mock_exam',
            words: pool
          });
          break;
        default:
          setActiveTab('practice');
      }
    },
    [activeLessonIndex, activeWords, unlockedWords]
  );

  // Record per-word, per-skill mistake / completion telemetry
  const handleRecordSkillEvent = useCallback(
    (word, modalityKey, { mistakesDelta = 0, completionsDelta = 0 } = {}) => {
      if (!word) return;
      const key = word.hanzi || word.id;
      if (!key) return;

      setProgress((prev) => {
        const prevTelemetry = prev.skillTelemetry || {};
        const prevWordEntry = prevTelemetry[key] || {};
        const prevModality = prevWordEntry[modalityKey] || {
          mistakes: 0,
          completions: 0
        };

        return {
          ...prev,
          skillTelemetry: {
            ...prevTelemetry,
            [key]: {
              ...prevWordEntry,
              [modalityKey]: {
                mistakes: Math.max(0, (prevModality.mistakes || 0) + mistakesDelta),
                completions: Math.max(
                  0,
                  (prevModality.completions || 0) + completionsDelta
                )
              }
            }
          }
        };
      });
    },
    []
  );

  // Complete a Stage or Practice Session and update XP, Streak, Stage Unlock, Mastery (0-5) & Daily Quests
  const handleCompleteStageSession = useCallback(
    ({
      stageId = null,
      wordsPracticed = activeWords,
      xpEarned = 50,
      maxCombo = 1,
      isBossWin = false,
      heartsRemaining = 3,
      completedModeKeys = []
    }) => {
      soundFX.playVictory();
      const today = new Date().toISOString().slice(0, 10);
      const lessonKey = `${activeCourse.id}__${activeLesson.id}`;
      const isDefaultUnit1 =
        activeCourse.id === 'course_default_unit1' && activeLesson.id === 'lesson_unit_1';

      setProgress((prev) => {
        const nextMastery = { ...(prev.mastery || {}) };
        wordsPracticed.forEach((w) => {
          const curLv = getWordMasteryLevel(nextMastery, w);
          const nextLv = Math.min(5, curLv + 1);
          if (w.id) nextMastery[w.id] = nextLv;
          if (w.hanzi) nextMastery[w.hanzi] = nextLv;
        });

        const prevLessonProg = prev.lessonProgress?.[lessonKey] || {
          unlockedStage: isDefaultUnit1 ? prev.unlockedStage : 1,
          completedStages: isDefaultUnit1 ? prev.completedStages : []
        };

        let nextUnlocked = prevLessonProg.unlockedStage;
        let nextCompleted = [...(prevLessonProg.completedStages || [])];

        if (stageId) {
          if (!nextCompleted.includes(stageId)) {
            nextCompleted.push(stageId);
          }
          if (stageId >= nextUnlocked && stageId < 4) {
            nextUnlocked = stageId + 1;
          }
        }

        const isNewDay = prev.lastStudyDate !== today;
        const nextStreak = isNewDay ? (prev.streak || 0) + 1 : Math.max(1, prev.streak || 0);
        const prevTodayXp =
          prev.dailyXpDate === today
            ? Number(prev.dailyXpEarned ?? prev.dailyXp ?? 0)
            : 0;
        const nextTotalXp = (prev.xp || 0) + xpEarned;

        // Check if any of today's Daily Quests match the completed session mode(s)
        const prevDq =
          prev.dailyQuests?.date === today
            ? prev.dailyQuests
            : { date: today, completedIds: [], claimedIds: [] };
        const nextCompletedQuestIds = [...(prevDq.completedIds || [])];

        dailyQuests.forEach((q) => {
          if (
            completedModeKeys.includes(q.modeKey) &&
            !nextCompletedQuestIds.includes(q.id)
          ) {
            nextCompletedQuestIds.push(q.id);
          }
        });

        return {
          ...prev,
          xp: nextTotalXp,
          playerLevel: Math.floor(nextTotalXp / 100) + 1,
          dailyXpDate: today,
          dailyXp: prevTodayXp + xpEarned,
          dailyXpEarned: prevTodayXp + xpEarned,
          streak: nextStreak,
          lastStudyDate: today,
          dailyQuests: {
            date: today,
            completedIds: nextCompletedQuestIds,
            claimedIds: prevDq.claimedIds || []
          },
          unlockedStage: isDefaultUnit1 ? nextUnlocked : prev.unlockedStage,
          completedStages: isDefaultUnit1 ? nextCompleted : prev.completedStages,
          lessonProgress: {
            ...(prev.lessonProgress || {}),
            [lessonKey]: {
              unlockedStage: nextUnlocked,
              completedStages: nextCompleted
            }
          },
          mastery: nextMastery,
          stats: {
            ...(prev.stats || {}),
            writingStagesCompleted:
              (prev.stats?.writingStagesCompleted || 0) + (stageId === 2 ? 1 : 0),
            perfectBossWins:
              (prev.stats?.perfectBossWins || 0) + (isBossWin && heartsRemaining === 3 ? 1 : 0),
            highestCombo: Math.max(prev.stats?.highestCombo || 0, maxCombo)
          }
        };
      });

      setActiveModal(null);
      triggerToast(`Stage Complete! +${xpEarned} XP`, 'Character mastery levels updated!');
    },
    [activeCourse.id, activeLesson.id, activeWords, dailyQuests, triggerToast]
  );

  // Save a Personal Note / Mnemonic per word in localStorage
  const handleSaveWordNote = useCallback((word, noteText) => {
    if (!word) return;
    setProgress((prev) => {
      const nextNotes = { ...(prev.wordNotes || {}) };
      if (word.hanzi) nextNotes[word.hanzi] = noteText;
      if (word.id) nextNotes[word.id] = noteText;
      return {
        ...prev,
        wordNotes: nextNotes
      };
    });
  }, []);

  // Configure Daily XP Goal target
  const handleSetDailyGoalXp = useCallback(
    (newTarget) => {
      const cleanTarget = Math.max(10, Number(newTarget) || 30);
      setProgress((prev) => ({
        ...prev,
        dailyGoalXp: cleanTarget
      }));
      triggerToast(`Daily XP Goal: ${cleanTarget} XP`, 'Saved your new daily study target.');
    },
    [triggerToast]
  );

  // Launch a Map Stage Node based on its heterogeneous configuration
  const handleSelectStageNode = (stage) => {
    soundFX.playPop();
    if (stage.launchType === 'flashcards') {
      setActiveModal({
        type: 'flashcards',
        stageId: stage.id,
        words: activeWords
      });
    } else if (stage.launchType === 'writing') {
      setActiveModal({
        type: 'writing',
        stageId: stage.id,
        words: activeWords
      });
    } else if (stage.launchType === 'dictation_only') {
      setActiveModal({
        type: 'audio_speech',
        stageId: stage.id,
        words: activeWords,
        focusMode: 'dictation'
      });
    } else if (stage.launchType === 'speaking_only') {
      setActiveModal({
        type: 'audio_speech',
        stageId: stage.id,
        words: activeWords,
        focusMode: 'speaking'
      });
    } else if (stage.launchType === 'boss') {
      setActiveModal({
        type: 'boss',
        stageId: stage.id,
        words: activeWords,
        isPractice: false
      });
    } else {
      // Dynamic Multi-Skill Mix or Full Gauntlet Node
      // Automatically includes 'cloze' starting from Lesson 3 onward (activeLessonIndex >= 2)
      const queue = buildDynamicModalityQueue(
        activeWords,
        stage.allowedModalities,
        stage.badgeText,
        activeLessonIndex
      );
      setActiveModal({
        type: 'mixed_session',
        stageId: stage.id,
        sessionConfig: {
          title: stage.title,
          badgeText: stage.badgeText,
          xpReward: stage.xpReward || 70,
          queue
        }
      });
    }
  };

  // Global Tracked Words & Mastery Statistics
  const allUniqueWordsMap = useMemo(() => {
    const map = new window.Map();
    courses.forEach((c) => {
      (c.lessons || []).forEach((l) => {
        (l.words || []).forEach((w, idx) => {
          const norm = normalizeWordEntry(w, idx);
          if (norm.hanzi && !map.has(norm.hanzi)) {
            map.set(norm.hanzi, norm);
          }
        });
      });
    });
    customDecks.forEach((d) => {
      (d.words || []).forEach((w, idx) => {
        const norm = normalizeWordEntry(w, idx);
        if (norm.hanzi && !map.has(norm.hanzi)) {
          map.set(norm.hanzi, norm);
        }
      });
    });
    return map;
  }, [courses, customDecks]);

  const globalStats = useMemo(() => {
    let masteredWordsCount = 0;
    let learningWordsCount = 0;
    let totalStarsEarned = 0;
    const totalTrackedWords = allUniqueWordsMap.size;

    allUniqueWordsMap.forEach((word) => {
      const lv = getWordMasteryLevel(progress.mastery, word);
      totalStarsEarned += lv;
      if (lv >= 4) masteredWordsCount += 1;
      else if (lv >= 1) learningWordsCount += 1;
    });

    return {
      totalTrackedWords,
      masteredWordsCount,
      learningWordsCount,
      totalStarsEarned,
      maxStarsPossible: totalTrackedWords * 5
    };
  }, [allUniqueWordsMap, progress.mastery]);

  const currentLevel = Math.floor(progress.xp / 100) + 1;
  const xpIntoLevel = progress.xp % 100;
  const todayIso = new Date().toISOString().slice(0, 10);
  const dailyGoalXp = Number(progress.dailyGoalXp) > 0 ? Number(progress.dailyGoalXp) : 30;
  const todayXpEarned = progress.dailyXpDate === todayIso ? Number(progress.dailyXpEarned || 0) : 0;
  const dailyGoalPercent = Math.min(100, Math.round((todayXpEarned / dailyGoalXp) * 100));
  const isDailyGoalMet = todayXpEarned >= dailyGoalXp;

  return (
    <div className="min-h-screen bg-slate-950 text-slate-100 flex justify-center font-sans select-none">
      <style>{`
        @keyframes gameShake {
          0%, 100% { transform: translateX(0); }
          20% { transform: translateX(-7px); }
          40% { transform: translateX(7px); }
          60% { transform: translateX(-5px); }
          80% { transform: translateX(5px); }
        }
        @keyframes gameGreenPulse {
          0% { transform: scale(1); }
          50% { transform: scale(1.02); box-shadow: 0 0 25px rgba(16, 185, 129, 0.35); }
          100% { transform: scale(1); }
        }
        @keyframes xpFloatUp {
          0% { opacity: 0; transform: translateY(8px) scale(0.9); }
          25% { opacity: 1; transform: translateY(0px) scale(1.05); }
          80% { opacity: 1; transform: translateY(-14px) scale(1); }
          100% { opacity: 0; transform: translateY(-22px) scale(0.95); }
        }
        @keyframes floatBob {
          0%, 100% { transform: translateY(0px); }
          50% { transform: translateY(-4px); }
        }
        @keyframes nodeRingPulse {
          0%, 100% { transform: scale(1); opacity: 0.75; }
          50% { transform: scale(1.08); opacity: 0.25; }
        }
        .animate-game-shake { animation: gameShake 0.42s ease-in-out; }
        .animate-game-green-pulse { animation: gameGreenPulse 0.48s ease-out; }
        .animate-xp-float { animation: xpFloatUp 1.15s ease-out forwards; }
        .animate-float-bob { animation: floatBob 2s ease-in-out infinite; }
        .animate-node-ring { animation: nodeRingPulse 2.2s ease-in-out infinite; }
        .game-btn-3d:active:not(:disabled) { transform: translateY(2px); }
      `}</style>

      <div className="w-full max-w-md min-h-screen bg-slate-900 border-x border-slate-800/80 flex flex-col justify-between pb-20 relative">
        {/* STICKY GAMIFIED TOP BAR: PLAYER LEVEL & TITLE, XP BAR, DAILY GOAL, STREAK, MASTERED & AUDIO CONTROLS */}
        <header className="sticky top-0 z-30 bg-slate-900/95 backdrop-blur-md border-b border-slate-800 px-4 py-2.5 space-y-2">
          <div className="flex items-center justify-between gap-2">
            <div className="flex items-center gap-2 min-w-0">
              <div className="w-8 h-8 rounded-xl bg-gradient-to-br from-rose-500 to-amber-500 flex items-center justify-center font-black text-sm text-slate-950 shadow shrink-0">
                {currentLevel}
              </div>
              <div className="min-w-0">
                <div className="text-xs font-black text-white truncate">
                  Lv.{currentLevel} {getPlayerTitle(currentLevel)}
                </div>
                <div className="w-24 h-1.5 bg-slate-800 rounded-full overflow-hidden mt-0.5">
                  <div
                    className="h-full bg-gradient-to-r from-rose-500 to-amber-400 transition-all duration-500"
                    style={{ width: `${xpIntoLevel}%` }}
                  />
                </div>
              </div>
            </div>

            <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5 shrink-0">
              {/* Speed Toggle Pill (1.0x vs 0.5x) */}
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={() => handleToggleSpeed(activeWords[0]?.hanzi || '你好')}
                title="Toggle Mandarin Speech Speed (1.0x Normal / 0.5x Slow)"
                className={`px-2 py-1 rounded-xl border text-[10px] font-black flex items-center gap-1 transition-all ${
                  progress.isSlowMode
                    ? 'bg-sky-500/20 border-sky-400 text-sky-300'
                    : 'bg-slate-800 border-slate-700 text-slate-300'
                }`}
              >
                <Turtle className="w-3 h-3" />
                <span>{progress.isSlowMode ? '0.5x' : '1.0x'}</span>
              </button>

              {/* Daily Streak Counter */}
              <div className="px-2 py-1 rounded-xl bg-amber-500/15 border border-amber-500/30 text-amber-300 text-xs font-black flex items-center gap-1">
                <Flame className="w-3.5 h-3.5 fill-amber-400 text-amber-400" />
                <span>{progress.streak}</span>
              </div>

              {/* Mastered Characters Counter */}
              <div className="px-2 py-1 rounded-xl bg-emerald-500/15 border border-emerald-500/30 text-emerald-300 text-xs font-black flex items-center gap-1">
                <Crown className="w-3.5 h-3.5 fill-emerald-400 text-emerald-400" />
                <span>{globalStats.masteredWordsCount}</span>
              </div>

              {/* Sound FX Mute / Unmute Toggle */}
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={handleToggleMuteSfx}
                title={progress.soundMuted ? 'Unmute UI Sound FX' : 'Mute UI Sound FX'}
                className="p-1.5 rounded-xl bg-slate-800 hover:bg-slate-700 border border-slate-700 text-slate-300"
              >
                {progress.soundMuted ? (
                  <VolumeX className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-rose-400" />
                ) : (
                  <Volume2 className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-emerald-400" />
                )}
              </button>
            </div>
          </div>

          {/* Daily XP Goal Bar (lights up when completed for the current date) */}
          <div
            onClick={() => setActiveTab('profile')}
            className={`cursor-pointer rounded-xl px-2.5 py-1.5 border flex items-center justify-between gap-2.5 transition-all ${
              isDailyGoalMet
                ? 'bg-emerald-500/15 border-emerald-400/60 shadow-sm shadow-emerald-500/20'
                : 'bg-slate-950/80 border-slate-800/90'
            }`}
          >
            <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5 shrink-0">
              <Zap
                className={`w-3.5 h-3.5 ${
                  isDailyGoalMet
                    ? 'text-emerald-400 fill-emerald-400 animate-bounce'
                    : 'text-amber-400'
                }`}
              />
              <span
                className={`text-[10px] font-black uppercase tracking-wider ${
                  isDailyGoalMet ? 'text-emerald-300' : 'text-slate-300'
                }`}
              >
                {isDailyGoalMet ? 'Daily Goal Complete! ★' : 'Daily XP Goal'}
              </span>
            </div>

            <div className="flex-1 h-2 bg-slate-800 rounded-full overflow-hidden">
              <div
                className={`h-full transition-all duration-500 ${
                  isDailyGoalMet
                    ? 'bg-gradient-to-r from-emerald-400 via-teal-300 to-amber-300'
                    : 'bg-gradient-to-r from-amber-500 to-rose-500'
                }`}
                style={{ width: `${dailyGoalPercent}%` }}
              />
            </div>

            <span
              className={`text-[10px] font-black shrink-0 ${
                isDailyGoalMet ? 'text-emerald-300' : 'text-amber-300'
              }`}
            >
              {todayXpEarned}/{dailyGoalXp} XP
            </span>
          </div>
        </header>

        {/* TOAST NOTIFICATION BANNER */}
        {toastBanner && (
          <div className="fixed top-14 left-1/2 -translate-x-1/2 z-50 w-[90%] max-w-sm rounded-2xl bg-slate-950/95 border border-amber-400/50 px-4 py-2.5 shadow-2xl flex items-center gap-2.5">
            <Sparkles className="w-4 h-4 text-amber-400 shrink-0" />
            <div className="min-w-0">
              <p className="text-xs font-black text-white truncate">{toastBanner.title}</p>
              {toastBanner.subtitle && (
                <p className="text-[11px] text-slate-300 truncate">{toastBanner.subtitle}</p>
              )}
            </div>
          </div>
        )}

        {/* MAIN TAB CONTENT */}
        <main className="flex-1 overflow-y-auto">
          {activeTab === 'map' && (
            <ProgressionMapView
              progress={progress}
              courses={courses}
              activeCourse={activeCourse}
              activeLesson={activeLesson}
              activeLessonIndex={activeLessonIndex}
              activePattern={activePattern}
              patternPreviewOverride={patternPreviewOverride}
              onChangePatternOverride={setPatternPreviewOverride}
              activeWords={activeWords}
              dailyQuests={dailyQuests}
              dailyQuestState={dailyQuestState}
              onJumpToQuest={handleJumpToQuest}
              onClaimQuestReward={handleClaimQuestReward}
              onSelectCourseAndLesson={(courseId, lessonId) => {
                setActiveCourseId(courseId);
                const foundCourse = courses.find((c) => c.id === courseId);
                const targetLessonId =
                  lessonId || foundCourse?.lessons?.[0]?.id || activeLessonId;
                setActiveLessonId(targetLessonId);
              }}
              speakMandarin={speakMandarin}
              onSelectStageNode={handleSelectStageNode}
              onOpenLibraryImporter={() => setActiveTab('library')}
            />
          )}

          {activeTab === 'practice' && (
            <SmartReviewArenaView
              progress={progress}
              courses={courses}
              activeCourse={activeCourse}
              activeLesson={activeLesson}
              activeWords={activeWords}
              customDecks={customDecks}
              unlockedWordsCount={unlockedWordsCount}
              speakMandarin={speakMandarin}
              speakingState={speakingState}
              onLaunchSmartReviewSession={(queue, filterLabel) => {
                soundFX.playPop();
                setActiveModal({
                  type: 'mixed_session',
                  stageId: null,
                  questModeKey: 'smart_review',
                  sessionConfig: {
                    title: 'Smart Review Arena',
                    badgeText: filterLabel,
                    xpReward: 75,
                    queue
                  }
                });
              }}
              onLaunchSentenceBuilderDrill={() => {
                soundFX.playPop();
                const queue = activeWords.map((w, idx) => ({
                  stepId: `sb_${w.id}_${idx}`,
                  word: w,
                  modality: 'sentence_builder',
                  reason: 'Sentence Builder • Word Order Puzzle'
                }));
                setActiveModal({
                  type: 'mixed_session',
                  stageId: null,
                  questModeKey: 'sentence_builder',
                  sessionConfig: {
                    title: 'Sentence Builder Puzzle',
                    badgeText: 'Word Order',
                    xpReward: 65,
                    queue
                  }
                });
              }}
              onLaunchClozeDrill={(pool) => {
                soundFX.playPop();
                const sourceWords =
                  Array.isArray(pool) && pool.length > 0 ? pool : activeWords;
                const queue = sourceWords.slice(0, 8).map((w, idx) => ({
                  stepId: `cloze_${w.id}_${idx}`,
                  word: w,
                  modality: 'cloze',
                  reason: 'Fill-in-the-Blank • 选词填空'
                }));
                setActiveModal({
                  type: 'mixed_session',
                  stageId: null,
                  questModeKey: 'cloze',
                  sessionConfig: {
                    title: 'Fill-in-the-Blank (选词填空)',
                    badgeText: 'Cloze Context Drill',
                    xpReward: 70,
                    queue
                  }
                });
              }}
              onLaunchToneSandhiDrill={(pool) => {
                soundFX.playPop();
                setActiveModal({
                  type: 'tone_sandhi_drill',
                  questModeKey: 'tone_sandhi_drill',
                  words: Array.isArray(pool) && pool.length > 0 ? pool : activeWords
                });
              }}
              onLaunchHskMockExam={(pool) => {
                soundFX.playPop();
                setActiveModal({
                  type: 'hsk_mock_exam',
                  questModeKey: 'hsk_mock_exam',
                  words: Array.isArray(pool) && pool.length > 0 ? pool : activeWords
                });
              }}
              onLaunchBlitzMatch={(pool) => {
                soundFX.playPop();
                setActiveModal({
                  type: 'blitz_match',
                  questModeKey: 'blitz_match',
                  words: pool && pool.length > 0 ? pool : activeWords
                });
              }}
              onLaunchActivity={(activityType) => {
                soundFX.playPop();
                if (activityType === 'writing') {
                  setActiveModal({
                    type: 'writing',
                    stageId: null,
                    questModeKey: 'writing',
                    words: activeWords
                  });
                } else if (activityType === 'dictation_only') {
                  setActiveModal({
                    type: 'audio_speech',
                    stageId: null,
                    questModeKey: 'dictation',
                    words: activeWords,
                    focusMode: 'dictation'
                  });
                } else if (activityType === 'speaking_only') {
                  setActiveModal({
                    type: 'audio_speech',
                    stageId: null,
                    questModeKey: 'speaking',
                    words: activeWords,
                    focusMode: 'speaking'
                  });
                } else {
                  setActiveModal({
                    type: 'flashcards',
                    stageId: null,
                    questModeKey: 'flashcards',
                    words: activeWords
                  });
                }
              }}
              triggerToast={triggerToast}
            />
          )}

          {activeTab === 'library' && (
            <LibraryView
              progress={progress}
              courses={courses}
              activeCourse={activeCourse}
              customDecks={customDecks}
              isSlowMode={progress.isSlowMode}
              onSetSlowMode={handleSetSlowMode}
              speakMandarin={speakMandarin}
              speakingState={speakingState}
              onAddWordsToDeck={(deckName, newRawWords) => {
                setCustomDecks((prev) => {
                  const normalizedNew = newRawWords.map((w, idx) =>
                    normalizeWordEntry(w, Date.now() + idx)
                  );
                  const existingIdx = prev.findIndex(
                    (d) => d.name.toLowerCase() === deckName.toLowerCase()
                  );
                  if (existingIdx >= 0) {
                    const updated = [...prev];
                    updated[existingIdx] = {
                      ...updated[existingIdx],
                      words: [...(updated[existingIdx].words || []), ...normalizedNew]
                    };
                    return updated;
                  }
                  return [
                    ...prev,
                    {
                      id: `deck_${Date.now()}`,
                      name: deckName,
                      description: 'Custom user vocabulary deck',
                      words: normalizedNew
                    }
                  ];
                });
              }}
              onAddImportedCourse={(newCourse, switchToMap = false) => {
                setCourses((prev) => [...prev, newCourse]);
                setActiveCourseId(newCourse.id);
                if (newCourse.lessons?.[0]?.id) {
                  setActiveLessonId(newCourse.lessons[0].id);
                }
                if (switchToMap) {
                  setActiveTab('map');
                }
              }}
              onSelectCourseOnMap={(courseId, lessonId) => {
                setActiveCourseId(courseId);
                if (lessonId) setActiveLessonId(lessonId);
                setActiveTab('map');
              }}
              onPracticeDeck={(deckWords, mode) => {
                const targetPool =
                  Array.isArray(deckWords) && deckWords.length > 0
                    ? deckWords.map((w, i) => normalizeWordEntry(w, i))
                    : activeWords;
                setActiveModal({
                  type: mode === 'writing' ? 'writing' : 'flashcards',
                  stageId: null,
                  words: targetPool
                });
              }}
              onExportAllData={() => {
                const payload = {
                  exportedAt: new Date().toISOString(),
                  progress,
                  courses,
                  customDecks
                };
                const blob = new Blob([JSON.stringify(payload, null, 2)], {
                  type: 'application/json'
                });
                const url = URL.createObjectURL(blob);
                const a = document.createElement('a');
                a.href = url;
                a.download = `macalaoshi-backup-${new Date().toISOString().slice(0, 10)}.json`;
                a.click();
                URL.revokeObjectURL(url);
                triggerToast('Backup Exported!', 'Saved JSON snapshot of courses, decks & mastery.');
              }}
              onImportBackupData={(parsedBackup) => {
                if (!parsedBackup || typeof parsedBackup !== 'object') {
                  triggerToast('Invalid Backup', 'Backup file structure was not recognized.');
                  return;
                }
                if (parsedBackup.progress) {
                  setProgress((prev) => ({ ...prev, ...parsedBackup.progress }));
                }
                if (Array.isArray(parsedBackup.courses) && parsedBackup.courses.length > 0) {
                  setCourses(parsedBackup.courses);
                }
                if (Array.isArray(parsedBackup.customDecks)) {
                  setCustomDecks(parsedBackup.customDecks);
                }
                triggerToast('Backup Restored!', 'Courses, custom decks, and mastery levels loaded.');
              }}
              onCompleteStrokeRuleDojo={(completedRule) => {
                const today = new Date().toISOString().slice(0, 10);
                setProgress((prev) => {
                  const prevRules = Array.isArray(prev.completedStrokeRules)
                    ? prev.completedStrokeRules
                    : [];
                  const nextRules = prevRules.includes(completedRule.id)
                    ? prevRules
                    : [...prevRules, completedRule.id];
                  const nextMastery = { ...(prev.mastery || {}) };
                  (completedRule.unseenChars || []).forEach((u) => {
                    const cur = Number(nextMastery[u.char] || 0);
                    nextMastery[u.char] = Math.min(5, cur + 1);
                  });
                  const prevTodayXp =
                    prev.dailyXpDate === today
                      ? Number(prev.dailyXpEarned ?? prev.dailyXp ?? 0)
                      : 0;
                  const nextXp = (prev.xp || 0) + 25;
                  return {
                    ...prev,
                    xp: nextXp,
                    playerLevel: Math.floor(nextXp / 100) + 1,
                    dailyXpDate: today,
                    dailyXp: prevTodayXp + 25,
                    dailyXpEarned: prevTodayXp + 25,
                    completedStrokeRules: nextRules,
                    mastery: nextMastery
                  };
                });
                triggerToast(
                  `Rule ${completedRule.number} Mastered! +25 XP`,
                  `Completed Unseen Intuition Challenge (${completedRule.unseenChars
                    .map((u) => u.char)
                    .join(', ')})`
                );
              }}
              triggerToast={triggerToast}
            />
          )}

          {activeTab === 'profile' && (
            <ProfileView
              progress={progress}
              globalStats={globalStats}
              allUniqueWordsMap={allUniqueWordsMap}
              courses={courses}
              customDecks={customDecks}
              currentLevel={currentLevel}
              dailyGoalXp={dailyGoalXp}
              todayXpEarned={todayXpEarned}
              onSetDailyGoalXp={handleSetDailyGoalXp}
              onToggleSpeed={handleToggleSpeed}
              onExportAllData={() => {
                const payload = {
                  exportedAt: new Date().toISOString(),
                  progress,
                  courses,
                  customDecks
                };
                const blob = new Blob([JSON.stringify(payload, null, 2)], {
                  type: 'application/json'
                });
                const url = URL.createObjectURL(blob);
                const a = document.createElement('a');
                a.href = url;
                a.download = `macalaoshi-backup-${new Date().toISOString().slice(0, 10)}.json`;
                a.click();
                URL.revokeObjectURL(url);
                triggerToast('Backup Exported!', 'Downloaded full study backup JSON.');
              }}
              onImportBackupData={(parsedBackup) => {
                if (parsedBackup?.progress) {
                  setProgress((prev) => ({ ...prev, ...parsedBackup.progress }));
                }
                if (Array.isArray(parsedBackup?.courses) && parsedBackup.courses.length > 0) {
                  setCourses(parsedBackup.courses);
                }
                if (Array.isArray(parsedBackup?.customDecks)) {
                  setCustomDecks(parsedBackup.customDecks);
                }
                triggerToast('Backup Restored!', 'All progress and courses restored.');
              }}
              confirmResetOpen={confirmResetOpen}
              setConfirmResetOpen={setConfirmResetOpen}
              onConfirmReset={() => {
                const freshZeroState = {
                  ...INITIAL_PROGRESS_STATE,
                  dailyQuests: {
                    date: new Date().toISOString().slice(0, 10),
                    completedIds: [],
                    claimedIds: []
                  },
                  wordNotes: {},
                  completedStrokeRules: [],
                  lessonProgress: {},
                  mastery: {
                    nihao: 0,
                    你好: 0,
                    xiexie: 0,
                    谢谢: 0,
                    zaijian: 0,
                    再见: 0
                  },
                  skillTelemetry: {},
                  stats: {
                    writingStagesCompleted: 0,
                    perfectBossWins: 0,
                    highestCombo: 0
                  }
                };
                try {
                  localStorage.setItem(STORAGE_KEY_PROGRESS, JSON.stringify(freshZeroState));
                } catch (e) {
                  // Ignore storage errors
                }
                setProgress(freshZeroState);
                setConfirmResetOpen(false);
                triggerToast(
                  'Study Progress Reset to 0',
                  'XP, streak, mastery & nodes reset. Courses and custom decks kept intact.'
                );
              }}
              onLaunchWeakWordsReview={(weakPool) => {
                const wordsToUse =
                  Array.isArray(weakPool) && weakPool.length > 0 ? weakPool : activeWords;
                const queue = buildDynamicModalityQueue(
                  wordsToUse,
                  ['recognition', 'writing', 'listening', 'sentence_builder'],
                  'Weakest Words Booster'
                );
                setActiveModal({
                  type: 'mixed_session',
                  stageId: null,
                  sessionConfig: {
                    title: 'Weakest Words Booster',
                    badgeText: 'Mastery Lv 0–2 Review',
                    xpReward: 70,
                    queue
                  }
                });
              }}
            />
          )}
        </main>

        {/* BOTTOM NAVIGATION BAR (4 TABS: MAP, SMART REVIEW, LIBRARY, PROFILE) */}
